新题部分  
1 对结果开心的重要决定  
Describe a time you made an important decision and were happy with  
the result  
You should say:  
- What the decision was  
- Why you made the decision  
- How easy it was for you to make the decision  
And explain why you were happy with the result  
Answer：  
The important decision I want to talk about is that I made up my mind  
to devote six months to preparing for my language test instead of  
rushing to apply for overseas programs. Back then, most of my peers  
were eager to send out their applications as early as possible. However,  
my language scores were far from satisfactory, and I realised submitting  
applications with weak results would largely reduce my chances of  
getting offers. Actually, this was quite a tough call for me. I felt anxious

## PDF第3页

about falling behind my classmates, and I constantly worried whether  
six‑month preparation would end up being a waste of time. I talked  
things over with my parents and senior students, analysed my strengths  
and weaknesses, and finally stuck to this plan. During those six months,  
I kept a strict daily study routine and made steady progress step by  
step. Eventually I achieved my target score. I am genuinely satisfied with  
this decision, because not only did I obtain qualified language results,  
but I also built up greater self‑discipline and confidence, which benefit  
my further studies enormously.  
中文翻译：  
我想聊的这个重要决定，就是我下定决心花六个月专心备考语言考试，而不是  
仓促递交海外项目申请。那时候，我的大多数同龄人都急于尽早提交申请。但  
我的语言成绩远远达不到理想标准，我意识到拿着不理想的分数递交申请，会  
大幅降低拿到录取的概率。实际上做这个决定对我来说相当煎熬。我害怕落后  
于同班同学，也一直担心六个月的备考到头来会不会白白浪费时间。我和父  
母、学长学姐反复沟通，分析自身优缺点，最终坚持了这个计划。在那六个月  
里，我严格执行每日学习计划，一点点稳步提升。最后我考到了目标分数。我  
发自内心对这个决定感到满意，因为我不仅拿到合格的语言成绩，还培养出更  
强的自律与自信，这对我之后的学习大有裨益。

## PDF第4页

Part3  
Q1. Which is better, to make a decision quickly or slowly?  
Answer：  
I believe whether to decide quickly or slowly hinges entirely on how  
significant the matter is. For trivial daily stuff like picking lunch or  
choosing entertainment, quick decisions help us save precious time. But  
for life‑shifting choices related to education, career or finance, people  
should take adequate time to gather information, weigh risks and  
imagine potential consequences. Hasty judgements under pressure  
often lead to regrets. Of course, being overly hesitant is also  
problematic; we still need to set a reasonable time limit before reaching  
our final conclusion.  
中文翻译：  
我认为快速还是缓慢做决定，完全取决于事情的重要程度。选午饭、找娱乐这  
类日常琐事，快速抉择可以帮我们节约宝贵时间。但对于教育、职业、财务这  
类会改变人生的选择，人们应当留出充足时间收集信息、权衡风险，预想可能  
的后果。压力之下仓促判断往往会留下遗憾。当然，过度犹豫同样不可取，我  
们还是要设定合理时限再得出最终结论。

## PDF第5页

Q2. Do you think most people's decisions are made in their 20s?  
Answer：  
A great many critical life decisions do tend to take place in people’s  
twenties. During this period, young adults select their career paths,  
consider romantic relationships and decide which city they intend to  
settle in. Even so, it would be inaccurate to claim most decisions only  
happen at this age. Quite a number of individuals rethink their life  
trajectories in their 30s or 40s, switching jobs or completely changing  
lifestyles. Age is just one variable; real‑life experiences and  
opportunities also shape people’s major choices.  
中文翻译：  
大量人生关键抉择确实往往发生在人们二十多岁的时候。这个阶段年轻人挑选  
职业道路、考量亲密关系，决定未来定居的城市。但如果说大部分重大决定只  
发生在这个年纪，那就不够准确。不少人会在三十、四十岁重新思考人生轨  
迹，更换工作甚至彻底改变生活方式。年龄只是其中一个变量，现实经历与机  
遇同样会塑造人们的重大选择。  
Q3. What if people in a group have different opinions on a decision?  
Answer：

## PDF第6页

When group members hold conflicting opinions, open‑minded  
discussion and reasonable compromise become really vital. Every  
participant ought to get an opportunity to explain their logic and  
concerns, so the whole group can fully understand diverse perspectives.  
It is wrong for one single person to force others to follow his or her own  
will. After listening to all viewpoints, the group can look for a balanced  
solution. If total agreement cannot be reached, voting is also a practical  
way to move forward, though everyone still needs to respect the final  
outcome.  
中文翻译：  
当团队成员意见不一致时，开明的讨论和合理妥协就十分关键。每一位参与者  
都应当拥有机会阐述自己的逻辑与顾虑，让整个团队充分理解多元的想法。某  
一个人强迫所有人服从自己的意愿是不可取的。听完全部观点之后，团队可以  
寻找平衡的方案。如果无法达成完全共识，投票也是推进工作的务实手段，但  
所有人依旧需要尊重最终结果。  
Q4. What little decisions do people make every day?  
Answer：

## PDF第7页

People are surrounded by countless small decisions in their ordinary  
daily lives. For instance, they need to decide what to eat for meals,  
which transport to take, what clothes to wear and whether to scroll  
social media or read books in their spare time. Although these choices  
seem insignificant separately, they gradually add up and influence our  
daily rhythm and even personal habits. Most of these minor choices are  
made automatically without deep thinking, yet they still shape our  
day‑to‑day experiences.  
中文翻译：  
人们的日常生活中充斥着无数小决定。比如决定三餐吃什么、选择何种交通方  
式、穿什么衣服，闲暇时间刷社交媒体还是读书。这些选择单独看微不足道，  
但日积月累，会影响我们的生活节奏，甚至个人习惯。大部分小决定不需要深  
度思考就下意识完成，但依旧塑造着我们每一天的体验。  
Q5. Do you think it is a good idea to make a small decision every day?  
Answer：  
I think making conscious small decisions each day can bring positive  
impacts, as it gives us a sense of control over our own life. Simple daily

## PDF第8页

choices, such as choosing to exercise or avoid mindless phone‑scrolling,  
can build good habits little by little. However, it is not sensible to  
over‑analyse every tiny detail. If we spend huge energy overthinking  
trivial things, we will waste limited mental resources. So we should strike  
a balance between active choice and automatic routines.  
中文翻译：  
我认为每天有意识地做一些小决定可以带来积极影响，它让我们拥有对生活的  
掌控感。比如选择运动、拒绝无意义刷手机这类简单日常抉择，可以一点点养  
成好习惯。但过度剖析每一件细枝末节也不明智。如果耗费大量脑力纠结鸡毛  
蒜皮，就会浪费有限的心理精力。所以我们要在主动抉择和惯性日常之间找到  
平衡。  
Q6. How can we tell the difference between an important and an easy  
decision?  
Answer：  
We can distinguish important decisions from trivial ones mainly by  
looking at their long‑term influence. Important decisions will generate  
far‑reaching consequences for our study, career or family life, and their  
effects may last for many years. By contrast, easy small‑scale decisions

## PDF第9页

only affect a short period of time and can be easily corrected later.  
Besides, important decisions usually involve higher risks, which means  
bad choices may bring hard‑to‑reverse negative results. That is why they  
demand more careful thought.  
中文翻译：  
区分重大决定和简单决定，主要看它带来的长期影响。重大决定会对我们学  
习、事业、家庭产生深远后果，影响可能持续很多年。与之相比，简单的小决  
定只会影响很短一段时间，事后很容易修正。除此之外，重大决定往往伴随更  
高风险，错误选择会带来难以挽回的负面结果。这也是为什么它们需要更加慎  
重的思考。

✨Part2 新题 2 做困难事情并成功的人  
Describe a person you know who did something difficult and was  
successful  
You should say:  
‑ Who this person is

## PDF第10页

‑ What difficult thing this person did  
‑ Why this person was successful  
And explain how you feel about this person  
Answer：  
The person I would like to describe is my cousin, who managed to  
launch his own small online business despite numerous obstacles three  
years ago. Before starting the project, he worked as an ordinary office  
worker with limited income and no relevant entrepreneurial experience.  
His goal was to sell handmade cultural‑creative products online, yet he  
faced many tough barriers. He had almost no initial capital, lacked  
marketing know‑how, and received doubts from several family members  
who worried he would lose all his savings. Instead of giving up under  
pressure, he spent every evening after work learning e‑commerce  
knowledge, studying customer preferences and refining his product  
designs. He tolerated slow sales in the early stage and constantly  
adjusted his strategies based on real‑world feedback. Step by step, his  
store gained more customers and stable profits. I really admire him  
because his success did not come from luck. It came from consistent  
hard work, patience and the courage to keep trying when things went  
wrong. His experience has inspired me to persist when I encounter

## PDF第11页

tough challenges in my own life.  
中文翻译：  
我想描述的人是我的表哥，三年前，他克服重重困难，创办了属于自己的小型  
线上店铺。创业之前，他只是一名普通上班族，收入有限，也没有任何创业相  
关经验。他的目标是在网上售卖手工文创产品，但遇到了重重阻碍。他几乎没  
有启动资金，不懂营销技巧，不少家人也表示质疑，担心他会赔光积蓄。面对  
压力他没有放弃，每天下班之后利用夜晚时间学习电商知识，研究客户喜好，  
打磨产品设计。他忍受了初期惨淡的销量，根据真实反馈不断调整经营策略。  
一步步，他的店铺收获更多顾客，也实现稳定盈利。我十分敬佩他，他的成功  
并非运气，而是源于坚持不懈的努力、耐心以及遭遇挫折依旧不断尝试的勇  
气。他的经历激励着我，当生活遇到难题时不要轻易退缩。  
Part3  
Q1. In which sectors are companies more likely to succeed in China?  
Answer：  
In contemporary China, consumer‑oriented sectors such as e‑commerce,

## PDF第12页

cultural creativity and health‑related services have relatively high  
chances of achieving success. These industries match the growing  
demands of ordinary citizens. Nevertheless, success is never guaranteed  
even in popular sectors. Market competition can be extremely fierce,  
and companies still need reliable products, thoughtful customer service  
and reasonable marketing strategies. No sector can promise easy profits  
for every participant.  
中文翻译：  
在当代中国，电商、文创、健康服务这类面向消费者的行业更容易取得成功。  
这些行业契合普通民众日益增长的需求。但就算身处热门赛道，成功也不是板  
上钉钉。市场竞争会异常激烈，企业依旧需要靠谱的产品、贴心客服和合理营  
销。没有哪个行业可以保证所有从业者轻松获利。  
Q2. Is it easy to start up a company?  
Answer：  
Frankly speaking, starting a company is far from easy in modern society.  
New entrepreneurs need to handle capital flow, product development,  
marketing and risk management all at the same time. Many start‑ups

## PDF第13页

fail within the first two years of operation. Although online platforms  
lower some entry barriers, intense market competition still creates huge  
pressure. People should make thorough preparations rather than  
holding unrealistic optimistic fantasies.  
中文翻译：  
坦白说，在现代社会创业绝非易事。新创业者要同时处理资金流转、产品开  
发、营销、风险管理诸多事务。大量初创企业开业头两年就宣告失败。虽然线  
上平台降低了一部分入行门槛，但激烈的市场竞争依旧带来巨大压力。人们应  
当做好充分准备，而不是抱有不切实际的乐观幻想。  
Q3. Other than making money, what is more important for a company?  
Answer：  
Apart from profit‑making, several things carry great weight for a  
company. First of all, maintaining product quality and honest business  
values helps build long‑term customer trust. Besides, taking social  
responsibility such as environmental protection and proper employee  
welfare also matters. A company that only chases short‑term profits will  
easily damage its public reputation and face hidden risks in the long

## PDF第14页

run.  
中文翻译：  
除盈利之外，还有几件事对企业至关重要。首先，坚守产品品质与诚信商业理  
念，可以建立长久的客户信任。此外，承担社会责任，例如环保、保障员工福  
利也很重要。一家只追逐短期利润的企业很容易损害公众口碑，长远来看潜藏  
诸多风险。  
Q4. Does money equal success?  
Answer：  
I definitely do not believe money is equal to success. Wealth is just one  
possible symbol of achievement. Success can take various different  
forms. For example, teachers gain success when they help students  
make progress, and doctors achieve a sense of fulfilment when they  
save patients’ lives. Without personal satisfaction, good relationships  
or positive social influence, large amounts of money alone cannot  
represent real success.

## PDF第15页

中文翻译：  
我绝不认为金钱等同于成功。财富只是成就的其中一种外在符号。成功拥有多  
种多样的表现形式。比如老师帮助学生取得进步就是一种成功，医生救治病患  
也能获得成就感。如果缺少内心满足、良好人际关系与正向社会价值，单单拥  
有大量金钱，并不能代表真正的成功。  
Q5. How can companies help protect the environment?  
Answer：  
Companies can contribute to environmental protection in multiple  
practical ways. They can cut waste production, adopt clean energy and  
use recyclable materials during production procedures. Moreover, they  
can launch public‑welfare campaigns to raise ordinary people’s  
environmental awareness. It is worth noting that environmental  
investment may raise short‑term costs, but it brings long‑term benefits  
for both society and the enterprise itself.  
中文翻译：  
企业可以通过很多实际方式助力环保。生产环节减少废弃物排放、使用清洁能  
源、选用可回收原材料。除此之外，企业还可以发起公益宣传，提升大众环保

## PDF第16页

意识。值得注意，环保投入会抬升短期成本，但长远来看，对社会和企业自身  
都有益处。  
Q6. Is it difficult to do business in today's world?  
Answer：  
Running a business in today’s world is quite challenging for most  
people. Rapid technological updates and fast‑changing consumer  
preferences force companies to keep adjusting themselves. Global and  
domestic market competition is intense. Meanwhile businesses also  
need to obey complex industry rules and deal with unexpected risks  
such as economic fluctuations. Even experienced business owners still  
face constant new challenges.  
中文翻译：  
对大多数人而言，当今时代经商压力不小。技术迭代飞快，消费者喜好瞬息万  
变，倒逼企业持续自我革新。国内和市场竞争十分激烈。同时企业还要遵守繁  
杂行业规范，应对经济波动这类突发风险。就算是经验丰富的经营者，也会不  
断遇到全新难题。

✨Part2 新题 3 快乐人士  
Describe a happy person you know  
You should say:  
‑ Who this person is  
‑ What he/she is like  
‑ How he/she shows happiness  
And explain why you think he/she is a happy person  
Answer：  
The happy person I would like to talk about is my maternal aunt, who is  
in her early forties. She works as a primary‑school art teacher and leads  
a simple but fulfilling daily life. Physically she looks ordinary, but she  
always wears a warm smile on her face and treats everyone around her  
in a gentle, easy‑going manner. She expresses her happiness in very  
down‑to‑earth little ways. For example, she will share delicious  
home‑cooked food with neighbours, take photos of beautiful sunset on

## PDF第18页

her way home, or chat joyfully with her students after class. She does  
not chase fancy luxury goods or high social status. Instead, she finds joy  
from small everyday moments. Even when she meets minor troubles in  
work or family life, she rarely complains. She tends to look on the bright  
side of things and tries to find solutions calmly. I believe she is  
genuinely happy, not because she lives a perfectly trouble‑free life, but  
because she owns a positive mindset. She knows how to appreciate  
ordinary beauty around her and keep a peaceful heart regardless of ups  
and downs. This optimistic personality makes her life full of warmth and  
contentment.  
中文翻译：  
我想聊的快乐的人是我的姨妈，四十出头。她是一名小学美术老师，过着简单  
却充实的生活。她长相普通，但脸上总带着温暖的笑容，对待身边所有人都温  
和随和。她用十分接地气的小方式流露快乐。比如，她会把自家做的美食分给  
邻居，回家路上拍下美丽的落日，课后开心地和学生聊天。她不去追逐昂贵奢  
侈品或者很高的社会地位，而是从日常细碎小事中寻找喜悦。就算工作家庭遇  
到小麻烦，她也很少抱怨，总是看到事情积极的一面，冷静寻找解决办法。我  
觉得她真正快乐，并不是因为她的人生一帆风顺毫无烦恼，而是因为她拥有积  
极的心态。无论境遇起伏，她懂得欣赏身边平凡的美好，守住平和的内心。这  
份乐观的性格让她的生活充满温暖与满足。

Part3  
Q1. When do you think people are happiest?  
Answer：  
People tend to feel happiest in different life stages and situations. Many  
individuals gain great happiness when they achieve long‑awaited  
personal goals, such as graduating from school or landing an ideal job.  
Some others feel the greatest joy when staying with beloved family and  
close friends. Actually happiness is not only linked to major big events;  
lots of people also get satisfaction from quiet, ordinary daily moments.  
中文翻译：  
人们感到最幸福的时刻分布在不同人生阶段与场景。很多人实现期盼已久目标  
的时候，比如毕业、拿到理想工作，会感受到巨大幸福。还有一部分人和挚爱  
家人、好友相伴时最快乐。其实幸福不只和重大事件挂钩，很多人也从安静平  
凡的日常片段中获得满足感。

## PDF第20页

Q2. How can the arts spread positive emotions in society?  
Answer：  
Art forms including music, paintings and films can deliver positive  
feelings effectively. Good artistic works can convey hope, kindness and  
courage to audiences. When people appreciate these pieces, they may  
resonate with the emotions inside and get comfort from them. Shared  
artistic experiences also connect different groups of people together,  
spreading optimistic attitudes across the whole community.  
中文翻译：  
音乐、绘画、电影这类艺术形式可以有效传递积极情绪。优秀的艺术作品向受  
众传递希望、善良与勇气。人们欣赏作品时，会和里面的情感产生共鸣，从中  
获得慰藉。共同的艺术体验还可以把不同群体联结起来，向整个社会传播乐观  
的态度。  
Q3. Do you think teachers are able to spot the unhappy children?  
Answer：

## PDF第21页

Teachers can often notice unhappy children to a certain degree, since  
they spend long hours observing students’ behaviour in classrooms.  
Changes such as losing interest in class activities or withdrawing from  
peers can serve as warning signals. Even so, some children hide  
negative feelings deeply. Teachers cannot fully understand every  
student’s inner world without sincere one‑on‑one communication.  
中文翻译：  
在一定程度上老师可以察觉到不开心的孩子，毕竟老师长时间观察学生课堂表  
现。比如对课堂活动失去兴趣、回避同伴交往，这些都是警示信号。但有些孩  
子会深深掩藏负面情绪。缺少真诚一对一沟通，老师无法完全读懂每一位学生  
的内心。  
Q4. Should teachers know what children are doing all the time?  
Answer：  
I do not believe teachers need to monitor children’s every single move  
all the time. Young people require suitable personal space to develop  
independent personalities. Teachers’ main responsibility is to offer  
guidance when students face risks or serious difficulties. Constant

## PDF第22页

surveillance will harm mutual trust and prevent children from learning  
self‑management abilities.  
中文翻译：  
我认为老师不需要时时刻刻监控孩子的一举一动。青少年需要适度个人空间，  
来发展独立人格。老师的核心职责是，当学生面临风险、重大困境的时候给予  
引导。无休止的监视会破坏互相信任，也不利于孩子学会自我管理。  
Q5. What would make a student happy at school?  
Answer：  
Several factors contribute to students’ happiness at school. First,  
positive relationships with classmates and supportive teachers play a  
vital role. Making progress in study and gaining a sense of achievement  
also brings joy. In addition, interesting after‑school activities and a  
relaxed campus atmosphere are beneficial. Too much heavy academic  
pressure will easily take away young students’ sense of happiness.  
中文翻译：

很多因素会影响学生在校的幸福感。首先，和同学相处融洽，得到老师的支持

Q1. What should people wear to a job interview?  
Answer：  
For job interviews, people’s clothing should match the culture of the  
specific industry they apply for. For formal sectors such as finance or  
law, neat formal suits are highly recommended. For creative industries  
like design, neat casual outfits are acceptable. The core principle is to  
look tidy, respectful and self‑confident. Over‑flashy or overly‑relaxed  
clothes may leave a negative first impression on interviewers.  
中文翻译：  
求职面试时，着装应当匹配所应聘行业的企业文化。金融、法律这类正式行  
业，整洁西装是首选。设计等创意行业，得体休闲装束也可以。核心原则是整

## PDF第27页

洁、体现尊重，展现自信。过于张扬或者过分随意的穿搭，容易给面试官留下  
不好的第一印象。  
Q2. Why do we need to attend interviews when applying for jobs?  
Answer：  
Job interviews serve as vital two‑way communication channels. On one  
hand, employers can observe applicants’ real‑life communication skills,  
personalities and problem‑solving abilities, which cannot be fully shown  
on resumes. On the other hand, candidates can learn more about the  
company’s working atmosphere and position requirements  
face‑to‑face. It helps both sides judge whether they are suitable for each  
other.  
中文翻译：  
求职面试是非常重要的双向沟通渠道。一方面，雇主可以观察求职者真实沟通  
能力、性格、解决问题的本事，简历无法完整展现这些特质。另一方面，求职  
者也可以面对面了解公司氛围和岗位要求。面试帮助双方判断彼此是否互相适  
配。

Q3. What should people do to prepare for a job interview?  
Answer：  
Good preparation covers several key points. First of all, applicants ought  
to research the company background and position requirements  
thoroughly. They also need to practise answering common interview  
questions and prepare practical examples from past experience. Besides,  
they should arrange their appearance in a proper way and plan their  
travel route in advance to avoid being late. Mental preparation to stay  
calm is equally significant.  
中文翻译：

高频面试题，准备过往经历的真实案例。同时打理好个人形象，提前规划出行  
路线防止迟到。做好心理建设、保持冷静同样很重要。

conversations still deliver information that written documents cannot

Describe a person you know who is very organized  
You should say:  
‑ Who this person is  
‑ What this person usually does to stay organized  
‑ In what situations you have noticed this quality  
And explain why you think being organized is important to him/her  
Answer：  
The well‑organized person I want to talk about is one of my university  
roommates. He majors in computer science and always keeps his study

## PDF第32页

and daily life in perfect order. He builds detailed to‑do lists every  
Sunday night to arrange tasks for the coming week. He divides big  
assignments into several small achievable steps and marks deadlines  
clearly. Besides study, he also keeps his personal desk, wardrobe and  
digital files neatly sorted. I have witnessed this outstanding quality on  
many occasions. When our group needed to finish a demanding course  
project last semester, he sorted out all the materials, assigned  
reasonable tasks for every team member and tracked each milestone.  
Thanks to his arrangement, our group avoided confusion and handed in  
high‑quality work ahead of schedule. For him, being organised is far  
more than a superficial neat habit. He tends to face heavy study  
pressure and numerous competing tasks. Good organisation helps him  
reduce unnecessary panic, avoid missing important deadlines and make  
full use of limited time. Without this trait, he would easily get  
overwhelmed by piles of academic work. That explains why he treats  
organisational skills as one of his most valuable personal strengths.  
中文翻译：  
我想聊的做事非常有条理的人，是我的一位大学室友。他主修计算机科学，学  
习和日常生活始终井井有条。每个周日晚上他都会制作详尽待办清单，规划下  
一周任务。把大型作业拆解成一个个可以完成的小步骤，清晰标注截止日期。

## PDF第33页

除学习之外，书桌、衣柜，甚至电子文件，他都会整理归类。很多场景下我都  
见识过他这个优点。上学期我们小组要完成难度很高的课程项目，他整理全部  
资料，给每位组员分配合理任务，跟进每一个进度节点。得益于他的安排，小  
组没有陷入混乱，还提前交出高质量成果。对他来说，有条理不只是表面爱整  
洁的习惯。他经常面对繁重学业和大量并行任务。良好规划帮他减少不必要的  
慌乱，避免错过截止时间，充分利用有限时间。如果没有这个特质，成堆的学  
习任务很容易压垮他。这也是为什么他把条理性视作自己最重要的个人优势之  
一。  
Part3  
Q1. Do you think it is important for managers to be well organized?  
Answer：  
Organisation is an extremely essential quality for managers. Managers  
are responsible for allocating work resources, setting project timelines  
and coordinating different team members. Without clear organisation,  
projects will easily fall into chaos, tasks will overlap and important steps  
may get ignored. Disorganised management will bring low efficiency  
and even financial losses for the whole company.

## PDF第34页

中文翻译：  
条理性对于管理者而言极其重要。管理者要分配工作资源，设定项目时间节  
点，协调不同团队成员。缺少清晰规划，项目很容易陷入混乱，任务重复，关  
键环节被忽略。管理层面缺乏条理，会造成整体效率低下，甚至给公司带来经  
济损失。  
Q2. Should parents teach their children to be organized?  
Answer：  
Parents ought to help children develop organised habits from an early  
age. Tidiness and planning are practical life skills instead of trivial  
requirements. Parents can guide kids to tidy toys, make simple plans for  
homework and arrange their spare time. These small practices gradually  
cultivate children’s sense of order, which will benefit their future study  
and adult life in the long run.  
中文翻译：  
家长应当从小帮助孩子养成有条理的习惯。整理、规划是实用生活技能，而非

## PDF第35页

无关紧要的小要求。家长可以引导孩子收拾玩具，为作业制定简单计划，安排  
课余时间。这些小小的练习逐步培养孩子秩序感，长远来看，会让他们未来的  
学习和成年生活受益。  
Q3. What can people do to become more organized?  
Answer：  
People can improve their organisational ability through several practical  
methods. First, they can write down to‑do lists and mark clear deadlines  
instead of keeping all tasks inside their heads. Second, break large  
overwhelming projects into small manageable stages. Additionally,  
setting aside fixed short periods for regular sorting, whether for  
physical objects or digital documents, also works well. Consistency  
matters more than one‑off huge efforts.  
中文翻译：  
人们可以通过几种实操方法提升条理性。第一，写下待办清单，标注明确截止  
时间，不要把全部任务都记在脑子里。第二，把庞大棘手的项目拆解成容易处  
理的小阶段。此外，留出固定短时间定期整理实体物品或者电子文档也很有  
效。持续坚持，远比一次性突击整理更加重要。

## PDF第36页

Q4. Are organized people more likely to be successful at work or in their  
studies?  
Answer：  
Generally speaking, organised individuals enjoy advantages in study  
and work, yet organisation alone cannot guarantee success. Good  
planning helps them manage pressure, complete tasks on time and  
reduce careless mistakes. However, real achievements also require  
creativity, professional knowledge, communication competence and  
good luck. Organisation serves as a helpful foundation rather than a  
magic ticket to guaranteed success.  
中文翻译：  
总体来看，有条理的人在学习、工作当中具备优势，但仅仅有条理并不能保证  
成功。良好规划帮助他们应对压力，按时完成任务，减少低级失误。但真正的  
成就同样需要创造力、专业知识、沟通能力以及机遇。条理性只是有益基础，  
并不是通往成功的万能通行证。  
Q5. Do you think children should learn to be organized from a young

## PDF第37页

age?  
Answer：  
I believe it is beneficial for children to build organised habits from early  
childhood. When kids are young, their living and study tasks are  
relatively simple, so it is the perfect time to form good behavioural  
patterns. That being said, parents should not set overly strict standards.  
Too rigid requirements may create unnecessary mental burden for  
young children. Gentle guidance is much more suitable.  
中文翻译：  
我认为孩子从小养成条理习惯是有益的。孩童时期生活学习任务相对简单，是  
塑造良好行为模式的黄金时期。虽说如此，家长不应当设置过分严苛的标准。  
死板的条条框框会给小孩子带来不必要心理负担，温和引导会更加合适。  
Q6. Why is it important for people to be organized?  
Answer：  
Being organised brings multiple practical benefits to ordinary people. It

## PDF第38页

helps us make full use of limited time, lower the risk of forgetting  
important duties and reduce unnecessary anxiety caused by mess. Both  
study‑related tasks and daily household affairs become much easier to  
handle. Without basic organisation, people tend to waste plenty of  
energy dealing with chaos that could have been avoided.  
中文翻译：  
做事有条理会给普通人带来多重实际好处。帮助我们充分利用有限时间，降低  
遗忘重要事务的风险，减少混乱带来的无谓焦虑。不管是学习任务还是日常家  
务，处理起来都会轻松很多。缺少基本条理性，人们就要耗费大量精力去处理  
本可以避免的混乱。

✨Part2 新题 6 嘈杂的地方  
Describe a noisy place you have been to  
You should say:  
‑ Where it is

## PDF第39页

‑ When you went there  
‑ What you did there  
And explain why you feel it’s a noisy place  
Answer：  
The noisy place I want to describe is a large‑scale commercial food  
street located in the downtown area of my city. I visited this spot with  
several good friends on a Saturday evening about two months ago. We  
planned to hang out together, try various local snacks and spend some  
relaxing leisure time. After we arrived, we walked along the street,  
looked at different food stalls, tasted several popular street foods and  
chatted with each other. This place is extremely noisy for several  
obvious reasons. First of all, dozens of food vendors shout loudly to  
attract passing customers. Large crowds of visitors talk, laugh and call  
out to their companions everywhere. Meanwhile background music  
from small shops and loud vehicle noises from nearby main roads also  
mix into the environment. All these different kinds of sound overlap  
together. Even when my friends and I stood quite close to each other,  
we still needed to raise our voices so we could hear one another clearly.  
Although the noise level was fairly high, I still had a nice night there.  
The loud atmosphere reflected its liveliness and popularity.

## PDF第40页

Nevertheless, staying there for too long made my ears feel  
uncomfortable, and I was quite relieved when we finally left the noisy  
area.  
中文翻译：  
我想描述的嘈杂地点，是我们市中心一条大型美食商业街。大概两个月前一个  
周六晚上，我和好几个朋友来到这里。我们打算一起逛逛街，尝尝各类本地小  
吃，度过一段放松的闲暇时光。到达之后，我们沿着街道闲逛，浏览各个小吃  
摊位，品尝热门小吃，互相聊天。这个地方十分嘈杂有几个很直观的原因。首  
先，几十个摊贩大声吆喝招揽过路顾客。大批游客到处交谈、大笑、呼喊同  
伴。与此同时，小店播放的背景音乐，临近主干道车辆的喧闹声也混杂进来。  
各种各样的声音叠加在一起。就算我和朋友站得很近，也必须提高音量，才能  
听清对方说话。尽管噪音很大，当晚我玩得还算开心，喧闹恰恰体现这里人气  
旺盛、生机勃勃。不过待久了耳朵会很不舒服，离开这片嘈杂区域的时候我才  
算松了一口气。  
Part3  
Q1. How can people consider others' feelings when chatting in public?

## PDF第41页

Answer：  
People can respect others in public by controlling their speaking  
volume. They ought to avoid shouting or having extremely heated loud  
conversations in quiet spaces such as libraries or subway carriages.  
Besides, it is better not to discuss highly private personal information in  
front of strangers. Being mindful of surrounding people does not  
require huge sacrifices, only basic self‑awareness of one’s own  
behaviour.  
中文翻译：  
在公共场合交谈时，人们可以通过控制说话音量顾及他人感受。在图书馆、地  
铁车厢这类安静环境，不要高声喧哗、激烈大声争论。同时尽量不要当着陌生  
人的面谈论高度私人的话题。顾及旁人不需要做出巨大牺牲，只需要对自身行  
为保有基本自觉。  
Q2. How would people usually respond to noises in your country?  
Answer：  
Chinese people react to noises according to specific situations. For  
temporary unavoidable noises such as busy street sounds, most citizens

## PDF第42页

will choose to tolerate them. But for continuous disturbing noise from  
neighbours or construction sites, many people will take action. They  
may communicate with the people who create noise or turn to relevant  
management departments to solve the problem.  
中文翻译：  
中国人面对噪音会根据不同场景做出不同反应。对于街道喧嚣这类无法避免的  
短暂噪音，大部分民众会选择包容。但如果是邻居、工地持续制造的扰民噪  
音，很多人会采取行动，和制造噪音的一方沟通，或者向相关管理部门求助解  
决问题。  
Q3. Which area is exposed to noise more, the city or the countryside?  
Answer：  
Cities generally suffer far more noise pollution compared with rural  
countryside. Urban areas have heavy traffic, dense crowds, commercial  
activities and ongoing construction projects, all of which produce  
constant noise. In the countryside, vehicle flow and human‑made  
activities are much less frequent. Of course, certain rural zones near  
highways or factories will also face noise troubles, but that is not the

## PDF第43页

mainstream condition.  
中文翻译：  
总体来讲，城市比乡村遭受更多噪音污染。城市交通繁忙、人口密集，商业活  
动、工地施工源源不断制造噪音。乡村地区车流和人为活动少很多。当然，靠  
近高速、工厂的部分乡村同样会受噪音困扰，但这不属于普遍情况。  
Q4. What kinds of noises are there in our life?  
Answer：  
We meet various categories of noise in daily life. Traffic noise from cars,  
buses and motorbikes is extremely common. Then there is human‑made  
noise such as loud talking, shouting and music. Besides, mechanical  
noise comes from building construction, home appliances and factory  
equipment. Some noises come from entertaining activities, while others  
are annoying and harmful to people’s hearing and mental health.  
中文翻译：  
日常生活中我们会遇到各式各样噪音。汽车、公交、摩托车带来的交通噪音最

## PDF第44页

为常见。还有人大声交谈、喊叫、播放音乐这类人为噪音。另外还有工地施  
工、家电、工厂设备带来的机械噪音。一部分噪音来自娱乐活动，也有一部分  
噪音十分扰民，损害听力和心理健康。  
Q5. Should children not be allowed to make noise under any  
circumstances?  
Answer：  
It would be unreasonable to forbid children from making noise in every  
single situation. Kids naturally express excitement through shouting and  
laughing when they play. What matters is the appropriate occasion. In  
quiet public areas like libraries or hospitals, children should be guided  
to keep quiet. While in playgrounds or amusement parks, joyful noise  
from playing kids is totally acceptable.  
中文翻译：  
不分任何场景都禁止孩子发出噪音是不合理的。孩子玩耍的时候，会本能通过  
喊叫大笑抒发兴奋情绪。关键在于分清场合。图书馆、医院这类安静公共空  
间，应当引导孩子保持安静。而在游乐场，孩子们玩耍带来的欢快声响是完全  
可以接受的。

## PDF第45页

Q6. Do you think it is good for children to make noise?  
Answer：  
Appropriate noise from playing children is generally a positive sign. It  
shows that youngsters are active, curious and enjoying themselves. If  
children stay completely silent all the time, that may even signal  
emotional problems. However, uncontrolled loud noise everywhere is  
not desirable. Adults need to teach kids to distinguish suitable  
occasions for being noisy versus being quiet.  
中文翻译：  
孩子玩耍时适度发出声响总体来说是好事，代表孩子活泼、好奇，玩得很投  
入。如果孩子时时刻刻完全沉默，反而有可能暗示情绪方面存在问题。但不分  
场合肆无忌惮大吵大闹并不可取。成年人要教会孩子区分，哪些场景可以尽情  
玩耍，哪些场合需要保持安静。

Part3

## PDF第48页

Q1. Which is more important, protecting the environment or developing  
the economy?  
Answer：  
In my opinion, environmental protection and economic development  
should go hand‑in‑hand instead of competing against each other. If we  
only chase short‑term economic growth at the cost of nature, polluted  
air and water will eventually damage people’s health and create huge  
repair costs later on. At the same time, extremely strict environmental  
rules that completely block economic progress are also impractical.  
Governments need to seek balanced solutions, encouraging green  
industries so we can enjoy both prosperous economy and a healthy  
natural environment.  
中文翻译：  
在我看来，环境保护与经济发展应当协同共进，而不是互相矛盾。如果我们只  
追逐短期经济增长，不惜牺牲自然，被污染的空气水源最终会损害民众健康，  
后续修复会付出巨大代价。与此同时，严苛到完全阻碍经济进步的环保条例同  
样不切实际。政府需要寻找平衡方案，扶持绿色产业，让我们同时拥有繁荣经  
济与健康自然环境。

## PDF第49页

Q2. Do you think staying in a natural place would help change  
people’s mood?  
Answer：  
Absolutely, spending time in natural surroundings has noticeable effects  
on people’s emotional state. Scientific research has shown that green  
scenery, fresh air and sounds of wildlife can lower stress‑related  
hormones in human bodies. When people feel exhausted or frustrated  
from city work and study, getting close to nature can ease negative  
feelings. Of course, nature cannot solve deep‑seated psychological  
troubles, but it serves as a simple and effective way to lift people’s  
daily mood.  
中文翻译：  
毫无疑问，身处自然环境对人的情绪有明显作用。科学研究表明，绿色风景、  
新鲜空气以及野生动物的声音，可以降低人体内压力相关激素。当人们因为城  
市学习工作身心疲惫、内心沮丧时，亲近大自然能够消解负面情绪。当然大自  
然无法解决深层次心理问题，但它是改善日常情绪简单有效的方式。

## PDF第50页

Q3. Do you think there should be many natural places in cities?  
Answer：  
I firmly believe modern cities ought to reserve abundant natural spaces  
for residents. Urban parks, wetlands and small green belts improve air  
quality, reduce city heat‑island effects and offer citizens easy access to  
relaxation. Without enough natural areas, city dwellers will be trapped  
among concrete buildings all year round. These natural spots do not  
have to be huge; even small community gardens can bring great  
benefits to people’s mental wellbeing.  
中文翻译：  
我坚定认为现代城市应当为居民保留充足自然空间。城市公园、湿地、小型绿  
化带改善空气质量，缓解城市热岛效应，给市民提供触手可及的放松场所。缺  
少足够自然空间，城市居民一整年都会困在钢筋水泥建筑之中。这些自然场所  
不必规模宏大，就算小型社区花园，也能极大促进民众心理健康。

Part3  
Q1. Should people spend time on charity, or just money?  
Answer：  
I believe both donating money and devoting personal time to charity  
carry unique value. Financial donations offer material support for  
disadvantaged groups. However, volunteering time can deliver  
emotional care, face‑to‑face companionship and practical labour that  
money cannot buy. For instance, visiting elderly people in nursing  
homes or tutoring underprivileged children require people’s physical  
presence. Ideally, individuals can contribute in both ways according to  
their real‑life conditions.  
中文翻译：  
我认为做慈善，出钱和投入时间都拥有各自独特价值。捐款可以给弱势群体提  
供物质支持。但奉献志愿时间，可以带来金钱无法买到的情感关怀、面对面陪

## PDF第54页

伴和实际劳动。例如看望养老院老人、辅导家境困难孩子，就需要人亲自到  
场。理想情况下，人们可以结合自身现实条件，两种方式都做一点贡献。  
Q2. What kind of things do people usually save money for?  
Answer：  
People save money for various purposes in daily life. Many people set  
aside savings for large‑ticket consumer goods such as electronic devices  
or travel experiences. Some save for major life events including further  
education, wedding ceremonies or purchasing housing. Besides, plenty  
of people build emergency savings to deal with unexpected accidents  
or health‑related issues. Saving goals shift as individuals move through  
different life stages.  
中文翻译：  
日常生活中，人们存钱有着各种各样的目的。很多人为电子产品、旅行体验这  
类大额消费品存钱。一部分人为升学、婚礼、买房这类人生大事储蓄。除此之  
外，不少人会存应急资金，用来应对突发意外或者健康问题。人生阶段发生变  
化，人们的存钱目标也会随之改变。

## PDF第55页

Q3. Is it easier to spend money or save money?  
Answer：  
For most ordinary consumers, spending money tends to be much easier  
than saving money. Modern online shopping platforms and attractive  
advertising constantly encourage people to make instant purchases. It  
takes strong self‑discipline to resist temptation and cut unnecessary  
expenses. That said, some people with strong financial awareness find  
saving a natural habit. Still, resisting consumption temptation remains a  
real challenge for the majority of the public.  
中文翻译：  
对于大多数普通消费者，花钱通常比存钱容易得多。现代网购平台和极具吸引  
力的广告，不停鼓动人们立刻消费。抵制诱惑、削减不必要开支，需要很强自  
律能力。当然，部分理财意识很强的人，存钱已经成为本能习惯。但对大部分  
普通人来说，抵抗消费诱惑依旧是实实在在的挑战。  
Q4. Do you think it is easier to teach teenagers how to manage money  
when they are around 16 or 17 years old?

## PDF第56页

Answer：  
Sixteen or seventeen‑year‑old teenagers are at a suitable age to learn  
money management. At this stage, young people start to form  
independent thinking and gradually face more consumption choices of  
their own. They can receive pocket money and practise simple  
budgeting. Even so, teenagers are still easily influenced by peer  
pressure and commercial advertising. Therefore, parental guidance  
remains essential while giving them space to make small financial  
mistakes and learn valuable lessons.  
中文翻译：  
十六七岁的青少年，正是学习理财的合适年纪。这个阶段年轻人开始拥有独立  
思考能力，会面对越来越多属于自己的消费选择。他们可以拿到零花钱，练习  
简单预算规划。即便如此，青少年依旧容易受同辈攀比和商业广告影响。所  
以，给予他们试错空间的同时，父母的引导依旧必不可少，让他们从小小的金  
钱失误中吸取经验。  
Q5. Should parents teach their children to spend money freely or to  
save money?

## PDF第57页

Answer：  
Parents should neither teach children to spend without restriction nor  
force them to save every single penny. The key is to develop balanced  
financial awareness. Kids need to understand that money is earned  
through hard work. They can spend money on things they truly value,  
but they also ought to learn to reserve savings for future needs. Both  
rational spending and reasonable saving form the core of healthy  
financial habits.  
中文翻译：  
家长既不该教孩子无节制消费，也不能逼迫孩子把每一分钱都存起来。关键是  
培养平衡的金钱观念。孩子需要懂得金钱来自辛苦劳动。他们可以为真正有价  
值的事物花钱，但同时也要学会为未来需求储备存款。理性消费加上适度储  
蓄，共同构成健康金钱习惯的核心。  
Q6. Should schools teach children how to manage money from a young  
age?  
Answer：  
I think basic money‑management knowledge deserves a place in school

## PDF第58页

education. Many young people only gain financial knowledge from  
family, and family concepts vary hugely. Schools can offer neutral,  
systematic lessons about budgeting, saving and the risks of  
over‑consumption. These practical life skills will benefit teenagers long  
after graduation, even though finance will not become a major exam  
subject.  
中文翻译：  
我认为基础理财知识应当纳入学校教育。很多年轻人只能从家庭获取金钱观  
念，而各个家庭的观念差异巨大。学校可以提供中立系统课程，教会学生做预  
算、储蓄，以及过度消费的风险。这些实用生活技能，即便不会作为考试主  
科，也会让青少年毕业之后长期受益。

✨Part2 新题 9 小时候学到的新技能  
Describe a new skill you learned when you were a child  
You should say:

## PDF第59页

‑ What the skill was  
‑ Who taught you this skill  
‑ How you learned it  
And explain how you felt about learning the skill  
Answer：  
The new skill I learned as a child was riding a regular two‑wheel bicycle,  
when I was about nine years old. My father was the person who  
patiently taught me this practical life skill. Before that, I only rode small  
bikes with auxiliary training wheels. When those side wheels were  
removed, I constantly lost balance and fell down many times. At the  
beginning, I felt frightened and wanted to give up several times. My  
father did not push me hard. Instead, he ran beside me, held the back of  
the bike seat firmly, gave me clear safety tips and encouraged me not to  
fear small falls. We practised on the flat open ground in our community  
every weekend. Little by little, I began to keep my balance for short  
distances. One day unexpectedly, my father quietly let go without  
telling me, and I managed to ride forward all by myself. The moment I  
realised I could ride independently, I was filled with excitement and  
pride. This experience not only gave me a useful transportation skill, but  
also delivered an important lesson: progress often comes after repeated

## PDF第60页

failures and continuous practice. That sense of achievement still stays  
fresh in my memory up to the present day.  
中文翻译：  
我小时候学会的一项新技能，是骑普通两轮自行车，那时候我大约九岁。是我  
的父亲耐心教会我这项实用生活技能。在此之前，我只会骑带辅助轮的小车。  
拆掉辅助轮之后，我频频失去平衡，摔了很多次。一开始我很害怕，好几次想  
要放弃。父亲没有逼迫我，他在我旁边跟着跑，稳稳扶住车后座，清晰告诉我  
安全要点，鼓励我不要畏惧小小的摔倒。每个周末我们就在小区平坦空地上练  
习。慢慢地，我可以短时间稳住平衡。有一天，父亲悄悄松开手没有告诉我，  
我竟然独自骑出去很远。当我意识到自己可以独立骑行，内心满是激动和自  
豪。这段经历不光让我掌握一项出行技能，还给我上了重要一课：进步往往来  
自反复失败和持续练习。那份成就感直到现在依旧历历在目。  
Part3  
Q1. Is it important for everyone to have a specialized skill?  
Answer：

## PDF第61页

Possessing a specialised skill brings great advantages for individuals, yet  
it may not be absolutely necessary for every single person. Professional  
skills improve people’s competitiveness in the job market and help  
them realise personal value. However, society also needs  
general‑talented people who are good at coordination and  
communication. What really counts is having one or more solid  
strengths, whether they are technical specialised skills or soft  
interpersonal abilities.  
中文翻译：  
拥有一项专业技能会给个人带来很大优势，但并不是对每个人来说都绝对必不  
可少。专业技能提升人们职场竞争力，帮助实现个人价值。但社会同样需要擅  
长协调沟通的综合性人才。真正重要的是拥有一项或多项过硬长处，不管是硬  
核专业技术，还是人际软实力。  
Q2. Can a child learn skills from another child?  
Answer：  
Children frequently pick up new skills from their peers. When kids play  
together, they observe one another’s behaviour and imitate useful

## PDF第62页

abilities. For example, they can learn drawing, ball games or simple  
hand‑craft techniques from playmates. Learning from other children  
often feels less stressful than learning from adults. Nevertheless, peer  
learning also has risks; bad habits can get copied just as easily as  
positive skills.  
中文翻译：  
孩子经常可以从同龄人身上学到新技能。孩子们一起玩耍的时候，会互相观察  
模仿，习得有用的能力。比如可以从玩伴那里学会画画、球类运动或者简单手  
工。向同伴学习，相比向大人学习压力往往更小。不过同伴学习也存在风险，  
坏习惯和好习惯一样很容易被模仿。  
Q3. Do you think it is easier for children to learn new skills than for  
adults?  
Answer：  
Generally speaking, children tend to learn new skills faster than adults.  
Their brains are more flexible and they have fewer fixed old habits to  
break. Young learners are also less afraid of making mistakes. Even so,  
adults have their own strengths: richer life experience and stronger

## PDF第63页

self‑control. Adults can understand complicated principles logically, so  
they may master complex professional skills more efficiently under the  
right conditions.  
中文翻译：  
总体来看，孩子学习新技能通常比成年人更快。他们大脑可塑性更强，没有太  
多固化旧习惯需要改掉。年轻学习者也不那么害怕犯错。不过成年人也具备自  
身优势：更加丰富的人生阅历，更强自控力。成年人可以逻辑理解复杂原理，  
条件合适的时候，掌握复杂专业技能效率也会很高。  
Q4. What are the differences between the way adults learn and the way  
children learn?  
Answer：  
Children mostly learn skills through imitation, play and repeated  
practice. They accept new knowledge intuitively without questioning  
too much. By contrast, adults prefer to understand underlying logic and  
principles first before putting knowledge into practice. Adults worry  
more about failure and wasting time. While children enjoy the process  
itself, adults often focus on practical results and real‑world application

## PDF第64页

of new‑learned skills.  
中文翻译：  
孩子大多依靠模仿、玩耍、反复练习来学技能，凭直觉接纳新知识，不会过多  
质疑。与之对比，成年人更喜欢先弄懂底层逻辑原理，再动手实践。成年人更  
担心失败，害怕浪费时间。孩子享受学习过程本身，而成年人往往更看重实际  
结果，以及新学技能的现实用途。  
Q5. What are the differences between learning in a group and learning  
by yourself?  
Answer：  
Group‑based learning creates motivation through peer interaction.  
Learners can exchange ideas, correct each other’s mistakes and gain  
inspiration from teammates. However, group study may get distracted  
by chatting. Self‑study gives people complete freedom to control  
learning pace and content. Still, solo learners lack instant feedback and  
may get stuck when they encounter confusing points without outside  
help. Both approaches have distinct pros and cons.

## PDF第65页

中文翻译：  
小组学习依靠同伴互动激发动力。学习者互相交换想法，纠正错误，从同伴身  
上获取灵感。但小组学习容易闲聊分心。自学可以完全自由掌控学习节奏和内  
容。不过自学缺少即时反馈，遇到困惑卡点的时候，没有外界帮助就容易停滞  
不前。两种学习方式各有明显优缺点。  
Q6. What skills should children learn before they go to school?  
Answer：  
Before starting formal school education, children ought to master basic  
life and social skills. Simple self‑care skills such as dressing themselves  
or washing hands are essential. Besides, they need to learn how to listen  
to others, share toys and follow simple rules. Academic knowledge is  
not the priority at this stage. Good behavioural and social skills lay solid  
foundations for their future classroom study.  
中文翻译：  
正式入学之前，孩子应当掌握基础生活与社交技能。自己穿衣洗手这类简单自  
理能力必不可少。此外他们要学会倾听他人、分享玩具、遵守简单规则。这个  
阶段学科知识并不是首要任务。良好行为社交能力，为之后课堂学习打下坚实

## PDF第66页

基础。

warm and vivid. I really admire her passion for photography. For her,  
taking photos is more than just a hobby to kill spare time. It serves as a  
special way for her to record time and preserve precious memories.  
Watching her works encourages me to slow down and notice those  
easily‑overlooked beautiful little moments in my own everyday life.

机。她拍照的地点十分广泛。我们去到旅游景点，她会拍下自然风光与当地人  
文特色。平常校园日子里，她也捕捉平凡瞬间：春日樱花、操场落日，或是课  
间欢笑的同学们。她不执着于宏大著名风景，反而擅长从日常细碎场景发现  
美。她会花费大量空闲时间调整光线色彩，打磨照片成品。她很多作品画面温

暖鲜活。我很欣赏她对摄影的热爱。对她来说，拍照不只是打发时间的爱好，  
更是记录时光、留存珍贵回忆的独特方式。欣赏她的作品，也鼓励我慢下来，  
留意自己生活里那些容易被忽略的美好小瞬间。

Q1. Some people don't mind others walking into their shot when they're  
taking photos. Why?

Different attitudes towards strangers entering photographs mainly  
come from people’s expectations for their pictures. Some people only  
take casual documentary‑style snapshots for memory. They do not  
pursue perfect, flawless frames, so passers‑by appearing in the  
background do not trouble them. On the other hand, those preparing  
carefully‑composed artistic shots will feel annoyed. They invest lots of  
time waiting for ideal conditions and hope to get clean, well‑arranged  
final images.  
中文翻译：

对于陌生人入镜的不同态度，主要来自人们对照片的期待不一样。一部分人只

无所谓。但另一部分精心构思艺术画面的人就会感到恼火，他们投入大量时间  
等待理想拍摄环境，希望拿到干净构图完整的成片。  
Q2. Why do tourists like taking photos while they are travelling?  
Answer：  
Tourists take photos for several main reasons. First of all, photographs  
keep tangible memories of their journeys. Years later, pictures can bring  
back travel experiences that might otherwise fade from memory.  
Besides, many people share travel photos with family and friends, or  
post them on social media to show their experiences. Some tourists also  
treat photography as part of travelling itself, enjoying the process of  
capturing wonderful local scenery.  
中文翻译：  
游客旅行时拍照有几个主要原因。第一，照片留存实实在在旅途回忆。多年之  
后，照片可以唤起快要淡忘的旅行经历。此外很多人把旅行照片分享给亲友，  
或者发布社交媒体，展示自己的见闻。还有一部分游客把摄影本身当作旅行的

## PDF第70页

一部分，享受捕捉当地美好风景的过程。  
Q3. Do you think it’s ok to take photos everywhere?  
Answer：  
I do not believe people have the right to take photographs absolutely  
everywhere. In some private spaces such as museums with  
no‑photography rules, religious sites or private residences, visitors  
should respect local regulations and other people’s privacy. Taking  
random photos of strangers without permission also counts as  
inappropriate behaviour. Public open‑air areas are generally fine for  
shooting, but rules and other people’s feelings should always come  
first.  
中文翻译：  
我并不认为人们可以在任何地方随意拍照。博物馆禁拍展区、宗教场所、私人  
住宅这类私密空间，游客应当遵守规定，尊重他人隐私。未经许可随意拍摄陌  
生人同样属于不妥行为。露天公共区域一般可以拍照，但要始终优先考虑规则  
和他人感受。

most about my grandfather is his gentle but firm personality. He went  
through a great many hardships in his younger days, yet he never  
becomes bitter or complains about fate. Instead, he maintains an  
optimistic attitude towards life and treats everyone around him

ordinary happy moments in life. I truly look up to him and value every  
chance we get to spend time with each other.

Part3  
Q1. What do old people usually do in their daily life?  
Answer：  
Modern senior citizens take part in diverse daily activities. Many keep fit  
by doing square dancing, tai chi or slow walking in public parks. Some  
devote time to hobbies such as calligraphy, gardening or watching  
traditional opera. Quite a number of grandparents also help look after  
their grandchildren. Nevertheless, some elderly people live alone, and  
their social circle may become relatively limited after retirement.

## PDF第76页

中文翻译：  
现代老年人的日常生活十分丰富。很多人跳广场舞、打太极、公园散步锻炼身  
体。一部分人投入书法、园艺、看戏曲这些爱好。不少祖父母还会帮忙照看孙  
辈。当然也有一部分老人独居，退休之后社交圈子会相对缩小。  
Q2. Why is there a generation gap between the young and the old?  
Answer：  
The generation gap mainly results from different life experiences. Older  
generations grew up under totally different social and material  
conditions. Their values, consumption habits and views towards  
technology are shaped by their unique times. Young people grow up in  
a digital‑era society. Without sufficient patient communication, both  
sides may find it hard to fully understand each other’s standpoints.  
That creates misunderstandings and the so‑called generation gap.  
中文翻译：  
代沟主要来自截然不同的人生经历。老一辈成长的社会物质条件完全不一样，

## PDF第77页

他们的价值观、消费习惯、科技观念被他们所处时代塑造。年轻人成长于数字  
时代。缺少充分耐心沟通，双方很难完全理解彼此立场，于是就产生误解，也  
就是所谓代沟。  
Q3. Has old people's life quality improved when compared to the past?  
Answer：  
Compared with several decades ago, the overall quality of elderly  
people’s life has seen obvious improvement. Medical care conditions  
are much better, social welfare systems offer more support for seniors,  
and modern household appliances reduce house‑work burdens. Even  
so, new challenges show up. Many elderly folks struggle with digital  
devices, and loneliness among empty‑nest elderly still remains a  
noticeable social issue.  
中文翻译：  
对比几十年前，老年人整体生活质量有明显提升。医疗条件变好，社会福利制  
度给老人更多支持，现代家电减轻家务负担。但同时也出现新挑战。很多老年  
人很难熟练使用数码设备，空巢老人的孤独问题依旧是突出社会难题。

## PDF第78页

Q4. What can young and old people learn from each other?  
Answer：  
Older people can pass down rich life wisdom and practical life  
experience to the young. They teach youngsters patience and how to  
handle setbacks. On the other side, young generations are familiar with  
digital technology and new social ideas. They can help the elderly get  
used to modern society. Mutual respect and patient communication  
make cross‑age learning possible for both groups.  
中文翻译：  
老年人可以把丰富人生智慧、现实生活经验传递给年轻人，教会晚辈耐心以及  
如何应对挫折。反过来，年轻人熟悉数字技术、新兴社会观念，可以帮助老年  
人适应现代社会。互相尊重加上耐心沟通，两代人才可以互相学习。

This experience happened last Spring Festival, when I paid a new‑year  
visit to one of my elderly relatives at his home. This middle‑aged relative  
is really crazy about a certain type of competitive online game. During  
our family gathering, he kept talking non‑stop about this game: special  
character skills, exciting match highlights and updates of the new game  
versions. Honestly, I have never played this game myself, and I had very  
little background knowledge about the whole topic. Most of the details  
he mentioned sounded confusing and failed to arouse my curiosity.  
However, I still kept listening carefully instead of cutting him off. For one  
thing, it would be quite rude to interrupt an elder family member who  
was sharing his passion. Besides, he rarely got the chance to talk about

## PDF第80页

his favourite hobby with young people. Although the content itself did  
not attract me, I respected his enthusiasm. While I sat there listening,  
my mind sometimes wandered a little bit. Even so, I nodded and gave  
simple responses from time to time to show politeness. After the  
conversation finished, I felt a little mentally tired, yet I did not regret  
staying patient. I understood that listening itself can be a meaningful  
way to show respect to other people.  
中文翻译：  
这件事发生在去年春节，我去一位亲戚家里拜年。这位中年亲戚十分痴迷某一

片段、新版本更新内容。说实话，我自己从来没有玩过这款游戏，对这个话题  
几乎没有背景了解。他讲的大部分细节我听着一头雾水，完全无法勾起我的好  
奇心。但我依旧认真听着，没有打断他。一方面，打断长辈兴致勃勃的分享非  
常不礼貌。另外，他很少有机会和年轻人聊自己热爱的爱好。就算话题本身吸  
引不了我，我尊重他这份热情。听的过程当中我的思绪偶尔会有点走神，但我  
还是会点头，时不时简单回应以示礼貌。谈话结束之后，我精神上略感疲惫，  
但并不后悔保持耐心。我明白了，倾听本身，也是向他人表达尊重的一种重要  
方式。  
Part3

## PDF第81页

Q1. How can we tell if a listener is listening carefully?  
Answer：  
We can judge whether a person listens attentively from both body  
language and verbal feedback. Careful listeners maintain suitable eye  
contact, nod appropriately and do not constantly check their mobile  
phones. They can also give relevant follow‑up questions or brief  
comments based on what they just heard. If a person gives only empty  
“uh‑huh” replies without responding to actual content, he or she is  
probably not truly paying attention.  
中文翻译：  
我们可以通过肢体语言和语言反馈，判断一个人是否在认真倾听。认真倾听的  
人会保持适度眼神交流，适时点头，不会不停刷手机。还会根据刚刚听到的内  
容提出相关追问或者简短评论。如果一个人只会空洞地嗯嗯啊啊，不对内容做  
出回应，那他大概率没有真正投入聆听。  
Q2. What would happen if the listener didn’t listen carefully?

## PDF第82页

Answer：  
Poor listening creates a series of negative results in communication.  
Important information may get missed, which leads to  
misunderstandings and mistakes in later actions. Moreover, the speaker  
will feel ignored and disrespected. This damages mutual trust and  
makes future conversation less effective. In serious situations, bad  
listening can cause conflicts between family members, friends or work  
partners.  
中文翻译：  
不好好倾听会给沟通带来一连串负面后果。重要信息被遗漏，造成误解，后续  
行动出现差错。同时说话者会感觉被忽视、不被尊重。这会破坏彼此信任，让  
之后沟通效率变低。情况严重的时候，倾听不到位甚至会引发家人、朋友、同  
事之间的矛盾。  
Q3. What kind of topics do young people like to talk about today?  
Answer：

## PDF第83页

Young people nowadays tend to talk about a wide range of subjects.  
Popular themes include entertainment content such as shows and short  
videos, career‑related plans, study pressure and personal hobbies. They  
also discuss social hot issues, travel plans and interesting daily  
anecdotes. Of course, individual interests vary a lot. Some youngsters  
prefer deeper topics such as philosophy, while others stick to light  
casual daily chat.  
中文翻译：  
如今年轻人会聊各种各样的话题。热门主题包括综艺短视频这类娱乐内容、职  
业规划、学业压力和个人爱好。他们也会讨论社会热点、旅行计划、生活趣  
事。当然每个人兴趣差别很大，一部分年轻人喜欢哲学这类深度话题，另一些  
人只进行轻松日常闲聊。

physical sports‑goods shop in the city centre shopping mall. I visited  
this store on a Sunday afternoon three months ago, because I planned  
to buy a pair of running shoes for regular exercise. At first I just casually

about my real needs: how often I would go running, what type of  
ground I usually exercised on and my budget range. Based on my  
answers, he recommended two suitable shoe models and explained the

pairs on, walk around to feel the fit, and answered every one of my  
questions in detail. I finally chose one pair that fitted me perfectly. I was

really satisfied with the whole shopping experience. The staff did not  
focus on making quick sales. He prioritised matching goods to my

positive impression, and I would happily recommend this shop to my

我想聊的一次良好购物服务，发生在市中心商场的一家运动用品实体店。三个

路面类型，还有我的预算。根据我的回答，他推荐两款合适的鞋，客观说明每  
一款的优缺点。他允许多次试穿，让我走动感受脚感，细致解答我每一个疑  
问。最后我选到一双十分合脚的鞋子。整体购物体验让我非常满意。店员没有  
一心追求快速成交，而是优先匹配我的实际需求。这种贴心实在的服务给我留  
下很好印象，我很愿意把这家店推荐给我的朋友们。  
Part3  
Q1. Can consumption drive economic growth?

## PDF第86页

Answer：  
Consumption acts as one major engine for domestic economic growth.  
When citizens spend money on goods and services, businesses gain  
income, expand production and create more job opportunities.  
However, blind over‑consumption is not desirable. A healthy economy  
requires balanced consumption, investment and production, rather than  
simply encouraging people to spend endlessly.  
中文翻译：  
消费是拉动国内经济增长的一大引擎。民众购买商品服务，企业获得营收，扩  
大生产，创造更多就业岗位。但盲目过度消费并不可取。健康经济需要消费、  
投资、生产三者平衡，而不是一味鼓动人们无休止花钱。  
Q2. What are the advantages and disadvantages of shopping online?  
Answer：  
Online shopping brings obvious strengths: wide product selection,  
time‑saving convenience and often lower prices. Nevertheless, it also

## PDF第87页

contains drawbacks. Shoppers cannot touch or try on goods before  
purchasing, so they risk receiving items that fail to match online  
descriptions. In addition, extra delivery waiting time and troublesome  
return procedures can create unpleasant experiences for consumers.  
中文翻译：  
网购优点十分突出：商品选择多、省时便利，价格往往更低。但它同样存在弊  
端。消费者购买前无法触摸试穿，收到实物有可能和网上描述不符。除此之  
外，漫长快递等待，繁琐退货流程，也会给消费者带来糟糕体验。  
Q3. What are the differences between online shopping and in‑store  
shopping?  
Answer：  
The biggest difference lies in customers’ sensory experience. Physical  
shops permit people to touch, try and examine products face‑to‑face,  
and instant possession of goods after paying. Online shopping offers  
greater convenience and richer choices, yet buyers must wait for  
delivery. Also, in‑store shopping includes real‑time face‑to‑face staff  
service, while online support mostly takes place through text messages

## PDF第88页

on screens.  
中文翻译：  
最大区别在于顾客的感官体验。实体店允许顾客亲手触摸、试用品，付款之后  
立刻拿到商品。网购更加便利，选择更丰富，但是买家必须等待快递。同时实  
体店可以获得店员面对面实时服务，而线上客服大多依靠屏幕文字沟通。  
Q4. Why do some people not like shopping in small shops?  
Answer：  
Some consumers avoid small local shops for several practical reasons.  
Small stores usually carry limited product ranges compared with big  
malls. Their prices may not be competitive either. Besides, some  
small‑shop owners offer inconsistent service quality. If customers meet  
bad attitudes, they will prefer large‑scale chain stores with more  
standardised rules.  
中文翻译：  
一部分消费者不喜欢小店，有几方面现实原因。对比大型商场，小店货品选择

## PDF第89页

有限，价格也未必有优势。另外部分小店服务水平参差不齐。如果顾客遇到不  
好的态度，就会更加偏爱制度更标准化的大型连锁门店。  
Q5. What are the advantages and disadvantages of shopping in small  
shops?  
Answer：  
Small independent shops have unique merits. They often sell special,  
distinctive goods you cannot find in big‑chain stores. Shop‑owners can  
provide warm, personalised customer service. On the negative side,  
stock variety is limited, prices might be higher and opening hours are  
sometimes unstable. So shoppers weigh these factors before choosing  
where to spend money.  
中文翻译：  
独立小店拥有独有的优势，经常售卖大型连锁店找不到的特色商品。店主可以  
提供温暖个性化服务。弊端就是货品选择有限，价格可能更高，营业时间有时  
不稳定。所以消费者会权衡这些因素再选择消费地点。

## PDF第90页

Q6. Why are shopping malls so popular in China?  
Answer：  
Shopping malls remain popular in China because they combine multiple  
functions in one single building. People can shop, eat various food,  
watch films and join entertainment activities all under one roof. They  
offer air‑conditioned comfortable public space suitable for family and  
friend outings. Malls satisfy comprehensive leisure demands beyond  
pure purchasing behaviour.  
中文翻译：  
商场在中国人气很高，因为一栋建筑之内集合多重功能。人们可以购物、吃各  
类美食、看电影、参加娱乐活动，全部集中在一处。商场提供舒适恒温公共空  
间，适合家人朋友外出聚会，满足除单纯买东西之外的综合休闲需求。

Part3  
Q1. Does art, like music or theater, have an impact on society?  
Answer：  
Art forms such as music and theatre exert far‑reaching influence over

## PDF第93页

society. Great artistic works can reflect real‑life social phenomena,  
spread shared values and arouse public emotional resonance. They can  
cheer people up in hard times and make audiences think deeply about  
social issues. Even so, not all art carries positive meaning. Some  
low‑quality artistic productions may spread harmful ideas. Therefore,  
society should encourage positive and inspiring art creations.  
中文翻译：  
音乐、戏剧这类艺术形式会对社会产生深远影响。优秀的艺术作品可以反映现  
实社会现象，传播共同价值观，唤起大众情感共鸣。艰难时期它们可以鼓舞人  
心，也促使观众对社会问题深度思考。但并非所有艺术都具备正向意义，部分  
劣质作品会传播有害思想。因此社会应当鼓励积极向上、启人心智的艺术创  
作。  
Q2. Is it good to have creative people in a team?  
Answer：  
Creative members bring huge advantages to any team. They can come  
up with fresh perspectives and innovative solutions when the group  
faces tough problems. Without creative thinkers, teams tend to stick to

## PDF第94页

old routines and struggle to make breakthroughs. Of course, creativity  
alone is not enough. Creative ideas still need practical‑minded  
teammates to turn inspiration into real achievable results. A balanced  
mix of creative and pragmatic people creates the most effective team.  
中文翻译：  
拥有富有创造力的成员，会给任何团队带来巨大优势。团队遇到棘手难题时，  
他们可以提出全新视角与创新解决方案。缺少创新型人才，团队容易墨守成  
规，很难取得突破。当然光有创造力远远不够，创意想法依旧需要务实的队  
友，把灵感转化成实实在在的成果。兼具创新头脑与务实执行者，才是最高效  
的团队配置。  
Q3. What impact can artistic buildings have on society?  
Answer：  
Well‑designed artistic buildings bring multiple benefits to local society.  
Unique architectural designs can beautify the urban landscape and  
become city cultural landmarks, attracting tourists from other places.  
They also lift citizens’ aesthetic taste and enrich public cultural life.  
However, overly fancy architectural projects that ignore practical

## PDF第95页

functions and cost control are not desirable. Art value should coexist  
with practical needs for public buildings.  
中文翻译：  
设计精良的艺术性建筑会给当地社会带来多重好处。独特建筑设计美化城市风  
貌，成为城市文化地标，吸引外地游客。同时提升市民审美，丰富公共文化生  
活。但过分追求造型花哨，却忽视实用功能与成本控制的建筑项目并不可取。  
公共建筑的艺术价值应当和实用需求并存。  
Q4. Do children these days still like doing arts and crafts?  
Answer：  
Many modern children still enjoy arts and crafts activities, although  
their ways of creating have changed. Apart from traditional  
paper‑cutting and hand‑making, lots of kids also do digital drawing on  
tablets. Nevertheless, heavy school homework occupies much of their  
spare time. Compared with previous generations, they have less free  
time for slow manual creation. So interest exists, yet practical time is  
quite limited.

## PDF第96页

中文翻译：  
很多当代孩子依旧喜欢美术手工活动，只是创作方式发生了变化。除传统剪纸  
手作，不少孩子也在平板上进行数字绘画。但繁重学校作业占据他们大量课余  
时间。和老一辈相比，留给慢悠悠手工创作的空闲时间变少。孩子本身有兴  
趣，但实际可支配时间十分有限。  
Q5. Why do some children enjoy making things by hand?  
Answer：  
Children take great pleasure in hand‑making for several reasons. First of  
all, they gain a strong sense of achievement when they turn simple raw  
materials into brand‑new finished works. The process of mixing, cutting  
and pasting also satisfies kids’ natural curiosity and desire for  
exploration. Besides, handcraft gives them space to show their own  
imagination, without strict right‑or‑wrong standards like regular school  
subjects.  
中文翻译：  
孩子喜欢做手工有多方面原因。首先，把简单原材料变成全新成品的时候，他

## PDF第97页

欲。此外手工创作给他们施展想象力的空间，不像学校学科那样有着严格的标  
准答案。  
Q6. Do you think art classes are important for children?  
Answer：  
Art classes play an irreplaceable role in children’s growth. They are not  
just for training professional artists. Art lessons cultivate kids’  
imagination, aesthetic judgement and hands‑on ability. They offer an  
outlet for children to express inner feelings which they cannot easily put  
into words. Even if a child will not work in art‑related industries later,  
these abilities still benefit their whole life.  
中文翻译：  
美术课在孩子成长过程中拥有不可替代的作用。开设美术课并不是只为培养职

言语表达内心情绪的渠道。就算孩子未来不从事艺术行业，这些能力依旧会让  
他们终身受益。

The enjoyable evening I want to describe happened last autumn, on a

get‑together. None of us had heavy study deadlines that weekend, so

the floor, played light‑hearted board games and shared interesting

stories from our campus life. We chatted freely about our worries, small

not go to noisy bars or crowded entertainment venues. All the

What made this evening so special was the relaxed atmosphere. There  
was no pressure to perform or keep up appearances. We could just be  
our true selves around each other. Those warm moments created  
precious memories, and I still look back on that night with great  
fondness.  
中文翻译：

后，我们席地而坐，玩轻松的桌游，互相分享校园生活里的趣事。我们无拘无

喧闹酒吧或者人挤人的娱乐场所，全部的快乐都来自真诚交谈与舒服的陪伴。  
这个夜晚格外珍贵，在于氛围十分松弛。不需要刻意伪装，不用顾及体面，我  
们可以在彼此面前做最真实的自己。那些温暖片段构成珍贵回忆，直到现在回  
想起来，我依旧十分怀念那个夜晚。

## PDF第100页

Part3  
Q1. What are the differences between young people’s leisure time  
activities and those of old people?  
Answer：  
Young people and the elderly tend to choose quite different leisure  
activities. Young generations prefer social gatherings, online  
entertainment and outdoor adventurous activities. They pursue  
excitement and novelty in their spare hours. In contrast, older people  
favour slower‑pasted pastimes such as gardening, walking and watching  
traditional television programs. Their activities focus more on relaxation  
rather than new stimulation. Of course, individual personal hobbies can  
break these general patterns.  
中文翻译：  
年轻人和老年人选择的休闲活动往往差别很大。年轻人更喜欢聚会、线上娱  
乐、户外有挑战性的活动，闲暇时光追求新鲜感与刺激。与之相比，老年人偏  
爱节奏舒缓的消遣，例如养花、散步、看传统电视节目，活动重心更多放在放  
松身心，而非寻求新鲜刺激。当然，个人爱好也会打破这种普遍规律。

## PDF第101页

Q2. What can we do to bring family members closer together?  
Answer：  
Several practical ways can strengthen bonds among family members.  
Setting aside regular family time for shared meals or short outings is  
really helpful. During this time, people should reduce phone‑screen  
distractions and have face‑to‑face conversations. In addition, family  
members can take part in simple group tasks together, such as cooking  
or housework. Mutual respect and patient listening, more than  
expensive gifts, build genuine family closeness.  
中文翻译：  
有不少切实可行的办法拉近家庭成员之间的距离。留出固定家庭时间，一起吃  
饭或者短途出游很有帮助。相处的时候少看手机，进行面对面交流。除此之外  
家人可以共同完成简单集体任务，比如做饭做家务。比起贵重礼物，互相尊重  
和耐心倾听，才可以建立真正紧密的亲情。  
Q3. How do people get along in a big family?

## PDF第102页

Answer：  
Getting along well in a large extended family requires tolerance and  
mutual understanding. Different generations hold varied viewpoints on  
many issues, so people need to avoid forcing their own opinions onto  
others. It is unrealistic for every family member to agree all the time.  
Keeping appropriate personal boundaries and showing respect can  
reduce conflicts. Small acts of care also help maintain harmonious  
relationships within big families.  
中文翻译：  
在大家庭中和睦相处，需要包容和互相体谅。不同代际看待很多问题观念不一  
样，不要把自己想法强加给别人。要求所有人时时刻刻意见完全一致并不现  
实。保持恰当边界，互相尊重，就可以减少矛盾。一些小小的关心举动，也有  
助于维系大家庭内部的和谐。

The regular activity that I consider a waste of my time is mindlessly

only spend five minutes relaxing, I easily lose track of time. The

watching one video after another. I know it does not bring me valuable  
knowledge or real rest. Many videos are superficial, repetitive and  
merely designed to grab my attention. Once I put my phone down, I  
often feel empty and regret that I have squandered time which I could  
have used for reading or physical exercise. Despite knowing its

## PDF第104页

drawbacks, I still fall into this habit. It offers instant easy amusement  
when my brain feels tired. That is why I keep doing it. From my  
perspective, it counts as a waste of time because it produces no  
long‑term benefit for my study or personal growth.

上真正的休息。很多视频内容浅薄重复，目的仅仅是抓住我的眼球。放下手机  
之后，我常常内心空虚，后悔自己挥霍了本可以用来读书或者运动的时间。明  
明知道坏处，我还是改不掉这个习惯。大脑疲惫的时候，短视频可以立刻给到  
轻松的娱乐，这就是我依旧会刷的原因。在我看来，它之所以浪费时间，是因  
为它对我的学习和个人成长，带不来任何长期收益。  
Part3  
Q1. Why do some people refuse to abide by rules?

## PDF第105页

Answer：  
Multiple reasons lead people to break rules. Some individuals believe  
rules restrict their personal freedom, and they over‑value their own  
short‑term interests. Others think minor violations will never get  
discovered or punished. Moreover, if rules feel unfair or unreasonable in  
people’s eyes, they will also choose not to follow them. Nevertheless,  
breaking rules usually brings hidden risks for both individuals and  
society.  
中文翻译：  
多种原因会导致人们不遵守规则。一部分人认为规则限制个人自由，过度看重  
自身短期利益。还有人觉得小小的违规不会被发现，也不会受到惩罚。此外，  
如果人们判定规则本身不公平、不合理，同样会拒绝遵守。但违反规则，往往  
会给个人和社会都带来潜藏风险。  
Q2. What kinds of things make people feel pressured?  
Answer：  
Modern people face pressure from many sources. Academic or work  
tasks, tight deadlines and fierce competition create huge mental stress.

## PDF第106页

Relationship troubles with family, friends or partners are another  
common source. Financial worries about housing and daily living costs  
also weigh heavily on ordinary people. Different age groups will meet  
different main pressure points in their life journey.  
中文翻译：  
现代人的压力来源多种多样。学业工作任务、紧迫截止日期、激烈竞争，会带  
来巨大精神负担。和家人朋友伴侣之间的人际关系矛盾，也是很常见的压力来  
源。住房、日常生活开销带来的经济焦虑，同样压在普通人身上。不同年龄阶  
段的人，人生当中面临的主要压力点各不相同。  
Q3. Will you continue doing something when you are aware that it’s a  
waste of time?  
Answer：  
Sometimes people keep doing activities even knowing they waste time.  
These behaviours often provide instant comfort or temporary stress  
relief. It is hard to break bad habits purely relying on self‑awareness.  
Real change needs practical measures, such as setting time limits or  
finding better substitutes. Simply realising a habit is unproductive is

## PDF第107页

often not powerful enough for people to stop it immediately.  
中文翻译：  
有些时候，就算明明知道一件事浪费时间，人们还是会继续做。这类行为往往  
可以带来即时的慰藉，短暂释放压力。仅仅依靠思想觉悟，很难改掉坏习惯。  
真正的改变需要实际手段，例如设置时间限制，寻找更好的替代消遣。单纯意  
识到习惯没有价值，往往不足以让人立刻停止。  
Q4. How do you balance life and work?  
Answer：  
Balancing life and work requires reasonable planning and self‑discipline.  
People ought to set clear boundaries between work tasks and personal  
private time. When work finishes, avoid constantly checking work  
messages during rest hours. It is also vital to reserve fixed time for  
exercise, hobbies and communication with family. Perfect balance may  
not exist, but we can strive for a healthy state without total burnout.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第108页

平衡生活和工作，需要合理规划加上自律。人们应当给工作任务和私人时间划  
清明确界限。工作结束之后，休息时段不要不停查看工作消息。留出固定时间  
运动、发展爱好、陪伴家人同样至关重要。或许不存在绝对完美的平衡，但我  
们可以努力追求一种不至于身心耗竭的健康状态。

Part3  
Q1. Do you think it is a good trend to have more big cities?  
Answer：  
The expansion of big cities brings both advantages and unavoidable  
drawbacks. Big cities create abundant job opportunities and  
high‑quality educational and medical resources. But over‑expansion  
may also cause serious troubles such as over‑crowding, traffic jams and  
housing shortages. Simply pursuing larger city scale is not wise. Urban  
development should match local population and resource conditions.  
中文翻译：  
大城市不断扩张，同时带来优势与无法回避的弊端。大城市创造大量就业岗  
位，拥有优质教育医疗资源。但城市过度扩张，也会造成过度拥挤、交通堵

## PDF第111页

塞、住房紧缺等严峻问题。单纯追求城市规模扩大并不明智，城市发展应当匹  
配当地人口与资源条件。  
Q2. Do you think there are some people who enjoy crowded places?  
Answer：  
Quite a number of people actually enjoy busy, crowded locations. For  
outgoing social individuals, large crowds create a lively, energetic  
atmosphere. For example, music festivals, night markets and holiday  
scenic spots attract them precisely because of the bustling vibe. That  
said, introverted people will feel stressed and exhausted in identical  
surroundings. Preference for crowded environments heavily depends on  
one’s personality.  
中文翻译：  
确实有不少人喜欢热闹拥挤的地方。性格外向爱社交的人，人群可以带来生机  
勃勃的氛围。音乐节、夜市、节假日景点，恰恰因为喧嚣热闹才吸引他们。但  
同样的环境，内向的人待着就会倍感压力疲惫。是否喜欢人多的环境，很大程  
度由个人性格决定。

## PDF第112页

Q3. Why do people go to crowded places?  
Answer：  
People visit crowded places for various purposes. Some go for tourism  
or special large‑scale events such as concerts and festivals. Others  
simply need to complete daily errands in busy commercial districts.  
Many popular locations gather good resources, goods or  
entertainment. Even knowing there will be big crowds, people still  
choose to go, because the experience itself is worthwhile to them.  
中文翻译：  
人们去往人多的地方有着各式各样的目的。一部分人为旅游，或是参加音乐  
会、节日庆典这类大型活动。还有人只是需要去繁华商业区处理日常事务。很  
多热门地点汇聚优质资源、商品或者娱乐项目。就算明明知道会人山人海，人  
们依旧愿意前往，因为对他们来说，那份体验本身很值得。  
Q4. Will people use bikes more or less in cities in the future?  
Answer：

## PDF第113页

I believe bicycles will keep an important position in future city transport  
systems. Shared bikes offer cheap and convenient short‑distance travel  
solutions, and they help reduce traffic pressure and air pollution.  
However, bicycles cannot satisfy long‑distance commuting demands. So  
bike‑riding volume will not keep rising endlessly. It will remain a vital  
supplementary choice alongside buses and subways.  
中文翻译：  
我认为自行车在未来城市交通体系当中依旧占据重要地位。共享单车提供廉价  
便捷短途出行方案，同时缓解交通压力、减少空气污染。但自行车无法满足长  
距离通勤需求。所以骑行量不会无限制上涨。它会作为公交、地铁之外一个重  
要补充出行方式。  
Q5. Will there be more or less green space in cities in the future?  
Answer：  
In general, cities will attach greater importance to green space  
construction in the future. Citizens’ awareness of environmental  
wellbeing keeps rising, and urban planners understand parks and  
wetlands improve living quality. Even so, rapid urban construction

## PDF第114页

creates competition for land resources. We cannot guarantee unlimited  
growth. Green areas will expand, but at a moderate pace.  
中文翻译：  
总体来看，未来城市会更加重视绿地建设。市民对于环境宜居的意识持续提  
升，城市规划者明白公园湿地可以提升生活品质。但快速城市建设会带来土地  
资源争夺，绿地不可能无限制扩张。绿化面积会有所增加，但速度会比较温  
和。  
Q6. Which cities are overcrowded in your country?  
Answer：  
Several first‑tier metropolises in China face obvious over‑crowding  
pressure. Massive numbers of people flow into these cities seeking  
better jobs and education resources. As a result, public transport,  
housing and public facilities are under huge strain. To ease the burden,  
relevant authorities have carried out different urban planning policies to  
distribute population to surrounding satellite cities.

## PDF第115页

中文翻译：  
国内有几座超一线城市面临十分明显的人口拥挤压力。大量人口涌入，追求更  
好的工作与教育资源。这使得公共交通、住房、公共设施承受巨大负荷。为缓  
解压力，相关部门推行各类城市规划政策，引导人口向周边卫星城分流。

classmates. He is extremely fascinated by both Chinese and world

history books, watches well‑documented historical documentaries and

discussion groups to exchange viewpoints with other history  
enthusiasts. For him, history is not just a collection of boring dates and  
events for exams. He believes studying history helps people understand  
where modern society comes from. By learning about successes and  
mistakes of people in the past, we can draw valuable lessons for  
present‑day life. He often shares interesting historical stories with our  
friends. I really respect his passion. Unlike many students who only  
memorise facts for tests, he truly appreciates the charm of the past.  
Talking with him broadens my horizons and encourages me to pay more  
attention to historical knowledge myself.

## PDF第117页

试的枯燥年代和事件集合。他认为学习历史，可以帮助我们理解现代社会从何  
而来。研究古人的成功与失误，我们可以为当下生活吸取宝贵经验。他经常给  
朋友们分享有意思的历史故事。我很敬佩他这份热爱。很多学生只是为考试死

Part3  
Q1. Besides big historical events, what else is important when learning  
history?  
Answer：  
Learning history is more than just memorising major wars and political  
incidents. Ordinary people’s daily lives, local folk customs, economic  
conditions and cultural development are equally significant. These  
details show how real residents lived in certain eras. Without  
understanding social life at the grassroots level, people will only get a  
partial, one‑sided picture of the past. Comprehensive history study  
needs multiple layers of information.

## PDF第118页

中文翻译：  
学习历史不只是背诵重大战争和政治事件。普通人的日常生活、民俗风俗、经  
济状况、文化发展，同样十分重要。这些细节展现特定时代普通人真实的生存  
状态。不去了解底层社会生活，人们得到的过去就只是片面的图景。全面的历  
史学习，需要多层次信息作为支撑。  
Q2. Do you think people should learn ancient history before learning  
modern history?  
Answer：  
Studying ancient history first has its merits, yet it is not an absolute rule.  
Ancient history provides the background and roots for later‑day  
developments. However, modern history has closer connections with  
current social reality, so it is easier for learners to relate to. In  
educational practice, many courses combine both. The sequence can be  
adjusted according to learners’ age and cognitive level.  
中文翻译：  
先学古代史有它的好处，但并不是必须遵守的铁律。古代史为后世发展提供时  
代背景与根源。而现代历史和当下社会现实联系更紧密，学习者更容易产生代

## PDF第119页

入感。实际教学当中很多课程会把二者结合。学习顺序，可以根据学习者年龄  
和认知水平灵活调整。  
Q3. Is it important to learn how people in the past lived their lives?  
Answer：  
Absolutely, knowing ordinary people’s past life carries great value.  
History is not only made by kings, generals and famous politicians. The  
thoughts, hardships and daily choices of common folks also form a  
crucial part of human civilisation. Understanding their existence helps  
us see the full picture of historical periods, and makes history much  
more vivid and approachable for learners.  
中文翻译：  
毫无疑问，了解古人普通民众的生活意义重大。历史不单单由帝王将相、知名  
政治家创造。普通百姓的思想、苦难与日常抉择，同样是人类文明重要组成部  
分。理解他们的生存状态，我们才能看见一个时代完整全貌，历史对学习者来  
说也会变得更加鲜活易懂。

## PDF第120页

Q4. How do children learn history before they attend school?  
Answer：  
Before formal schooling, children pick up historical knowledge indirectly  
from various sources. They hear old stories and legends told by parents  
or grandparents. Some watch cartoons or picture books based on  
historical figures. This early history knowledge is mostly story‑based  
rather than systematic. It builds initial interest, even if some contents  
are not completely historically accurate.  
中文翻译：  
正式上学之前，孩子从各类渠道间接接触历史知识。听父母爷爷奶奶讲古老传  
说故事，也会看基于历史人物改编的动画或者绘本。这阶段历史知识大多以故  
事形式呈现，不成体系。即便部分内容不完全忠于史实，但可以建立最初的兴  
趣。  
Q5. At what age do you think people should begin to learn history?  
Answer：

## PDF第121页

Children can start getting exposed to history from a fairly young age  
through simple stories. Systematic textbook‑based history study can  
begin in primary school. Very young kids cannot handle complex  
historical analysis, so story‑oriented materials fit them better. As their  
cognitive ability grows, teachers can gradually introduce more complex  
historical concepts and multi‑angle analysis.  
中文翻译：  
孩子很小就可以通过简单故事接触历史。系统课本式历史学习，可以从小学阶  
段开启。年纪太小的孩子无法处理复杂历史分析，故事类素材更加适合他们。  
随着认知能力提升，老师可以逐步引入更复杂历史概念与多角度分析。  
Q6. Do you think children should begin learning history from an early  
age?  
Answer：  
Early‑age exposure to history brings many benefits for children. It  
cultivates their sense of time and helps them understand the origin of  
local culture and traditions. Even so, teaching should match kids’  
cognitive level. Over‑complicated academic historical materials are

## PDF第122页

unsuitable for young children. Interesting tales and picture resources  
serve as the best starting point.  
中文翻译：  
从小接触历史，会给孩子带来诸多益处。培养时间观念，帮助他们理解本土文  
化传统的由来。但教学内容要匹配孩子认知水平。过于复杂学术史料不适合低  
龄儿童，有趣故事、图画资料才是最好的入门载体。

support, I gradually mastered the essential operations. Finally I finished  
my course video assignment successfully. I am really thankful to him.

impressed me deeply.

帮助下，我慢慢掌握基础操作，最后顺利完成课程视频作业。我非常感激他。  
如果没有他的帮助，我要花大量时间自己苦苦摸索。他乐于分享知识的品质，  
给我留下很深印象。  
Part3  
Q1. Why is self‑discipline important when learning a new skill?  
Answer：  
Self‑discipline plays a critical role in mastering new skills. Most skills  
cannot be acquired overnight. They demand regular repeated practice  
over a long period of time. Without self‑discipline, learners will easily  
put off practice and give up when facing difficulties. Natural talent can  
give people a head start, yet consistent self‑controlled practice

## PDF第125页

ultimately decides how far people can progress.  
中文翻译：  
自律对于学习新技能至关重要。绝大多数技能无法一夜学会，需要长期规律反  
复练习。缺少自律，学习者遇到困难很容易拖延练习、半途而废。天赋可以让  
人起步更快，但能否持续自律练习，最终决定人们可以达到怎样的水平。  
Q2. What are the differences between learning from a teacher and  
learning by oneself?  
Answer：  
Learning with a teacher offers instant feedback and professional  
guidance. Teachers can spot hidden mistakes and set reasonable  
learning plans for you. However, self‑study gives full freedom to control  
your own learning pace and content. The downside of self‑learning is  
that you may stay unaware of your own errors. Many learners achieve  
best results by combining both two approaches together.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第126页

跟着老师学，可以获得即时反馈和专业指导。老师能够发现你自己看不到的错  
误，制定合理学习计划。自学的优势则是可以完全自由掌控学习节奏和学习内  
容。自学的弊端在于，你可能察觉不到自身错误。很多学习者把两种方式结  
合，才可以拿到最好的学习效果。  
Q3. How do adults learn a new skill?  
Answer：  
Adults learn new skills with clear practical goals in most cases. They  
tend to focus on real‑world application instead of pure theoretical  
knowledge. They make good use of past life experience to understand  
new concepts. At the same time, adults may carry fixed old habits which  
can create barriers. Compared with children, they have less spare time  
available for long‑term practice.  
中文翻译：  
成年人学习新技能，大多数情况下带有明确现实目标。他们更看重实际运用，  
而不是纯粹理论。他们可以借助过往人生经验理解新概念。但成年人也会被固  
化旧习惯阻碍学习。和孩子对比，成年人能够用来长期练习的空余时间更少。

## PDF第127页

Q4. How can parents and teachers teach children new skills?  
Answer：  
Adults should guide children through patient demonstration and  
encouraging feedback. They ought to break complex skills into small  
achievable steps. Praising children’s progress helps build confidence. It  
is not wise to push kids too hard. When mistakes happen, offer  
constructive advice rather than harsh criticism. That creates a positive  
environment for skill‑building.  
中文翻译：  
家长和老师应当耐心示范，多给予鼓励反馈来引导孩子。把复杂技能拆解成一  
个个容易完成的小步骤。肯定孩子取得的进步，可以建立自信心。逼迫太紧是  
不可取的。出现错误的时候，给出建设性意见，而不是严厉指责。这样才能营  
造学习技能的积极环境。  
Q5. Where can children learn new skills?  
Answer：

## PDF第128页

Children gain new skills from multiple sources. Formal classroom  
education at school is one main channel. Besides, they can learn from  
family members, playmates, after‑school courses and online educational  
videos. Real‑life activities such as games and group projects also serve  
as valuable learning chances for young kids.  
中文翻译：  
孩子可以从很多渠道学到新技能。学校课堂教育是主要途径。除此之外，向家  
人、玩伴、课外培训班、线上教育视频学习。游戏、小组实践活动这类真实生  
活场景，同样是小朋友宝贵的学习机会。  
Q6. Why should children learn new skills?  
Answer：  
Learning new skills brings multiple benefits for children. It develops  
their practical abilities and expands their potential. Mastering new  
things also builds self‑confidence and sense of achievement. Even if  
some skills will not be used in future careers, the learning process itself  
trains their patience and problem‑solving mindsets.

## PDF第129页

中文翻译：  
学习新技能会给孩子带来多重益处。锻炼实操能力，拓展自身发展可能性。掌  
握新本领同时建立自信与成就感。就算部分技能未来工作用不上，学习过程本  
身就锻炼了耐心与解决问题的思维模式。
