Part3  
Q1. Can movies help people better understand the cultural background  
of a country?  
Answer：  
Good movies can serve as windows showing a nation’s cultural  
background. They display local customs, social values and people’s  
real‑life conditions through vivid plots and scenes. However, audiences  
need to be cautious. Some commercial films are over‑dramatised for  
box‑office purposes, and do not reflect authentic local culture. So  
viewers should judge the content critically instead of believing  
everything they see on screen.  
中文翻译：  
优秀电影可以充当了解一个国家文化背景的窗口。通过生动情节画面，展现当

## PDF第132页

地风俗、社会价值观与民众真实生活状态。但观众需要保持审慎。部分商业电  
影为票房过度戏剧化，并不能反映真实本土文化。所以观众应当带着批判性眼  
光分辨内容，不能银幕上看到什么就全部信以为真。  
Q2. Are movies more likely to help people become more creative than  
books?  
Answer：  
It is hard to simply claim movies are more helpful for creativity than  
books. Books leave huge space for readers’ own imagination, since  
readers need to construct scenes and characters inside their heads.  
Movies offer finished visual images, which reduce the need for viewers  
to imagine. Both media can inspire creativity, but they work in  
completely different ways for the human mind.  
中文翻译：  
很难简单判定电影比书籍更能激发创造力。书籍留给读者巨大想象空间，读者  
需要在脑海中自行构建场景人物。电影直接给到成型视觉画面，会减少观众自  
主想象空间。两种媒介都可以启发创造力，但作用于人脑的方式完全不一样。

## PDF第133页

Q3. What are the differences between watching movies at home and in  
a cinema?  
Answer：  
The cinema offers immersive large‑screen visual effects and  
surround‑sound audio, creating a powerful collective viewing  
atmosphere. But ticket costs are high and viewers must follow public  
rules. Watching films at home is far more flexible. You can pause the  
movie, eat whatever you like and arrange your own schedule. Yet home  
equipment cannot fully recreate the strong sensory experience of a  
movie theatre.  
中文翻译：  
电影院提供沉浸式大屏画面与环绕音响，拥有强烈集体观影氛围。但电影票开  
销高，观众还要遵守公共秩序。在家看电影自由度高很多，可以随时暂停，随  
意吃东西，时间完全自己掌控。但家庭设备无法复刻影院那种强烈感官体验。  
Q4. What kinds of movies are the most popular in China?

## PDF第134页

Answer：  
Multiple movie genres enjoy great popularity in China. Inspirational  
domestic realistic stories, comedies and adventure films attract large  
audience groups. Blockbuster science‑fiction productions also draw  
plenty of viewers. People’s tastes are diverse; no single style dominates  
the whole market. Holiday release periods usually see the highest  
box‑office performance.  
中文翻译：  
国内有多种电影类型广受欢迎。国产现实励志题材、喜剧、冒险片受众庞大。  
科幻大片同样吸引大量观众。大众审美口味多元，没有哪一种类型独占全部市  
场。节假日档期的电影，往往票房表现最好。  
✨Part2 新题 21 不喜欢的短途旅行  
Describe a short trip you often take but do not like  
You should say:  
‑ Where you go  
‑ When you go there

## PDF第135页

‑ Why you go there  
And explain why you do not like the trip  
Answer：  
The short trip I take regularly but dislike is the journey from my home to  
the downtown hospital, which I visit with my mother every month. We  
have to make this trip on fixed weekends to accompany my elderly  
grandmother for regular medical check‑ups. It is not a long distance,  
roughly forty minutes by public transport, yet the whole process always  
drains my energy. We need to set off quite early in the morning to avoid  
huge hospital crowds. The bus route is often jammed during weekend  
rush hours, so we frequently get stuck in traffic. Inside the vehicle, it is  
usually stuffy and packed with passengers. Even though the destination  
serves an important purpose, the journey itself brings me little joy. I  
cannot enjoy sightseeing or relax along the way. Most of the time I just  
sit there feeling bored and impatient, staring out of the window waiting  
to arrive. There is no practical alternative transport for us, so I have to  
repeat this unpleasant short journey month after month.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第136页

我经常要进行却并不喜欢的短途出行，就是每个月我和妈妈去往市中心医院的  
路程。我们固定周末要陪年迈的外婆做定期体检。路程不算远，坐公交大概四  
十分钟，但整个过程总是耗尽我的精力。我们必须一大早就出发，避开医院庞  
大的人流。周末高峰时段公交线路经常拥堵，我们常常遇上堵车。公交车车厢  
里闷热拥挤。尽管此行目的十分重要，但这段旅途本身几乎无法带给我愉悦。  
沿途我没法欣赏风景，也不能好好放松。大多数时候我只能无聊又焦躁地坐  
着，望着窗外等待抵达目的地。我们没有其他更合适的出行选择，所以只能月  
复一月重复这段令人不快的短途行程。  
Part3  
Q1. Which is better for knowing more about a country? Travelling or  
reading books about it?  
Answer：  
Travelling and reading books each have their own strengths for  
understanding a country. Books offer systematic historical background  
and theoretical knowledge, free from the limits of time and money.  
However, reading cannot give you real‑life sensory experience. When  
you travel, you meet local people, taste local food and witness

## PDF第137页

real‑world social conditions. To gain a well‑rounded understanding,  
combining both approaches would be the wisest choice.  
中文翻译：  
旅行和阅读书籍，在了解一个国家这件事上各有优势。书本可以提供系统的历  
史背景与理论知识，不受时间金钱条件制约。但读书无法给到真实的感官体  
验。旅行的时候，你可以接触当地人，品尝本土美食，亲眼看见真实社会状  
况。想要获得全面的认知，把两种方式结合才是最好的选择。  
Q2. How much time do you think people should spend on a trip  
abroad?  
Answer：  
There is no universal standard for how long an overseas trip should last.  
Short trips of three to five days suit tourists who only want to visit  
famous landmarks. Two weeks or longer allows travellers to dive deeper  
into local culture and slow down their pace. People’s available holiday  
time, budget and personal energy all shape the length of their journey.  
Quality matters more than pure duration.

## PDF第138页

中文翻译：  
境外旅行该持续多久并没有统一标准。三到五天短途，适合只想打卡知名景点  
的游客。两周甚至更久的行程，可以让旅行者深度感受本土文化，放慢节奏。  
人们可支配假期、预算、个人精力都会决定旅途时长。旅行质量远比单纯的时  
间长短更加重要。  
Q3. Who prefers travelling abroad? The young or the old?  
Answer：  
Young people are generally more eager to travel overseas. They are  
more willing to accept unfamiliar cultures, and they have greater  
physical stamina for long‑distance flights and tight travel schedules.  
That being said, many elderly groups also take foreign trips after  
retirement. They have more free time, yet they tend to choose more  
comfortable and relaxed tour arrangements.  
中文翻译：  
总体来说年轻人更向往出国旅行。他们更愿意接纳陌生文化，体力也更能承受  
长途航班和紧凑行程。当然不少老年人退休之后也会出境旅游，他们拥有更多  
空闲时间，但会偏向选择舒适松弛的旅行安排。

## PDF第139页

Q4. Do Chinese people like travelling abroad?  
Answer：  
Large numbers of Chinese citizens enjoy overseas travelling, especially  
when travel restrictions are lifted. Many hope to experience different  
cultures, taste foreign food and take in unique natural scenery. Even so,  
overseas trips require considerable money and holiday leave. Therefore,  
not everyone can afford such experiences. Domestic tourism still  
remains the first option for most ordinary people.  
中文翻译：  
大量中国人喜欢出境游，尤其是旅行限制放开之后。很多人希望体验异国文  
化、品尝外国美食，欣赏独特自然风光。但出国旅行需要可观的金钱与假期，  
并不是所有人都负担得起。国内游依旧是大多数普通人的首要选择。

And explain why you think this person is popular

The popular person I would like to talk about is a young university  
lecturer at my former college. I usually see him during lectures, school  
public sharing events, or on the university’s online social media  
accounts. He possesses a warm and approachable personality, and he  
never acts arrogant despite receiving wide praise from students. His  
classes are never dull and textbook‑driven. He mixes real‑life social  
cases, interesting stories and interactive discussions into his teaching  
content. Outside of class, he is willing to communicate with students  
patiently. He gives thoughtful advice on academic confusion and even  
personal life puzzles. What truly makes him popular is that he genuinely  
respects every student. He does not only focus on top‑performing

## PDF第141页

pupils, but also pays attention to average learners. Students feel  
comfortable sharing their worries with him. His kindness, rich  
knowledge and humorous teaching style together win him wide‑spread  
admiration across the whole campus.  
中文翻译：  
我想描述的受欢迎的人，是我之前大学里的一位年轻讲师。我通常会在课堂、  
学校公开分享活动，或是学校线上社交账号上看到他。他性格温和亲切，即便  
收获学生们大量好评，也从不高傲自大。他的课堂不会枯燥死板照本宣科，会  
把现实社会案例、趣味故事和互动讨论融入教学内容。课后，他愿意耐心和学  
生沟通，针对学业困惑，甚至生活上的难题给出周全建议。他之所以如此受欢  
迎，真正的原因在于他发自内心尊重每一位学生。他不会只看重成绩优异的学  
生，同样也关注普通学习者。学生们可以毫无压力向他倾诉烦恼。他的善良、  
渊博学识以及幽默授课风格，让他在整个校园广受喜爱。  
Part3  
Q1. Why do people want to be popular?  
Answer：

## PDF第142页

Many people hope to become popular for several psychological  
reasons. Popularity means being accepted and recognised by others,  
which satisfies people’s natural need for a sense of belonging. Popular  
individuals gain more opportunities in social activities and sometimes in  
work environments. Even so, chasing popularity blindly can be risky,  
because trying to please everyone may make people lose their true  
selves.  
中文翻译：  
很多人渴望受人欢迎有多方面心理原因。受欢迎代表得到他人接纳与认可，满  
足人天生的归属感。受欢迎的人，在社交甚至职场中可以获得更多机会。但盲  
目追逐人气会有风险，刻意讨好所有人，有可能让人丢失真实的自我。  
Q2. Is it easier to become popular nowadays?  
Answer：  
In the digital age, it is comparatively easier to gain public attention than  
in the past. Social‑media platforms offer channels for ordinary people to  
show their talents to huge audiences. However, gaining short‑term  
online fame is different from earning genuine long‑term respect. Quick

## PDF第143页

popularity can fade away just as fast as it appears. Real lasting  
popularity still relies on personal qualities.  
中文翻译：  
数字时代，相比过去获得大众关注变得相对容易。社交媒体平台给到普通人渠  
道，向海量观众展示自己的才华。但短暂网络走红，和赢得长久真正的尊重不  
是一回事。速成的人气消失起来也很快。真正持久的受欢迎，依旧依靠个人本  
身的品质。  
Q3. What are the qualities of being a good teacher?  
Answer：  
A good teacher needs more than solid professional knowledge.  
Patience, strong communication skills and genuine care for students are  
equally essential. Great teachers can explain complex knowledge in  
simple terms, inspire students’ curiosity and respect individual  
differences among learners. Besides, they should set positive moral  
examples for young people. Subject knowledge alone cannot make  
somebody an outstanding educator.

## PDF第144页

中文翻译：  
一名好老师，不只是需要扎实专业学识。耐心、良好沟通能力，发自内心关心  
学生，同样不可或缺。优秀教师可以把复杂知识讲得通俗易懂，激发学生求知  
欲，尊重学生个体差异。此外，他们要给年轻人树立正向道德榜样。单凭学科  
知识，并不能成就一名出色教育者。  
Q4. Do you think good teachers are always popular among students?  
Answer：  
Generally speaking, competent teachers tend to win students’  
affection, but this is not always guaranteed. Some teachers are highly  
knowledgeable yet strict and serious in manner. Their students respect  
them deeply, but may not treat them as hugely popular figures in class.  
Popularity and teaching effectiveness are two separate concepts that  
should not be completely equated.  
中文翻译：  
总体来看能力出众的老师容易收获学生喜爱，但这并不是绝对。有些老师学识  
很高，但行事严格不苟言笑。学生内心敬重他们，但未必会觉得他们是班级里  
人气很高的人。受欢迎程度和教学效果是两套不同概念，不能完全划等号。

## PDF第145页

Q5. Is it important for a teacher to be popular?  
Answer：  
Being popular is a nice bonus for teachers, yet it is not the most vital  
requirement. The core goal of teaching is to pass on knowledge and  
guide students to grow. A teacher can be effective even without being  
widely liked. However, proper rapport with students will lower barriers  
of communication and improve teaching outcomes.  
中文翻译：  
受人欢迎对老师来说是锦上添花，但并非最重要条件。教学的核心目标是传授  
知识，引导学生成长。就算不是广受喜爱，老师依旧可以教学成果出色。不过  
和学生保持良好关系，会降低沟通隔阂，提升教学效果。  
Q6. Why are some students popular in school?  
Answer：  
Different strengths make students popular on campus. Some stand out

## PDF第146页

for outstanding academic performance or impressive sports talent.  
Others gain friendship because of easy‑going personalities, being  
helpful and good at communication. Physical appearance or fashionable  
hobbies can also make someone draw peers’ attention. Multiple  
factors combine to shape a student’s popularity among classmates.  
中文翻译：

育特长脱颖而出；还有一些人因为性格随和、乐于助人、擅长交际收获友谊。  
外貌、新潮爱好同样会吸引同辈目光。多重因素共同决定一个学生在同学当中  
的人气。

The successful sportsperson I greatly admire is a well‑known Chinese  
table‑tennis athlete. He has devoted most of his youth to professional  
table‑tennis training and competitions. He has won numerous gold  
medals in world‑class tournaments, including world championships and  
Olympic events. Beyond shining competition results, what impresses me  
most is his personal qualities. Off the pitch, he is described as  
down‑to‑earth and disciplined. He does not seek unnecessary media  
spotlight. Even after reaching the peak of his career, he still keeps strict  
daily training routines and stays modest about his achievements. He has  
suffered serious sports injuries and tough defeats during matches many  
times. However, he never gives in to setbacks. Instead, he analyses his  
weaknesses and keeps improving himself. For me, his achievements are  
praiseworthy, but his mental toughness is even more inspiring. His story  
teaches me that real success rarely comes by luck. It is built on  
long‑term hard work and the courage to face failures bravely.

乒乓球专业训练和赛事。他拿下无数世界级赛事金牌，包括世锦赛以及奥运  
会。除去亮眼的比赛成绩，最打动我的是他人格品质。赛场之外，大家评价他

日常训练，面对成绩始终谦逊。运动生涯中，他多次遭遇严重伤病，赛场上也  
经历过艰难失利。但他从来不会向挫折屈服，而是分析自身短板，持续提升自  
我。对我来说，他取得的成就固然值得称赞，但他强大的内心更加鼓舞人心。  
他的经历告诉我，真正的成功极少来自运气，而是建立在长久努力和勇敢直面  
失败的勇气之上。

Q1. Why are there so few top athletes?  
Answer：  
Becoming a top‑level athlete demands multiple rare conditions. Natural  
physical talent, years of extremely tough training, high‑quality coaching  
and good luck are all required. Lots of young sports hopefuls drop out  
because of injuries, unbearable pressure or limited talent. Only a tiny

## PDF第149页

minority can survive fierce competition and finally reach the highest  
stage.

练、高水平教练，还需要机遇。很多年轻体育苗子因为伤病、难以承受的压力  
或是天赋局限被淘汰。只有极少数人可以熬过激烈竞争，最终站上最高舞台。  
Q2. What is the most popular sport in your country?  
Answer：  
Table tennis enjoys massive popularity across China. It is widely known  
as our national sport. People of various ages play it in community sports  
grounds, schools and stadiums. Besides table tennis, basketball and  
badminton also attract huge participant groups. Different sports appeal  
to different age groups among ordinary citizens.  
中文翻译：  
乒乓球在中国拥有极高人气，被称为国球。各个年龄层的人，在社区运动场

地、学校、球馆都会打乒乓球。除此之外篮球和羽毛球参与人数也非常多。不

Q3. Is it easy to identify children’s talents?  
Answer：  
It is partially possible to spot children’s early‑stage talents, yet it  
cannot be one‑hundred‑percent accurate. Some kids show obvious gifts  
at a young age, but many talents only gradually emerge as they grow  
older. External factors such as training opportunities and family support  
also play huge roles. Early performance cannot fully decide future

支持这类外部条件同样作用巨大。幼年的表现不能完全决定未来潜力。  
Q4. Is talent important in sports?

Answer：  
Natural talent serves as a helpful starting point for sports players.  
Athletes with suitable physical conditions have inherent advantages in  
certain disciplines. Even so, raw talent alone will not create champions.  
Persistent training, strong mental quality and professional guidance are  
equally vital. Many gifted young athletes fail because they lack  
hard‑working attitudes.  
中文翻译：

专业指导同等重要。不少很有天赋的年轻选手，就因为不够刻苦最终没能成  
功。  
Q5. What qualities should an athlete have?

Apart from physical capability, top‑level athletes need several inner

## PDF第152页

qualities. Strong self‑discipline, the ability to handle pressure and  
resilience after defeat count as essential traits. Team‑work awareness is  
also necessary for group sports. Talent brings advantages, but  
perseverance and mental strength separate ordinary players from  
outstanding ones.  
中文翻译：

失利之后快速恢复的韧性，都是必备特质。团体项目还需要团队协作意识。天  
赋带来优势，但毅力和心理素质，才区分开普通选手和杰出选手。  
Q6. Should students have physical education and do sports at school?

scores, youngsters will lack necessary physical exercise. PE should be  
treated as a key subject rather than an unimportant extra activity.

力，还可以培养团队精神。如果学校只看重文化课分数，青少年就会缺少必要  
身体锻炼。体育课应当视作一门重要科目，而不是无足轻重的附加活动。

个月大概会来一到两次，大多选在周末。我喜欢这里有多重原因。首先，它向

围。一踏入馆内，我就可以逃离城市喧嚣干扰。不管是读书、写作业，或是单  
纯安静思考，这个空间都能带给我很强的平静与舒适感。  
Part3  
Q1. Do you think all old buildings should be demolished?  
Answer：  
I firmly believe old buildings should not all be pulled down blindly.  
Many historic old structures carry precious cultural and historical value.  
They serve as important physical witnesses to past times. Of course,  
some extremely unsafe worn‑out buildings without cultural significance  
do need reconstruction. The government should make judgements  
case‑by‑case instead of following one single rule for everything.  
中文翻译：  
我坚决认为不应该把老建筑全部盲目拆除。很多历史老建筑承载珍贵历史文化

## PDF第156页

价值，是过往时代重要实物见证。当然，一部分没有文化意义、建筑结构极度  
危险破旧的楼房，确实需要改造重建。政府应当具体建筑具体分析，而不是用  
一套标准一概而论。  
Q2. Do you think old buildings are valuable?  
Answer：  
Old buildings can possess great value in multiple dimensions.  
Historically, they record architectural styles, lifestyles and social  
conditions of bygone eras. Tourists are also drawn to well‑preserved  
ancient buildings, which help develop local tourism. Even so, not every  
old building is worth protecting. Safety risks and high maintenance  
costs also need to be fully considered.  
中文翻译：  
老建筑可以拥有多方面巨大价值。历史层面，它们记录过去时代的建筑风格、  
生活方式和社会面貌。保存完好的古建筑也会吸引游客，带动地方旅游业。但  
并不是所有老建筑都值得保护，安全隐患与高昂维护成本也需要充分考量。

Q1. What effect does stress have on health?  
Answer：  
Long‑term stress brings severe harm to people’s physical and mental  
wellbeing. It can cause sleeping troubles, loss of appetite and constant  
tiredness. In the long run, continuous pressure may raise risks of various  
illnesses. Mentally, stress can create anxiety, bad temper and low mood.  
Therefore, learning to release pressure is essential for maintaining  
overall health.  
中文翻译：  
长期压力会对人的身心健康造成严重伤害。它会带来睡眠障碍、食欲不振、持  
续疲惫。长久来看，持续压力会提升各类疾病风险。心理层面，压力会引发焦  
虑、易怒、情绪低落。因此学会释放压力，对维持整体健康至关重要。

## PDF第165页

Q2. Which group pays more attention to health, young people or old  
people?  
Answer：  
Generally speaking, elderly people tend to pay more conscious  
attention to physical health. As bodies gradually age, they realise the  
importance of keeping fit. Many young people take their good health  
for granted. They frequently stay up late and ignore exercise. Even so,  
more youngsters have started focusing on fitness in recent years.  
中文翻译：  
总体来说，老年人会更加有意识关注身体健康。随着身体慢慢衰老，他们明白  
保养身体的重要性。很多年轻人把身体健康视作理所当然，经常熬夜，忽视锻  
炼。不过近几年，越来越多年轻人也开始注重健身。

Q1. Are there many tall buildings in your country?  
Answer：  
There is a huge number of tall buildings across China, especially in  
large‑sized metropolises. Skyscrapers serve as office blocks, apartment  
housing and commercial shopping centres in city centres. Even some  
second‑tier cities are constructing more high‑rises as urbanisation  
moves forward. However, in small towns and rural areas, low‑rise houses  
still dominate the landscape. So the distribution of tall buildings heavily  
depends on local economic and urban‑development conditions.  
中文翻译：  
中国拥有数量庞大的高层建筑，尤其是在大型大都市。市中心的摩天大楼用作  
写字楼、住宅公寓以及商业购物中心。随着城市化推进，就连部分二线城市也  
在建更多高楼。但在小城镇和农村，低矮房屋依旧是主流。所以高层建筑的分  
布很大程度取决于当地经济和城市发展条件。

Q2. What are the differences between those tall buildings in your

Tall buildings in China differ greatly in function, appearance and design  
concept. Some are purely commercial office skyscrapers with sleek  
modern glass facades. Some are residential high‑rises focusing on living  
comfort. A small number of public high‑rises adopt creative  
cultural‑themed designs for museums or concert halls. Their height,  
building materials and inner layouts all change according to their  
intended uses. Economic budgets and local cultural features also shape  
their final look.

度、建材、内部布局都按照用途变化。预算与本土文化特色同样会塑造建筑最

## PDF第170页

Q3. Why are different places laid out and designed differently?  
Answer：  
Urban layout and architectural design vary from place to place due to  
multiple factors. Local climate and geographical conditions set basic  
limits for construction. Besides, local cultural traditions, population  
density and economic goals also play important roles. A bustling  
downtown business district prioritises commercial efficiency, while  
residential zones value quietness and living quality. Planners must  
adjust designs to fit real‑life local circumstances instead of copying one  
single model everywhere.  
中文翻译：  
不同地区城市布局与建筑设计各不相同，源于多重因素。当地气候地理条件给  
建筑设置基础限制。除此之外本土文化传统、人口密度、经济目标也起到重要  
作用。繁华市中心商业区优先考虑商业效率，居民区则看重安静与居住品质。  
规划者必须结合当地现实调整方案，不能到处照搬一套模板。  
Q4. What are the advantages of living in tall buildings?

## PDF第171页

Answer：  
Living in high‑rise buildings brings several obvious benefits. Residents  
can enjoy better panoramic views and sufficient natural daylight. Noise  
from streets on the ground floor is greatly reduced. In crowded cities,  
high‑rises help save valuable land space by housing more people within  
one building. Many modern high‑rises also equip residents with shared  
facilities such as elevators and security systems, improving daily  
convenience for dwellers.  
中文翻译：  
住在高层建筑有几处明显优势。住户可以拥有更好的全景视野，采光也更充  
足，来自地面街道的噪音大幅降低。人口密集城市当中，高层住宅可以在一栋  
楼容纳更多住户，节约宝贵土地。很多现代高层还配备电梯、安保等共享设  
施，提升居民日常生活便利度。  
Q5. Why do some people like to remodel and decorate their homes  
themselves?  
Answer：  
Many people choose to decorate their houses by themselves for two

main reasons. First, they can fully control styles, colours and material  
choices according to their personal tastes, instead of accepting generic  
decoration plans from companies. Second, self‑remodelling allows them  
to manage the budget flexibly and cut unnecessary spending. Even so,  
home renovation requires professional knowledge, so DIY decoration  
also contains potential risks for inexperienced people.  
中文翻译：  
很多人选择自己装修房屋主要有两点原因。第一，可以完全按照个人喜好把控

灵活管控预算，削减不必要开销。即便如此，家装改造需要专业知识，缺乏经

Q6. What kind of interior design style do most people like?

among most Chinese people. Minimalist modern designs without overly  
fancy decorations are widely favoured, because they create comfortable  
living spaces and are easy to keep tidy. Some groups also prefer warm

retro‑style decoration. Nevertheless, personal income, family size and

Q1. What kind of videos do people in your country like to watch?  
Answer：  
Chinese audiences enjoy a wide variety of online video content. Short  
comedy clips, real‑life vlogs, food‑making videos and educational  
knowledge‑sharing materials attract huge view counts. Young people  
also favour drama excerpts and talent‑show highlights. Middle‑aged  
and older viewers tend to prefer life‑tips videos, news commentaries  
and traditional cultural content. People’s video preferences shift

according to their age and personal hobbies.

偏爱生活技巧、新闻评论、传统文化类内容。人们的视频喜好随着年龄和个人  
爱好发生变化。  
Q2. Which is more helpful, watching videos or reading books?  
Answer：  
Neither videos nor books are absolutely superior; they serve different  
purposes. Videos deliver vivid visual and auditory information, making  
complicated knowledge easier to understand for beginners. However,  
books allow readers to control reading speed freely and spend longer  
thinking deeply over content. Videos suit intuitive learning, while  
printed books are better for in‑depth analysis. Combining both  
resources creates the best learning outcome.

## PDF第177页

初学者更容易理解复杂知识。但读书读者可以自由控制阅读速度，花更长时间  
深度思考内容。视频适合直观学习，纸质书籍更适合深度钻研。把两种资源结  
合，学习效果最好。  
Q3. What skills can people learn from watching videos?

videos. Viewers can learn cooking, hand‑craft techniques, photography  
and language‑learning methods. Some instructional videos even teach  
office software operations or simple physical fitness exercises. Still,  
videos can only offer theoretical demonstrations. To truly master a skill,  
real‑world repeated practice remains indispensable.

Q6. What makes a video go viral online?  
Answer：  
Multiple factors make a video become viral on the internet. Powerful

## PDF第180页

emotional resonance, fresh unique perspectives or entertaining plots  
can catch audiences’ attention. Timely hot‑topic connections also help  
spread content rapidly. Nevertheless, luck and platform  
recommendation algorithms also play a large role. Even high‑quality  
works cannot guarantee widespread popularity in complex online  
environments.  
中文翻译：  
多重因素让视频在网络走红。强烈的情感共鸣、新颖独特视角、有趣剧情，都  
可以抓住观众眼球。紧跟当下热点话题也会加速传播。但运气以及平台推荐算  
法同样起到很大作用。就算内容质量很高，在复杂网络环境下，也无法保证一  
定广泛传播。

✨Part2 保留题 3 去过的无聊地方  
Describe a boring place  
You should say:

## PDF第181页

‑ Where it is  
‑ Who you went there with  
‑ What you did there  
And explain why you think it is a boring place  
Answer：  
The boring place I want to talk about is a small local souvenir market  
located near a well‑known tourist spot in my city. I visited this market  
two years ago during a public holiday, together with two of my  
classmates. We originally hoped to hunt for distinctive local handicrafts  
as small gifts for family members. However, our experience turned out  
to be really disappointing. Almost every shop sold identical  
mass‑produced souvenirs. Most goods were cheap trinkets with little  
local cultural characteristic. We walked from one stall to another, yet we  
barely found anything interesting. The shop‑keepers kept calling out to  
pass‑by visitors trying to push sales, which created a noisy, stressful  
atmosphere. There were no creative exhibitions, no local folk‑art  
performances and nothing special to explore. After wandering around  
for less than forty minutes, we decided to leave early. It felt like a  
complete waste of our holiday time. That is why I regard this  
tourist‑oriented market as a pretty boring place.

## PDF第182页

中文翻译：  
我想聊的无聊的地方，是本市一处知名景点旁边的小型纪念品集市。两年前的  
一个公共假期，我和两位同学一起去到那里。我们原本想淘一些有本土特色手  
工艺品，当作送给家人的小礼物。但体验却十分令人失望。几乎每家店铺售卖  
的都是一模一样批量生产的纪念品。大多商品都是廉价小饰品，几乎没有本土  
文化特色。我们一个摊位逛到另一个，几乎找不到有意思的东西。店主不停吆  
喝招揽过路游客强行推销，环境嘈杂，让人有压迫感。这里没有创意展览，没  
有民间艺术表演，没有值得探索的特色事物。逛了还不到四十分钟，我们就决  
定提前离开。感觉完全浪费假期时间。这就是为什么我觉得这个面向游客的集  
市相当无聊。  
Part3  
Q1. Why do most children think education is boring?  
Answer：  
Quite a number of children feel education is boring for understandable  
reasons. Some teaching methods rely heavily on mechanical  
memorisation and repetitive exercises, lacking fun interactive links.

## PDF第183页

When learning content feels disconnected from real‑life experience, kids  
struggle to generate curiosity. That said, boredom also arises if the  
learning material is either too easy or far too difficult for students’  
current level.  
中文翻译：  
不少孩子觉得学习很无聊，背后原因可以理解。一部分教学方式过度依赖机械  
背诵、重复刷题，缺少有趣互动环节。如果学习内容和现实生活脱节，很难激  
发孩子好奇心。此外，如果学习内容对于学生现有水平过简单或者难度过高，  
同样会产生枯燥感。  
Q2. Why aren’t young people willing to listen to the experiences of  
older people?  
Answer：  
A major cause lies in their different life backgrounds. The advice from  
older generations is based on their own historical social environment,  
which may feel out‑of‑date for young people living in the digital era.  
Besides, teenagers long for independent judgement. Sometimes they  
resist suggestions simply because they hate being told what to do.

## PDF第184页

Mutual patient communication can improve this situation.  
中文翻译：  
主要原因来自两代人截然不同的生活背景。老一辈的人生建议建立在他们所处  
的时代社会环境，对于身处数字时代的年轻人，会感觉已经过时。此外青少年  
渴望独立判断，有时候抗拒建议，仅仅是反感被人说教。互相耐心沟通可以改  
善这种情况。  
Q3. What can people do when they feel bored?  
Answer：  
People have many practical ways to deal with boredom. Some choose  
to take up light hobbies such as reading, drawing or listening to music.  
Others go outdoors for walks or physical exercise to refresh their mind.  
Boredom can also become a good chance for self‑reflection. The key is  
to avoid mindless short‑video scrolling which only creates temporary  
amusement without real fulfilment.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第185页

人们应对无聊有很多切实可行的办法。一部分人培养轻松爱好，读书画画听音  
乐；另一部分人出门散步运动，转换精神状态。无聊同样可以成为自我反思的  
契机。关键要避免漫无目的地刷短视频，那只能带来短暂消遣，得不到真正的  
充实感。  
Q4. Why are some teachers’ classes boring? Are there any solutions?  
Answer：  
Some classes become dull mainly because teaching styles are too rigid.  
Teachers keep giving long monologues without interactive activities.  
Course content is limited to textbooks alone, without linking to  
real‑world cases. Possible solutions include adding group discussions,  
vivid real‑life examples and multimedia resources. Teachers also need to  
observe students’ feedback and adjust their rhythm in time.  
中文翻译：  
部分课堂枯燥，主要是教学模式过于死板。老师长时间单向灌输，缺少互动活  
动，内容局限课本，不结合现实案例。解决办法可以加入小组讨论、生动现实  
案例以及多媒体素材。老师也要观察学生反馈，及时调整授课节奏。

## PDF第186页

Q5. Why do some young people feel bored when talking with old  
people?  
Answer：  
The generation gap contributes to this feeling. Older people often talk  
about their past experiences which young listeners cannot relate to.  
Their topics may centre on outdated life circumstances unfamiliar to  
youngsters. Without shared topics, conversations lack common ground.  
However, young people can still practise active listening, and find small  
shared themes to keep communication going.  
中文翻译：  
代沟造成这种感受。老年人经常聊自己过往经历，年轻人很难产生共鸣，话题  
围绕年轻人不熟悉的旧时代生活。缺少共同话题，对话就没有交集。但年轻人  
依旧可以练习积极倾听，寻找少量共同话题维持交流。  
Q6. Do most people think news about celebrities is boring?  
Answer：

## PDF第187页

Attitudes towards celebrity news are divided among the public. Some  
audiences are fascinated by stars’ private lives and follow  
entertainment updates closely. Other people consider most celebrity  
gossip empty and meaningless. They believe media should focus more  
on social‑relevant news rather than chasing stars’ trivial daily stories.  
Personal interests determine people’s opinions on this type of news.  
中文翻译：  
大众对于名人新闻态度两极分化。一部分观众对明星私生活很感兴趣，密切关  
注娱乐动态。另一些人认为大部分明星八卦空洞无意义，觉得媒体应当更多关  
注社会新闻，而不是追逐明星生活琐事。个人兴趣决定人们对这类新闻的看  
法。

✨Part2 保留题 4 早起经历  
Describe a time when you got up early  
You should say:

## PDF第188页

‑ When it was  
‑ What you did  
‑ Why you got up early  
And how you felt about it  
Answer：  
The early‑rising experience I would like to talk about happened last  
spring. I got up extremely early at half past four in the morning to go  
mountain‑climbing with my classmates. We planned to reach the  
mountain top to watch the sunrise over the horizon. In order to arrive  
before sunrise, we had to set off very long before dawn. When my alarm  
clock rang, it was still completely dark outside. I felt sleepy and  
reluctant to climb out of my warm bed. Still, I forced myself to get  
dressed quickly and met my classmates at the pre‑arranged meeting  
spot. We took a taxi to the foot of the mountain and began hiking  
upwards step by step. The mountain air was fresh and cool, and we  
could see faint hints of light appearing in the distant sky. Eventually we  
successfully reached the peak just as the sun slowly rose above the hills.  
The magnificent golden sunrise scenery totally made up for my sleep  
loss. Although I felt physically tired afterwards, I gained a strong sense  
of satisfaction from this special early‑morning adventure.

## PDF第189页

中文翻译：  
我想聊的早起经历发生在去年春天。我凌晨四点半就早早起床，和同学们去爬  
山。我们打算登上山顶看日出。为了赶在日出前抵达，我们必须天还没亮就出  
发。闹钟响起的时候外面依旧漆黑一片，我睡意浓重，很舍不得温暖被窝。但  
我还是强迫自己迅速穿好衣服，到约定地点和同学集合。我们打车到山脚下，  
一步一步向上攀登。山间空气清新凉爽，远方天际透出微弱光亮。最终我们刚  
好在太阳慢慢越过山头的时候成功登顶。壮丽的金色日出美景，完全弥补了睡  
眠不足。尽管之后身体感觉疲惫，但这次特别的清晨冒险带给我很强的满足  
感。  
Part3  
Q1. Do you know anyone who likes to get up early?  
Answer：  
Quite a number of people around me prefer getting up early. Many  
elderly relatives form the habit of rising with the sun after decades of  
life. Some young people who keep fitness routines also choose to get  
out of bed early for morning exercise. Early risers can enjoy quiet

## PDF第190页

morning hours for reading, planning daily tasks or doing sports before  
the world becomes busy.  
中文翻译：  
我身边不少人喜欢早起。很多上了年纪的长辈，几十年生活养成日出而作的习  
惯。部分坚持健身的年轻人，也选择早起晨练。习惯早起的人，可以在世界还  
没喧嚣起来的时候，享受安静清晨，读书、规划一天任务或者运动。  
Q2. Why do people get up early?  
Answer：  
People get up early for various practical reasons. Some must start work  
or commute long distances, so an early start guarantees they will not be  
late. Others make use of quiet morning time for exercise, study or  
personal hobbies. A small group of individuals simply hold long‑term  
biological clock habits of waking up before sunrise. Work requirements,  
personal goals and natural body rhythms all influence people’s rising  
time.

## PDF第191页

中文翻译：  
人们早起有各类现实原因。一部分人要上班，通勤距离长，早起保证不迟到。  
另一些人利用安静早晨运动、学习、发展个人爱好。还有少部分人长期生物钟  
习惯，天没亮就醒。工作需求、个人目标、身体自然节律都会影响起床时间。  
Q3. What kinds of occasions need people to arrive early?  
Answer：  
Multiple important occasions require people to turn up ahead of  
scheduled time. Job interviews, major examinations and long‑distance  
transportation such as flights all demand early arrival. Large‑scale group  
activities like sports competitions or group tours also expect  
participants to arrive early for preparation. Showing up early  
demonstrates respect and reduces risks caused by unexpected delays.  
中文翻译：  
很多重要场合要求人们提前抵达。求职面试、大型考试、坐飞机这类长途出  
行，都需要早到。体育赛事、团体旅行这类大型集体活动，同样希望参与者提  
前到场做准备。提前到场体现尊重，也降低突发延误带来的风险。

## PDF第192页

Q4. Why do some people like to stay up late?  
Answer：  
Many people favour staying up late mainly because night‑time is the  
only private time belonging to themselves. After finishing study or work  
during daytime, they can freely enjoy hobbies without outside  
disturbance. Some individuals also suffer from irregular biological clocks  
and cannot fall asleep easily. However, frequent late nights will gradually  
create harm to physical health.  
中文翻译：  
很多人喜欢熬夜，主要因为夜晚才是完全属于自己的私人时间。白天忙完学习  
工作，夜里可以不受打扰自由做喜欢的事。还有一部分人生物钟紊乱，很难早  
早入睡。但经常性熬夜，会慢慢损害身体健康。  
Q5. Is it good to arrive early in any situation?  
Answer：

## PDF第193页

Arriving early brings benefits in most formal scenarios, yet it is not  
always desirable. Showing up excessively early for casual private  
meetings may put pressure on hosts who are still making preparations.  
For informal friend gatherings, arriving 5‑10 minutes ahead is  
appropriate, but arriving one hour too early will cause inconvenience for  
others. So people need to judge according to event nature.  
中文翻译：  
大多数正式场合早到有好处，但并不是所有情况都适合。私人非正式会面，如  
果到得过早，会给还在准备的主人带来压力。朋友休闲聚会，提前 5‑10 分钟  
刚刚好，提前一小时就会给别人添麻烦。人们需要根据活动性质做出判断。  
Q6. What kind of people like getting up early?  
Answer：  
Early risers cover diverse groups. The elderly are one typical group.  
Besides them, self‑disciplined young people who pursue  
self‑improvement often choose to get up early to exercise or study.  
People with heavy work duties and long commutes also have to form  
the habit of rising early. It can be a natural biological tendency or a

## PDF第194页

self‑created life discipline.  
中文翻译：  
习惯早起的人群多种多样。老年人是典型群体。除此之外，追求自我提升、自  
律的年轻人，经常早起运动学习。工作繁重、通勤距离长的人，也不得不养成  
早起习惯。早起既可以是天生生物钟，也可以是后天自我建立的生活自律。

✨Part2 保留题 5 喜欢在家 / 花园种菜的人

home or in the garden

‑ Who this person is  
‑ What plants he/she grows  
‑ How he/she grows the plants  
And explain why he/she loves growing plants

## PDF第195页

Answer：  
The person who loves growing plants is my paternal grandmother. She  
has been keen on gardening for more than twenty years. At her small  
balcony and tiny backyard garden, she grows a rich variety of plants.  
Apart from beautiful flowering shrubs such as roses and jasmine, she  
also plants practical edible greens including green vegetables, chives  
and small tomatoes. She takes care of these plants with great patience  
every single day. She waters them at proper time periods, adds natural  
organic fertiliser, clears away weeds and carefully watches out for plant  
diseases or insect pests. She reads simple gardening articles to learn  
better planting skills. For her, growing plants is far more than a pastime.  
When seeds gradually sprout, bloom or bear fruit, she gains a strong  
sense of achievement. Watching living things thrive brings her inner  
peace and joy. In addition, home‑grown vegetables are fresh and safe  
for family meals. This combination of pleasure and practical value  
explains her deep passion for gardening.  
中文翻译：  
热爱种植植物的人是我的奶奶。她爱好园艺已经二十多年。在她家小阳台和小  
小的后院花园，她种了各式各样植物。除月季、茉莉这类开花灌木，她还种实

## PDF第196页

用可食用蔬菜：青菜、小葱、小番茄。她每天十分耐心照料植物，按时浇水，  
施加天然有机肥，清除杂草，细心留意病虫害。她还阅读简单园艺文章学习更  
好种植技巧。对她来说，种菜养花不只是消遣。种子慢慢发芽、开花、结果的  
时候，她收获满满的成就感。看着生命蓬勃生长带给她内心安宁和喜悦。而且  
自家种的蔬菜新鲜安全，可以给家人做菜。乐趣加上实用价值，解释了她为什  
么如此热爱园艺。  
Part3  
Q1. What are the advantages of growing vegetables or flowers at  
home?  
Answer：  
Home gardening brings multiple benefits. Growing edible vegetables  
supplies fresh, chemical‑free food for family members. Raising flowers  
beautifies living surroundings and eases people’s daily pressure. The  
process of taking care of plants itself serves as a relaxing hobby. Even  
limited balcony space allows city dwellers to get close to nature without  
travelling far away.

## PDF第197页

中文翻译：  
家庭种植蔬菜花卉有多方面好处。种菜可以给家人提供新鲜无化学药剂的食  
材；养花美化居住环境，舒缓日常压力。照料植物的过程本身就是放松的爱  
好。就算只有阳台狭小空间，城市居民不用远行就可以亲近大自然。  
Q2. Is it easy to grow plants at home?  
Answer：  
Growing plants at home is not always simple. Different species demand  
different sunlight, water and soil conditions. Many new plant‑lovers kill  
their plants because of over‑watering or unsuitable light. People need to  
learn basic knowledge about each plant’s requirements. With  
accumulated practical experience, home gardening will gradually  
become much easier.  
中文翻译：

求各不相同。很多新手因为浇水过多或者光照不合适把植物养死。人们需要学  
习每种植物基础养护知识，积累实操经验之后，家庭园艺才会慢慢变得轻松。

Q3. Do many people grow vegetables or flowers at home in your

vegetables and flowers. City residents living in apartments mostly grow  
flowers and small potted greens on balconies. Due to space restrictions,  
urban‑dwelling people rarely plant large quantities of vegetables  
compared with suburban or rural residents.

市居民，大多在阳台养花和小型盆栽绿植。受空间限制，城市住户很少像郊区

from seeds. It works as an effective stress reliever from busy  
modern‑day life. Some others enjoy the practical reward of harvesting  
home‑grown food. Both emotional satisfaction and real‑world gains  
attract plant‑lovers.  
中文翻译：

着种子生长的乐趣。园艺是现代忙碌生活很好的解压方式。还有人享受收获自  
家种植食材的实用回报。情感满足加上现实收益，共同吸引园艺爱好者。  
Q5. Why do some people prefer to grow their own fruits and vegetables

Home‑produced fruits and vegetables give people confidence over food  
safety. Growers can control fertilisers and avoid harmful chemical  
pesticides. Besides, harvesting food you planted yourself creates a

unique sense of accomplishment. Of course, home planting yields are

一般不大，对大部分家庭，无法完全替代市场买菜。  
Q6. Do you think students should learn to grow plant?

✨Part2 保留题 6 想颁布的新法律  
Describe a new law you would like to introduce in your country

‑ What law it is  
‑ What changes this law brings

‑ How you came up with the new law  
And explain how you feel about this new law  
Answer：

## PDF第202页

If I could introduce a new national law, I would make a law requiring all  
public‑service venues to install accessible facilities for disabled citizens.  
At present, quite a lot of public buildings such as small shopping malls,  
community cultural centres and bus stops still lack complete barrier‑free  
equipment. This new law would force these public locations to build  
ramps, accessible restrooms and clear guidance signs for people with  
physical disabilities. Once the law comes into force, disabled residents  
can take part in social activities independently without relying on  
others’ constant help. I believe this law will gain wide support from  
the public, although some small‑scale business owners may worry  
about reconstruction costs. This idea occurred to me when I once saw a  
disabled person struggling to enter a public building with no slope  
access. I feel this law is highly necessary. A civilised society ought to  
offer equal public‑service opportunities for every group of citizens,  
including people living with disabilities.  
中文翻译：  
如果我可以颁布一条新国家法律，我希望立法，要求所有公共服务场所为残障  
公民配备无障碍设施。现在，不少小型商场、社区文化中心、公交站点等公共  
建筑，依旧缺少完整无障碍配套。这条新法律将强制这类公共场所修建无障碍  
坡道、无障碍卫生间，以及清晰的指引标识。法律生效之后，残障市民可以独

## PDF第203页

立参与社会活动，不必时时刻刻依赖他人帮助。我相信这条法律会获得大众广  
泛支持，尽管部分小商户会担忧改造开销。我产生这个想法，是曾经亲眼看到  
残障人士因为没有坡道，艰难进入一栋公共建筑。我认为这条法律十分必要。  
一个文明社会，应当给包括残障群体在内的每一类公民平等的公共服务机会。  
Part3  
Q1. What rules should students follow at school?  
Answer：  
Students need to observe a series of school‑based rules. They ought to  
attend classes on time, keep good classroom discipline and treat  
teachers and classmates politely. Besides, they must protect school  
public property and refrain from bullying behaviour. School rules are  
not designed to restrict freedom blindly. Instead, they create safe and  
orderly study surroundings for every student on campus.  
中文翻译：  
学生需要遵守一系列校规。应当按时上课，维持课堂纪律，礼貌对待老师同

## PDF第204页

学。同时爱护学校公共财物，杜绝欺凌行为。校规不是为了盲目限制自由，而  
是为每一位在校学生打造安全有序的学习环境。  
Q2. Do people in your country usually obey the law?  
Answer：  
The vast majority of citizens in China consciously abide by national laws.  
People receive legal education from school and society from an early  
age. Minor violations such as random jay‑walking still appear from time  
to time. Serious criminal offences remain comparatively rare. Strict  
punishment together with public education together maintain overall  
social order.  
中文翻译：  
绝大多数中国公民自觉遵守国家法律。人们从小接受来自学校社会的法治教  
育。像乱穿马路这类轻微违法行为依旧时有发生，但严重刑事犯罪相对少见。  
严厉处罚加上普法教育，共同维护整体社会秩序。  
Q3. What kinds of behavior are considered as good behavior?

## PDF第205页

Answer：  
Good behaviour covers both public and private‑domain performance. In  
public spaces, being polite, queue‑jumping‑free and respecting other  
people’s rights count as good conduct. In personal life, honesty,  
keeping promises and taking responsibility for your own actions are  
also essential. Good behaviour is reflected in countless small daily  
moves, not merely grand gestures.  
中文翻译：  
良好行为涵盖公共与私人领域表现。公共场合懂礼貌、不插队、尊重他人权  
益，属于良好举止。个人生活当中，诚实守信，为自己行为负责同样必不可  
少。好的行为体现在无数日常细小举动，而不只是轰轰烈烈的大事。  
Q4. Do you think children can learn about the law outside of school?  
Answer：  
Children can acquire legal knowledge from many sources outside  
school. Family education, news media, short‑video clips and real‑life

## PDF第206页

social events all deliver legal‑related messages. Even so, information  
from outside sources may be fragmented or sometimes misleading.  
Therefore school systematic legal teaching still cannot be replaced.  
中文翻译：  
孩子在校外可以从很多渠道学到法律知识。家庭教育、新闻媒体、短视频、真  
实社会事件，都会传递和法律相关信息。但校外获取的知识碎片化，有时还存  
在误导。所以学校系统法治教育依旧不可替代。  
Q5. What are the benefits for people to obey rules?  
Answer：  
Observing rules brings collective and individual benefits. For society,  
rules prevent chaos and protect public interests. For individuals,  
following regulations keeps people away from punishment and creates  
predictable safe surroundings. If large numbers of people ignore rules,  
everyone’s quality of life will decline. So rule‑abiding behaviour  
protects the interests of every member of society.

## PDF第207页

中文翻译：  
遵守规则对集体和个人都有益。对社会来说，规则防止混乱，保护公共利益。  
对个人，守规矩可以远离处罚，营造可预期的安全环境。如果大量人无视规  
则，所有人生活质量都会下降。所以守规矩保护社会每一位成员的利益。  
Q6. How can parents teach children to obey rules?  
Answer：  
Parents can teach rule‑following behaviour through two main  
approaches. First of all, adults need to set good personal examples in  
daily life. Secondly, parents should explain why rules exist instead of  
simply ordering kids to obey. Gentle guidance and reasonable rewards  
or minor punishments help children gradually build awareness of rules  
from an early age.  
中文翻译：  
家长主要通过两种方式教会孩子守规则。第一，成年人日常生活做好榜样。第  
二，向孩子解释规则存在的原因，而不是单纯下命令。温和引导，搭配合理奖  
惩，帮助孩子从小逐步建立规则意识。

Q1. Do you still keep in touch with your friends from childhood? Why or  
why not?  
Answer：  
Some people maintain close contact with childhood friends, while  
others drift apart. When people move to different cities for further  
education or jobs, living distance creates barriers for face‑to‑face  
meetings. Life experiences gradually become different, so shared topics  
may reduce. Nevertheless, modern social‑media tools make  
long‑distance contact much easier than in the past.  
中文翻译：  
一部分人和发小保持紧密联系，另一部分慢慢疏远。当人们为求学工作去往不

现代社交媒体工具，让远距离联络相比过去方便很多。  
Q2. How important is childhood friendship to children?

## PDF第211页

Answer：  
Childhood friendship carries huge significance for children’s growth.  
Through interacting with peers, youngsters learn skills such as sharing,  
compromise and conflict resolution. Good friends offer emotional  
support when kids feel upset. Early‑age friendship shapes children’s  
social competence and builds their sense of security in interpersonal  
relationships.  
中文翻译：  
童年友谊对孩子成长意义重大。通过和同伴交往，孩子学会分享、妥协、处理  
矛盾。好朋友在孩子难过的时候提供情感支持。幼年友谊塑造孩子社交能力，  
建立人际关系层面的安全感。  
Q3. What do you think of communicating via social media?  
Answer：  
Social‑media‑based communication has both merits and drawbacks. It  
breaks geographical limits and allows people to keep in touch with  
distant acquaintances conveniently. However, online interaction lacks  
real‑life non‑verbal signals such as facial expressions and gestures.

## PDF第212页

Misunderstandings happen more easily, and it cannot fully replace  
face‑to‑face conversations.  
中文翻译：  
社交媒体沟通有利有弊。打破地理限制，方便和远方熟人保持联络。但线上交  
流缺少表情、手势这类现实非语言信息，更容易产生误解，无法完全替代面对  
面交谈。  
Q4. Do you think online communication through social media will  
replace face‑to‑face communication?  
Answer：  
I do not believe online communication will completely take the place of  
in‑person interaction. Digital messages are convenient yet limited.  
Face‑to‑face talks deliver richer emotional information through  
eye‑contact, tone of voice and body language. Important conversations  
such as sincere comfort or serious discussion still require real‑world  
meetings between people.

## PDF第213页

中文翻译：  
我认为线上沟通不会彻底取代面对面交流。数字消息虽然便捷，但存在局限。  
面对面谈话，通过眼神、语气、肢体语言传递更丰富情感信息。真诚安慰、严  
肃讨论这类重要对话，依旧需要现实当中人与人见面完成。  
Q5. What's the difference between having younger friends and older  
friends?  
Answer：  
Friends of different age groups bring distinct experiences. Younger  
friends often fill conversations with fresh popular ideas and new‑trend  
topics. Older friends can offer mature life advice based on their richer  
life experience. Both kinds of friendships have value. Mixing friends  
from varied age ranges broadens one’s viewpoints greatly.  
中文翻译：  
不同年龄段的朋友带来截然不同体验。年纪更小的朋友聊天充满新潮想法；年  
长的朋友凭借更丰富人生阅历，可以给到成熟人生建议。两类友谊都有价值。  
和不同年龄的人交朋友，可以极大拓宽个人视野。

## PDF第214页

Q6. Has technology changed people's friendships? How?  
Answer：  
Modern technology transforms friendships in multiple ways. It enables  
us to stay connected with friends far away easily. Yet many people  
devote excessive time to online contacts while ignoring people  
physically around them. People can make new friends online, but virtual  
friendships may be shallow compared with long‑term real‑world bonds.  
中文翻译：  
现代科技从多方面改变友谊。让我们轻松和远方朋友维持联系。但很多人花过  
多时间线上社交，却忽视身边现实的人。人们可以网上结识新朋友，但虚拟友  
谊相比长久现实情谊，有可能更加浅薄。

extremely hard on science‑related subjects, especially biology and

Part3  
Q1. Do you think being a doctor is easy or difficult?  
Answer：

## PDF第217页

Being a doctor is undoubtedly an extremely challenging profession.  
Future medical workers must complete long‑term higher education,  
including difficult theoretical courses and countless practical  
internships. After formal employment, they face heavy workloads,  
irregular shifts and high‑pressure responsibilities for patients’ lives.  
Although the job brings social respect, the mental and physical burdens  
should never be underestimated. Only people with strong willpower can  
stick to this career path.  
中文翻译：  
毫无疑问，医生是一份极具挑战性的职业。未来的医务工作者必须完成长期高  
等教育，包括艰深理论课程以及无数实操实习。正式入职之后，他们面对繁重  
工作、不规律轮班，还要承担关乎患者生命的高压责任。虽然这份职业拥有社  
会尊重，但身心负担绝不能被低估。只有意志力强大的人，才可以坚守这条职  
业道路。  
Q2. Do you think learning biology is interesting for children?  
Answer：  
Biology can be highly fascinating for children if it is taught in proper

## PDF第218页

ways. Observing plants, small animals and living creatures satisfies  
kids’ natural curiosity about nature. However, abstract biological  
terminology and complicated textbook theories may feel boring for  
younger learners. Hands‑on experiments and outdoor observation  
activities make biology much more attractive for children.  
中文翻译：  
如果教学方式得当，生物学对孩子来说可以十分有趣。观察植物、小动物以及  
各类生物，可以满足孩子与生俱来对大自然的好奇心。但抽象生物术语、复杂  
课本理论，对低龄学习者会显得枯燥。动手实验和户外观察活动，会让生物这  
门学科对孩子吸引力大增。  
Q3. Why do some children want to become doctors?  
Answer：  
Children may wish to become doctors for several different reasons.  
Some are inspired by real‑life experiences, such as seeing doctors help  
sick family members. Others are influenced by cartoons or stories that  
praise medical workers. A small number of kids also know that doctors  
enjoy high social status. Of course, many childhood dreams will change

## PDF第219页

greatly as they grow older and gain more life experience.  
中文翻译：  
小孩子想当医生有各种各样的原因。一部分孩子受现实经历启发，比如看到医  
生救治生病的家人。另一部分受到歌颂医务工作者的动画、故事影响。少数孩  
子也知道医生拥有很高社会地位。当然，很多儿时梦想，会随着年龄增长，人  
生阅历增加发生巨大改变。  
Q4. Do you think governments should put a large amount of money  
into medical research?  
Answer：  
I believe governments ought to invest substantial funds in medical  
research. Advanced medical studies help develop new medicines and  
treatment methods, curing previously incurable diseases. Good medical  
research improves public health conditions for the whole society. Even  
though research requires huge financial input, the long‑term returns for  
citizens’ wellbeing are priceless.

## PDF第220页

中文翻译：  
我认为政府应当为医学研究投入大量资金。先进医学研究助力研发新药与新疗  
法，治愈过去无法医治的疾病。优质医学研究改善全社会公共健康水平。尽管  
科研需要巨额经费投入，但对于民众福祉的长期回报是无价的。  
Q5. Why is some doctors’ pay high and others’ low?  
Answer：  
Doctors’ income gaps come from multiple factors. Senior specialists  
with rich clinical experience and rare professional skills receive higher  
salaries. By comparison, newly‑graduated junior doctors still  
accumulating experience earn relatively less. Working location, hospital  
rank and departments also create salary differences. Complex high‑risk  
departments generally offer better compensation.  
中文翻译：  
医生收入差距来自多重因素。拥有丰富临床经验、稀缺专业能力的资深专家薪  
资更高。与之相比，刚毕业还在积累经验的年轻医生收入相对更低。工作地  
点、医院等级、科室同样造成薪资差异。复杂高风险科室整体待遇通常更好。

## PDF第221页

Q6. Do you think doctors should be paid more?  
Answer：  
In general, doctors deserve reasonable and competitive payment. Their  
work carries huge mental pressure and life‑and‑death responsibilities,  
and they spend decades on professional education. That being said, pay  
should also keep balanced within the whole social income system.  
Excessively high salaries may also bring new social problems. So  
reasonable compensation rather than blindly raising wages is the key.  
中文翻译：  
总体来看，医生应当获得合理且有竞争力的薪酬。他们的工作背负巨大精神压  
力，肩负生死相关责任，还要花费数十年接受专业教育。虽说如此，薪资也要  
在整个社会收入体系内保持平衡。薪酬过高同样会带来新的社会问题。所以关  
键是合理待遇，而非盲目涨薪。

Q1. Why do some people start their own business?  
Answer：  
People launch their own businesses for mixed reasons. Some hope to  
gain higher income than working as employees. Others long for greater  
freedom, wanting to make their own work decisions instead of following  
orders from superiors. A number of entrepreneurs also wish to turn  
their personal hobbies into a full‑time career. Nevertheless, starting a  
business also brings huge risks and uncertainties.  
中文翻译：

望把个人爱好变成全职事业。尽管如此，创业也伴随着巨大风险和不确定性。  
Q2. Should governments provide financial support to start‑ups?  
Answer：  
Appropriate financial support from governments can benefit new

## PDF第225页

start‑ups, yet support should be carefully managed. Funds can reduce  
the pressure for promising young enterprises, especially innovative  
tech‑oriented projects. However, blind subsidies may cause waste of  
public money. Governments need strict review mechanisms to select  
qualified projects and prevent misuse of public resources.  
中文翻译：

金浪费。政府需要严格审核机制筛选合格项目，避免公共资源被滥用。  
Q3. Do most people prefer shopping at big stores or small stores?  
Answer：  
Consumer preferences vary according to different shopping purposes.  
For daily groceries and standard mass‑produced goods, many  
customers favour large supermarkets and chain stores for low prices  
and rich selections. When people look for special handmade or unique  
goods, small independent shops become more attractive. Both  
large‑scale and small businesses occupy important places in the market.

候，很多顾客偏爱大型超市连锁店，价格低选择多。人们寻找特殊手工、独一  
无二商品时，独立小店会更有吸引力。大型商户和小商户，在市场当中都占据  
重要位置。  
Q4. What makes a business successful?  
Answer：  
Multiple key elements determine whether a business can succeed.  
High‑quality products or services are the fundamental foundation.  
Besides, reasonable pricing strategies, good customer service and  
effective marketing all play important roles. Moreover, business  
operators need to react flexibly to changing market demands. Luck may  
offer temporary advantages, but long‑term success relies on  
comprehensive solid strengths.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第227页

多重关键要素决定一门生意能否成功。高质量的产品或者服务是根本基础。除  
此之外合理定价策略、优质客服、有效营销都十分重要。经营者还要灵活应对  
不断变化的市场需求。运气或许带来短期优势，但长久成功依靠全面过硬的综  
合实力。  
Q5. What makes a business fail?  
Answer：  
Business failures arise from various causes. Poor product quality,  
unreasonable pricing or bad customer service can drive customers  
away. Some entrepreneurs ignore market needs and invest blindly  
without sufficient investigation. Cash‑flow shortages and fierce market  
competition also push many companies into failure. Running a business  
requires careful risk control at every stage.  
中文翻译：  
生意失败源于各式各样的原因。产品质量差、定价不合理、客服糟糕，都会流  
失顾客。部分创业者无视市场需求，调研不足就盲目投资。资金链断裂，激烈  
市场竞争，也会让很多企业走向失败。经营生意，每一个阶段都需要谨慎风险  
管控。

## PDF第228页

Q6. Is it easy to set up a new business in your country?  
Answer：  
Nowadays the administrative procedures for registering new companies  
have been greatly simplified in China. However, easy registration does  
not mean running a business is simple. New entrepreneurs still face  
fierce market competition, capital pressure and complex market risks.  
Starting a company has lower entry barriers, but surviving and  
developing in the market remains highly challenging.  
中文翻译：  
如今在中国注册新公司的行政流程已经被极大简化。但是注册手续简单不等于  
经营生意简单。新创业者依旧面对激烈市场竞争、资金压力，还有复杂市场风  
险。开办公司门槛变低，但在市场当中存活并且发展依旧充满挑战。

## PDF第229页

Describe a plan that you had to change recently  
You should say:  
‑ When this happened  
‑ What made you change the plan  
‑ What the new plan was  
And how you felt about the change  
Answer：  
The plan I had to change recently was my short‑term travel plan last  
summer vacation. I originally intended to take a week‑long trip to a  
coastal city with my best friend. We had already looked up tourist  
attractions, checked accommodation information and roughly worked  
out our daily travel schedule. Unfortunately, unexpected health  
problems occurred to my friend shortly before our planned departure.  
He felt physically weak and needed plenty of rest instead of travelling.  
Therefore, we had no choice but to cancel the original travel  
arrangement. We adjusted our plan and chose to stay in our home city.  
Instead of long‑distance travelling, we met up on weekends, visited local  
city parks, went to bookstores and tried different local restaurants. At

## PDF第230页

first, I felt a little disappointed because I had been looking forward to  
the seaside journey for a long time. But gradually I realised our  
alternative activities were also enjoyable. Even though our original travel  
dream was put off, we still had pleasant relaxed time together in our  
own city.  
中文翻译：  
我近期不得不更改的计划，是去年暑假我的短途旅行安排。我原本打算和我最  
好的朋友，去一座海滨城市玩一周。我们已经查好景点，确认住宿信息，大致  
制定好了每日行程。可就在计划出发前不久，我的朋友突发身体不适，身体虚  
弱，需要大量休养，不适合外出旅行。于是我们别无选择，只能取消原定出  
行。我们调整方案，选择留在本市。不再长途出游，改为周末见面，逛逛本地  
城市公园、书店，打卡各类本地餐馆。一开始我有些失落，因为我期待海边之  
旅已经很久。但慢慢我发现替代的活动同样很愉快。虽然原先的旅行梦想被推  
迟，我们依旧在本地度过轻松美好的相处时光。  
Part3  
Q1. Do people often change their plans?

## PDF第231页

Answer：  
In real‑life situations, people quite frequently have to adjust their plans.  
Unexpected events such as health troubles, bad weather or sudden  
work and study tasks may break original arrangements. Some  
individuals also change plans when they discover better alternatives.  
Even well‑prepared plans cannot completely avoid outside interruptions.  
So flexibility is quite important for modern‑day life.  
中文翻译：  
现实生活当中，人们经常不得不调整计划。健康问题、恶劣天气，或是突发的  
学习工作任务，这类意外事件会打乱原本安排。有些人发现更好选择的时候，  
同样会更改计划。就算准备充分的计划，也无法完全避开外界干扰。所以，灵  
活变通在现代生活中十分重要。  
Q2. Would you tell others if you change your plan?  
Answer：  
I believe it is polite and responsible to inform relevant people when you  
alter a shared plan. If other people have made preparations according  
to the old schedule, sudden changes without notice will cause

## PDF第232页

inconvenience for them. Of course, for totally personal private plans  
that influence nobody else, there is no need to inform other people. It  
all depends on whether other parties are involved in the original  
arrangement.  
中文翻译：  
我认为，当改动共同计划的时候，告知相关人员，既是礼貌也是责任。如果别  
人已经按照旧时间表做准备，不打招呼突然变动，会给对方带来麻烦。当然，  
如果是完全只关乎自己，不影响任何人的私人计划，就没必要告诉其他人。一  
切取决于原计划是否牵扯到其他当事人。  
Q3. Why do you think parents still make plans for their children  
nowadays?  
Answer：  
Parents keep making plans for children mainly out of concern. Young  
kids lack sufficient life experience to judge risks and make far‑sighted  
decisions. Parents hope to prevent children from making avoidable  
mistakes. Even so, as children grow older, they should gain more right  
to make their own choices. Excessive planning from parents may limit

## PDF第233页

young people’s independent development.  
中文翻译：  
父母依旧为孩子做计划，主要出于关心。年纪小的孩子缺少足够人生阅历，无  
法判断风险，做出有远见的决定。父母希望避免孩子犯下本可以规避的错误。  
即便如此，随着孩子长大，应当获得更多自主选择权。父母过度规划，有可能  
限制年轻人独立发展。  
Q4. How does technology help people make plans?  
Answer：  
Modern technology offers great convenience for people making plans.  
Calendar applications can record schedules and send reminder  
notifications. Online platforms help people book transport, hotels and  
tickets quickly. Search engines supply large amounts of reference  
information for travel or study plans. Nevertheless, technology cannot  
predict unexpected accidents, so it cannot guarantee that plans will go  
perfectly.

## PDF第234页

中文翻译：  
现代科技给人们做计划带来巨大便利。日历软件记录日程，推送提醒通知。线  
上平台快速预订交通、酒店、门票。搜索引擎为旅行、学习计划提供海量参考  
资料。但科技无法预测突发意外，因此不能保证计划万无一失。  
Q5. What kind of plans do people often make?  
Answer：  
People create various kinds of plans in daily life. Some are short‑term  
small‑scale plans for travel, study or weekend activities. Many also make  
long‑term plans about careers, further education, savings or family  
development. Short‑term plans focus on concrete daily actions, while  
long‑term ones set general directions for future life. Both are common  
among ordinary people.  
中文翻译：  
人们日常生活会制定各式各样的计划。一部分是旅行、学习、周末活动这类短  
期小计划。很多人也会针对职业、深造、储蓄、家庭发展制定长期规划。短期  
计划聚焦具体日常行动，长期计划为未来人生设定大方向。两类计划在普通人  
当中都十分常见。

## PDF第235页

Q6. Do you think people like the process of making plans more, or the  
moment of carrying them out?  
Answer：  
Different individuals have different preferences. Some people enjoy  
imagining the future and organising every detail during the planning  
stage. They gain satisfaction just from designing a perfect blueprint.  
Others feel bored by planning and only get fulfilment when they  
actually put things into practice. Neither attitude is absolutely right;  
personality determines people’s preference for these two stages.  
中文翻译：  
不同的人有不同偏好。一部分人享受规划阶段畅想未来、梳理每一处细节的过  
程，光是设计完美蓝图就可以获得满足。另一部分人对规划本身感到乏味，只  
有真正落地执行才会获得成就感。两种态度没有绝对对错，个人性格决定人们  
更偏爱哪一个阶段。

The group‑work experience I want to describe is a group research

team member took different responsibilities. Some collected  
questionnaire data, some sorted out statistics, and I was responsible for  
writing parts of the final report. We met several times to exchange  
progress. However, we also ran into obvious difficulties. At the

## PDF第237页

beginning, our group held different ideas about survey methods. Some

both approaches. We also had to deal with tight deadlines. Working in a  
group was necessary for this task, because the workload was too heavy  
for one single student. By dividing tasks and combining each person’s  
strengths, we finished the whole project efficiently and handed in a  
satisfying group report.

理统计资料，我负责撰写部分最终报告。我们多次碰面交换进度。但我们也遇  
到明显困难。一开始，小组内部对于调研方法意见分歧。一部分组员支持线上  
问卷，另一部分坚持街头面对面访谈。我们耐心讨论，把两种方式结合起来。  
同时我们还要应对紧张截止日期。这项任务必须团队协作，因为工作量太大，  
单凭一个学生无法完成。通过分工，结合每个人长处，我们高效做完整个项  
目，交出一份令人满意的小组报告。  
Part3

## PDF第238页

Q1. Why do some people prefer to work by themselves?  
Answer：  
Quite a number of people favour independent work. When working  
alone, they can make all decisions freely without negotiating with other  
teammates. They will not be affected by partners’ slow progress or  
different work styles. For individual‑oriented tasks, solo work is more  
time‑efficient. Even so, self‑work also means you cannot receive timely  
help when you meet tough obstacles.  
中文翻译：  
不少人更喜欢独自工作。独自工作时，可以自由做全部决定，不用和队友反复  
协商。也不会被搭档拖沓进度或者不同工作风格影响。适合个人完成的任务，  
单干效率更高。即便如此，独自工作意味着遇到棘手难题的时候，无法得到及  
时帮助。  
Q2. What should a leader do to make team members want to follow  
him or her?

## PDF第239页

Answer：  
A good team leader needs multiple qualities. He or she should set clear  
goals, assign proper tasks according to each member’s strengths.  
Besides giving instructions, a leader also ought to listen to  
team‑members’ suggestions patiently, give fair recognition for hard  
work and take responsibility for setbacks. Authoritarian orders will make  
team members resist. Respect and fair treatment win real loyalty from  
the group.  
中文翻译：  
优秀团队领导者需要多重特质。要设立清晰目标，根据每个人长处分配合适任  
务。除下达指令之外，领导者还应当耐心倾听组员建议，对大家的付出给予公  
正肯定，遇到挫折主动承担责任。专断的命令只会引发组员抵触。尊重与公平  
对待，才会赢得团队真正的信服。  
Q3. Should students learn to do group work?  
Answer：

## PDF第240页

Group‑work practice is very valuable for school‑age students. Real‑world  
jobs frequently require teamwork competence. Group projects offer  
students chances to practise communication, negotiation and  
task‑sharing skills. Of course, teachers need to supervise to prevent  
some students from avoiding duties. When organised properly, group  
tasks bring huge benefits for teenagers’ development.  
中文翻译：  
小组协作练习对于在校学生十分宝贵。现实工作当中，经常需要团队合作能  
力。小组项目让学生练习沟通、协商、分工的本领。当然老师需要做好监督，  
防止部分学生逃避责任。组织得当的前提下，团队任务对青少年成长大有裨  
益。  
Q4. What group tasks are there in schools?  
Answer：  
Schools organise various forms of group tasks. These include group  
coursework projects, class drama performances, sports team  
competitions and voluntary community service activities. Students need  
to cooperate to finish shared goals. These activities exist in different

## PDF第241页

subjects, ranging from liberal‑arts research to science experiments. They  
give young people abundant chances to practise teamwork.  
中文翻译：  
学校会组织各式各样的团队任务。包含课程小组课题、班级戏剧表演、体育团  
体赛事，还有社区志愿活动。学生需要互相协作完成共同目标。这类活动遍布  
不同科目，文科调研、理科实验都有。它们给到年轻人大量练习团队协作的机  
会。  
Q5. What advantages are there for students experiencing teamwork at  
school?  
Answer：  
Teamwork at school brings many long‑term benefits for students. Apart  
from finishing assignments, youngsters learn to respect different  
viewpoints, solve conflicts and distribute work reasonably. These soft  
skills cannot be fully gained from individual textbook study. Such  
accumulated experience will prepare them better for future university  
study and career life.

## PDF第242页

中文翻译：  
学校里的团队协作给学生带来很多长远益处。除完成作业之外，年轻人学会尊  
重不同观点、化解矛盾、合理分配工作。这些软实力，靠独自啃课本无法完全  
习得。这些积累的经验，让他们更好地为未来大学学习以及职场生活做好准  
备。  
Q6. How can you tell if a person is a good leader?  
Answer：  
We can judge a good leader not only by final results but also by how he  
or she treats team‑members. Good leaders inspire others instead of  
simply ordering them around. They listen to diverse opinions and take  
responsibility for failures instead of shifting blame to subordinates. A  
capable leader helps the whole group progress together rather than  
only showing off personal excellence.

凸显个人优秀。

## PDF第245页

Q1. Do you think children sometimes have to make important  
decisions?  
Answer：  
Young children rarely need to make truly life‑changing important  
decisions. They lack adequate life experience to judge long‑term  
consequences. Even so, teenagers gradually need to take charge of  
small but meaningful choices, such as picking extra‑curricular subjects.  
Adults should offer guidance instead of making every single choice for  
them.  
中文翻译：  
低龄儿童几乎不需要做真正改变人生的重大决定。他们缺少足够人生阅历，无  
法判断长远后果。但青少年慢慢需要自己做一些有意义的小抉择，例如选择课  
外科目。成年人应当给予引导，而不是替他们包办每一个选择。  
Q2. What important decisions do teenagers need to make after  
graduation?

## PDF第246页

Answer：  
After graduating from high school, teenagers face several vital  
decisions. They have to choose their university major, decide which city  
to study in, and consider whether to go for further education or step  
directly into the job market. These choices exert huge influence over  
their next several years of life. Therefore many young people seek  
advice from parents and teachers before final choices.  
中文翻译：  
高中毕业之后，青少年要面对好几项关键抉择。选择大学专业，决定求学城  
市，思考是继续深造还是直接就业。这些选择对他们之后数年人生影响巨大。  
所以很多年轻人做最终决定前，会向父母老师寻求建议。  
Q3. Who can children turn to for help when making a decision?  
Answer：  
When children face hard decisions, they can seek advice from several  
reliable sources. Parents provide life‑experience‑based suggestions.

## PDF第247页

Teachers give advice from the perspective of education and personal  
growth. Close friends of similar age can offer peer viewpoints. Still,  
other people’s advice can only serve as reference; the final choice  
belongs to the young person himself or herself.  
中文翻译：  
孩子难以做决定的时候，可以向几个可靠对象求助。父母给出基于人生阅历的  
建议；老师从教育和个人成长角度提供参考；同龄好友带来同辈视角。但他人  
建议只能当作参考，最终选择权属于年轻人自己。  
Q4. Do you think advertisements can influence our decisions when  
shopping?  
Answer：  
Advertisements exert obvious influence over people’s shopping  
decisions. Well‑designed ads create desire for products by building  
attractive images. Consumers may be persuaded to buy goods that they  
do not actually need. Even rational shoppers are unconsciously affected  
by repeated advertising information. So we need to stay critical while  
watching commercial content.

## PDF第248页

中文翻译：  
广告对人们购物决定会产生明显影响。精心设计的广告，通过打造诱人的产品  
形象勾起消费欲望。消费者有可能被说服，买下自己实际上并不需要的商品。  
就算理性消费者，也会被反复推送的广告信息潜移默化影响。所以面对商业内  
容，我们要保持批判性思维。  
Q5. Do you think the influence of advertising is good?  
Answer：  
Advertising has both positive and negative sides. On the bright side,  
advertisements deliver useful product information for consumers. On  
the negative side, excessive advertising stimulates blind consumption  
and creates unrealistic expectations. Whether its influence is good or  
bad depends on content and audiences’ own judgement. People  
should not accept all advertising claims without thinking.  
中文翻译：  
广告同时存在积极和消极两面。好的一面，广告向消费者传递有用产品信息。

## PDF第249页

消极的一面，过度广告刺激盲目消费，制造不切实际的期待。广告影响好坏，  
取决于内容以及受众自身判断力。人们不应该不加思考全盘接受广告说辞。  
Q6. How do people usually make important decisions?  
Answer：  
Different people adopt different ways when handling important  
decisions. Some collect large amounts of information and analyse pros  
and cons carefully. Some consult family, friends and experienced people  
for suggestions. A small number of people rely on intuition. In most  
cases, combining information collection plus advice from trusted people  
produces the most reasonable final outcome.  
中文翻译：

一部分咨询家人朋友、有经验的人获取建议；少数人依靠直觉。大多数情况  
下，搜集信息结合可信之人的建议，可以得出最合理的最终结果。

Describe a live sports event you watched and liked  
You should say:  
‑ What it was

The live sports event I really enjoyed watching was a university  
table‑tennis tournament. I watched this match in our school’s indoor  
sports hall last spring, together with many of my classmates. The  
competition was between our university team and another local college.  
A number of skilled players took part in singles and doubles matches.  
The atmosphere inside the gymnasium was extremely energetic.  
Audiences cheered loudly whenever players scored valuable points. I sat  
among the crowd, shouting and clapping alongside other spectators.

The players showed wonderful skills, fast ball returns and smart tactical  
adjustments during the fierce competition. Even when one side fell  
behind in scores, the athletes never gave up easily. What impressed me

校。不少实力很强的选手参加单打和双打项目。体育馆里面气氛格外热烈。选  
手拿下关键分数的时候，观众就大声欢呼。我坐在人群当中，和其他观众一起  
呐喊鼓掌。激烈的对抗当中，选手展现高超球技、快速回球，还有巧妙战术调  
整。就算一方比分落后，运动员也不会轻易放弃。让我印象最深的不光是精彩  
球技，还有选手身上的拼搏精神。现场观赛和网上看录播完全不一样。集体高  
涨的情绪感染在场每一个人，这也是这场现场体育赛事给我留下极好深刻印象  
的主要原因。

Describe a food that people eat on special occasions/events

## PDF第257页

You should say:

And explain why people eat it on that special occasion/event  
Answer：  
The special‑occasion food I want to talk about is mooncake, which is  
traditionally eaten during the Chinese Mid‑Autumn Festival. This  
traditional festival falls on the 15th day of the eighth lunar month.  
Families gather together, admire the bright full moon and share  
mooncakes. Mooncakes are round baked pastries with thick sweet  
fillings inside. The production process is relatively complex.  
Pastry‑makers first mix flour, oil and syrup to make the outer crust. Then  
they prepare fillings such as lotus‑seed paste or red‑bean paste.  
Workers wrap fillings inside the crust and press them into beautiful  
round moulds before baking them in ovens. The round shape of  
mooncakes carries symbolic cultural meaning. Roundness represents  
reunion. On Mid‑Autumn night, family members get together and taste  
moon‑cakes while appreciating the moon. Even family members living

## PDF第258页

far away will send mooncakes as gifts. This food connects people with  
the tradition of family reunion, which explains why mooncakes are  
essential for this special festival.  
中文翻译：  
我想聊的节日特色食物是月饼，是中国中秋节的传统吃食。这个传统节日在农  
历八月十五。家家户户团聚，共赏明月，分食月饼。月饼是圆形烘烤点心，内  
部是香甜厚实馅料。制作流程相对复杂。糕点师傅首先混合面粉、油、糖浆制  
作饼皮，之后准备莲蓉、红豆沙这类馅料。把馅料包进饼皮，压入精美的圆形  
模具，再送入烤箱烘烤。月饼圆圆的外形拥有文化象征寓意，圆形代表团圆。  
中秋夜晚，家人团聚，一边赏月一边品尝月饼。就算相隔远方的家人，也会互  
赠月饼作为礼物。这种食物承载阖家团圆的传统，这就是为什么月饼在这个特  
殊节日必不可少。  
Part3  
Q1. Why are there special foods on special occasions or events?  
Answer：

## PDF第259页

Special festival‑related food carries rich symbolic cultural meanings.  
Certain dishes represent good fortune, reunion or best wishes. Eating  
these specific foods becomes an important part of celebrating festivals.  
Passing down these food customs also helps hand traditional culture  
from older generations down to young people. Food and festival culture  
cannot be easily separated.  
中文翻译：  
节日特色食物承载丰富的象征文化内涵。特定菜肴代表吉祥、团圆，或是美好  
祝福。食用这些特定食物，成为过节重要一环。传承这些饮食习俗，也帮助把  
传统文化从老一辈传递给年轻人。食物和节日文化很难分割。  
Q2. What are the differences between everyday food and festival food?  
Answer：  
Daily‑life food mainly focuses on practical nutrition and  
cost‑effectiveness. By contrast, festival food puts more weight on  
symbolic cultural meanings. Festival dishes are often more complicated  
to prepare, and people do not eat them on ordinary days. Even though  
some festival food tastes delicious, it may contain higher sugar or fat

## PDF第260页

content than regular daily meals.  
中文翻译：  
日常饮食主要看重实用营养和性价比。与之对比，节日食物更加看重文化象征  
寓意。节日食物制作流程往往更复杂，平时很少吃到。即便部分节日美食味道  
很好，相比普通家常菜，糖或者脂肪含量可能更高。  
Q3. Are there any differences between the food people eat today and  
the food people ate in the past?  
Answer：  
Great changes have taken place in people’s diets. In the past, food  
choices were limited by transport and storage conditions. Many kinds of  
food could only be enjoyed in certain seasons. Nowadays convenient  
logistics and modern food‑processing technology make diverse  
ingredients available all year round. People also pay more attention to  
balanced nutrition rather than merely filling their stomachs.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第261页

人们的饮食发生巨大变化。过去，食物选择受运输、储存条件限制，很多食材  
只能特定季节吃到。如今便捷物流、现代食品加工技术，各类食材一年四季都  
可以买到。现代人也更加看重均衡营养，而不只是填饱肚子。  
Q4. Do people today prefer eating at home or in a restaurant?  
Answer：  
People’s choices depend on different situations. For ordinary daily  
meals, many people prefer home‑cooked food for health and budget  
reasons. For festivals, celebrations or social gatherings with friends,  
restaurants become the more popular option. Both home cooking and  
eating out occupy important positions in modern‑day people’s lives.  
中文翻译：  
人们的选择视场景而定。普通一日三餐，出于健康、预算考量，很多人更喜欢  
家常菜。过节庆祝，或是和朋友社交聚会，大家更愿意下馆子。在家做饭和外  
出就餐，在现代人生活当中都占据重要地位。  
Part2 保留题 15 学到重要东西的经历

## PDF第262页

Describe an experience when you learned something important  
You should say:

‑ Where it happened  
‑ What you learned  
And explain why you think it was important  
Answer：  
The meaningful learning experience I want to talk about took place  
during a volunteer activity I joined two years ago. I took part in a  
community volunteer programme in my neighbourhood on a weekend,  
helping elderly residents who lived alone with their daily‑life issues.  
Before this activity, I barely thought much about the difficulties that  
senior citizens might face in modern society. During the activity, I talked  
with several elderly people and assisted them with simple digital‑device  
operations. Many of them struggled with smartphone functions, online  
payments and health‑code systems. Gradually, I realised that advanced  
digital convenience for young people could turn into huge barriers for  
the old. I learned that technological progress does not automatically

## PDF第263页

bring benefits to every group in society. We need to show more  
patience and care for vulnerable groups. This lesson was really  
important to me. It broadened my social awareness and taught me not  
to judge the world only from my own perspective. It encouraged me to  
pay more attention to people around me who might be left behind by  
social changes.  
中文翻译：  
我想讲述的一次学到重要道理的经历，发生在两年前我参加的一次志愿活动  
中。某个周末，我参加社区志愿项目，在小区帮助独居老人处理生活难题。参  
加活动之前，我几乎没有认真思考过老年人在现代社会会面临哪些困境。活动  
期间，我和几位老人聊天，协助他们操作简单的电子设备。很多老人搞不懂智  
能手机功能、线上支付以及健康码系统。我慢慢意识到，年轻人享受的数字化  
便利，对老年人来说却可能变成巨大障碍。我学到，科技进步并不会自动惠及  
社会每一个群体。我们需要对弱势群体给予更多耐心与关怀。这次经历对我十  
分重要，拓宽了我的社会认知，教会我不要只用自己的视角去看待世界，也促  
使我更多关注那些被社会发展落下的人。

## PDF第264页

Q1. What skills should young people learn?  
Answer：  
Young people ought to master a mixture of practical hard skills and  
essential soft skills. Professional knowledge related to their future career  
lays the foundation for work competence. Besides that, communication  
skills, time‑management abilities and critical‑thinking mindsets are  
equally valuable. These soft skills cannot be acquired only from  
textbooks. Real‑life practice and constant reflection help young people  
grow into well‑rounded individuals.  
中文翻译：  
年轻人应当兼顾实用硬技能和重要软实力。和未来职业相关的专业知识奠定工  
作能力的基础。除此之外，沟通能力、时间管理、批判性思维同样十分宝贵。  
这些软实力无法仅仅依靠课本习得。现实实践加上不断反思，帮助年轻人成长  
为全面发展的人。  
Q2. Which is more important for young people, practical skills or  
theoretical knowledge?

## PDF第265页

Answer：  
Theoretical knowledge and practical skills are both indispensable for  
young learners. Solid theories offer underlying principles that guide  
real‑world practice. Without theory, practical operations may become  
blind and shallow. On the other hand, pure book‑learning without  
practice cannot solve real‑life problems. Therefore, education should  
combine both aspects and help students connect knowledge with  
real‑world situations.  
中文翻译：  
理论知识和实操技能对于年轻学习者来说缺一不可。扎实的理论提供指导实践  
的底层原理。缺少理论，实操会变得盲目肤浅。反过来，只啃书本不去实践，  
也解决不了现实问题。所以教育应当把二者结合，帮助学生把知识和现实场景  
联系起来。  
Q3. What is the best way to teach young people?  
Answer：  
There is no single perfect teaching method for all young people.  
Generally speaking, combining knowledge input with hands‑on practice

## PDF第266页

produces good results. Teachers should avoid one‑way cramming and  
create more chances for interaction and independent thinking. In  
addition, educators need to respect individual differences among  
learners. Adjusting teaching styles according to students’ personalities  
helps achieve better learning effects.  
中文翻译：  
不存在一套适用于所有年轻人的完美教学方式。大体来看，知识输入搭配动手  
实践效果很好。老师要避免单向填鸭式灌输，创造更多互动和独立思考的机  
会。此外教育者要尊重学习者个体差异，根据学生性格调整授课方式，以此取  
得更好的学习效果。  
Q4. Can people learn important things from friends?  
Answer：  
Absolutely, friends serve as an important source of learning. People  
observe their friends’ behaviours, attitudes and problem‑solving  
methods in daily communication. They can pick up useful life lessons  
without formal classroom teaching. However, friends may also pass on  
bad habits and wrong viewpoints. So individuals need to keep critical

## PDF第267页

awareness when absorbing ideas from peers.  
中文翻译：  
当然，朋友是重要的学习来源。日常相处中，人们观察朋友的行为、处事态度  
以及处理问题的方式，不用经过正式课堂，就可以学到宝贵人生经验。但朋友  
同样也会传递坏习惯和错误观点。所以从同伴身上汲取信息的时候，人需要保  
持批判性意识。  
Q5. Why are some people unwilling to learn new things?  
Answer：  
Several factors make people resist learning new knowledge. Some fear  
making mistakes and feeling incompetent when facing unfamiliar  
content. Others get stuck in their comfortable old routines and see no  
urgent need for change. Age can also play a role; some middle‑aged  
and elderly people lack confidence in learning fresh skills. Breaking old  
habits requires great mental effort, which many people want to avoid.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第268页

多重因素造成有些人不愿意学习新事物。一部分人面对陌生内容害怕犯错，害  
怕显露自己能力不足。另一部分人沉溺旧的舒适习惯，感觉没有迫切改变的必  
要。年龄也会产生影响，部分中老年人对学习新技能缺乏自信。打破旧习惯需  
要耗费巨大精神精力，很多人想要逃避这份消耗。  
Q6. What can schools do to encourage students to learn?  
Answer：  
Schools can take multiple measures to motivate students. Teachers  
ought to link textbook content to real‑life cases to increase subject  
attractiveness. Offering proper positive feedback and reasonable  
encouragement helps build students’ confidence. Besides, diverse  
practical activities can arouse curiosity. Over‑emphasis on exam scores  
alone will kill students’ inner desire for knowledge.  
中文翻译：  
学校可以采取多种方式激励学生学习。教师把课本内容和现实案例结合，提升  
学科吸引力。给予恰当正向反馈与鼓励，帮助学生建立自信。此外丰富的实践  
活动可以激发好奇心。一味过度看重考试分数，反而会磨灭学生内在求知欲。

The useful piece of advice I would like to talk about came from my  
university tutor. I was in a difficult period during my first year at

keeping up with heavy academic tasks. I spread my energy too thin,  
resulting in average grades and constant tiredness. My tutor noticed my

powerful: do not try to excel at everything; learn to set priorities and

focus your limited energy on the most important targets. Following his  
suggestion, I quit several less‑meaningful activities. I allocated most of  
my time to core coursework and only kept one or two hobbies I truly

pressure dropped greatly. This advice was extremely useful. It helped  
me escape from the trap of chasing perfection in every single area and  
taught me the wisdom of making choices under limited resources.  
中文翻译：

散，成绩平平，还总是身心疲惫。导师注意到我紧绷的状态，耐心找我谈话。

级，把有限精力聚焦在最重要的目标上。听从他的建议之后，我退出好几项意

贵，帮我跳出事事追求完美的困境，教会我资源有限条件下做出取舍的智慧。

## PDF第271页

Q1. What kind of advice do young people often get?  
Answer：  
Young people frequently receive advice concerning study, career and  
personal relationships. Parents and teachers often remind teenagers to  
work hard on their schoolwork and plan for future employment. They  
also give suggestions on how to get along well with others. Some  
advice focuses on developing good living habits. However, not all  
advice fits every young person’s real‑life situation.  
中文翻译：  
年轻人经常收到关于学习、职业和人际交往方面的建议。父母老师常常提醒青
