议围绕养成良好生活习惯。但并不是每一条建议，都适配每个年轻人的现实处  
境。  
Q2. Should children follow the advice given by their parents?  
Answer：

## PDF第272页

Children should respect their parents’ advice, but they do not need to  
obey every single suggestion blindly. Parents offer guidance based on  
their life experience, yet their viewpoints may not suit the modern  
world. Teenagers ought to think independently, absorb reasonable parts  
and reject impractical ideas. Mutual communication is better than  
complete obedience or total rejection.  
中文翻译：  
孩子应当尊重父母的建议，但不必盲目听从每一条意见。父母凭借人生阅历给  
出指引，但他们的观点未必适配现代社会。青少年要独立思考，吸纳合理部  
分，舍弃不切实际的想法。互相沟通远比完全顺从或者全盘否定要好。  
Q3. Why do many young people ignore advice from the elderly?  
Answer：  
A main reason lies in the generational gap. Older people’s suggestions  
are shaped by their own times, which may feel out‑of‑date for today’s  
youth. Besides, young people long for independence. They may resist  
advice simply because they dislike being told what to do. That does not  
mean all the older generation’s opinions are wrong, just that they

## PDF第273页

need proper communication to be accepted.  
中文翻译：  
主要原因来自代沟。老一辈的建议被他们所处的时代塑造，在如今年轻人看来  
可能已经过时。除此之外年轻人渴望独立，有时候抗拒建议，仅仅是反感被人  
说教。这不代表老一辈所有想法都是错的，只是需要合适沟通方式才能被接  
纳。  
Q4. Is it better to give advice directly or indirectly?  
Answer：  
Whether direct or indirect advice works better depends on specific  
contexts and personalities. For objective practical problems,  
straightforward advice is time‑saving and clear. But when dealing with  
people’s emotions and sensitive personal issues, indirect gentle hints  
prevent hurting others’ self‑esteem. People should adjust their way of  
giving suggestions according to the listener’s state.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第274页

直接提建议还是委婉暗示，取决于具体场景以及对方性格。客观现实问题，直  
截了当的建议清晰高效。但处理情绪、敏感私人问题时，委婉间接的方式可以  
避免伤害对方自尊。人们要根据接收建议者的状态调整表达方式。  
Q5. Who do people turn to when they need advice?  
Answer：  
When people are in need of suggestions, they choose different  
consultants. Many go to family members for life‑related guidance. Some  
seek opinions from experienced teachers or professional experts. Close  
friends are also a common choice, since they understand one’s real‑life  
situation well. The source of advice depends on what kind of problem  
they are facing.  
中文翻译：  
人们需要建议的时候，会求助不同对象。很多人找家人获取人生指引；一部分  
人咨询经验丰富的老师或者专业人士；亲密好友也是很常见选择，因为朋友很  
了解自己的真实处境。求助谁，取决于面临哪一类难题。

## PDF第275页

Q6. What qualities should people have to give good advice?  
Answer：  
Good advisors should possess several key qualities. They need to listen  
patiently and fully understand the other person’s real situation before  
offering opinions. They ought to be objective and avoid imposing their  
own personal preferences onto others. Good advice‑givers also realise  
their suggestions are only for reference, not absolute orders. Empathy  
and open‑mindedness are equally important.  
中文翻译：  
能给出好建议的人要具备几项关键特质。给出意见之前，要耐心倾听，充分理  
解对方真实处境。保持客观，不要把个人偏好强加给别人。优秀的提建议者明  
白自己的想法仅供参考，不是必须服从的命令。同理心与开放心态同样不可或  
缺。  
---  
✨Part2 保留题 17 安静地方

## PDF第276页

Describe a quiet place you like to spend your free time in  
You should say:  
‑ Where it is  
‑ How often you go there  
‑ What you do there  
And explain why you like this quiet place  
Answer：  
The quiet place I enjoy spending my spare time in is the reading corner  
on the top floor of our city public library. It is located in the downtown  
cultural zone, not far from the main business streets. I visit this spot  
about two or three times every month, mostly on weekend afternoons  
when I want to escape noisy city life. When I get there, I usually pick out  
one or two interesting books, sit by the large‑size window and read for  
several hours. Sometimes I bring my notebook and do light‑weight  
study tasks. Very few visitors come up to this particular floor, so  
conversations are extremely rare. People move softly and keep their  
voices down. I really appreciate this peaceful environment. Away from  
mobile‑phone notifications and street noise, I can calm my racing

## PDF第277页

thoughts. It allows me to slow down my fast‑paced daily rhythm. This  
quiet corner gives me mental relaxation and space for inner reflection,  
which is why it has become my favourite getaway in the city.  
中文翻译：  
我空闲时间喜欢待的安静地方，是市图书馆顶楼的阅览角。它坐落在市中心文  
化区，离主要商业街不远。我每个月大概去两到三次，大多选在周末下午，想  
要逃离城市喧嚣的时候。到那里之后，我通常挑选一两本感兴趣的书，坐在大  
窗边读上好几个小时。有时候我带上笔记本做轻松的学习任务。很少有访客来  
到这一层，几乎听不到交谈声。人们轻轻走动，压低声音。我十分喜爱这份安  
宁。远离手机消息提醒和街道噪音，我可以平复纷乱思绪，放缓快节奏的日  
常。这个安静角落给我精神放松和内心自省的空间，所以它成了我在城市里最  
喜欢的一处避风处。  
Part3  
Q1. Why do some people prefer quiet environments?  
Answer：

## PDF第278页

Many people favour quiet surroundings for mental reasons. Peaceful  
environments reduce outside distractions, helping people concentrate  
on reading, thinking or creative work. Besides, noise creates mental  
tiredness. Quiet spaces offer opportunities to release accumulated  
pressure. Of course, personality makes a difference. Introverted  
individuals generally gain more comfort from silence than outgoing  
people.  
中文翻译：  
很多人偏爱安静环境，出于精神层面的原因。安宁的环境减少外界干扰，帮助  
人们专注阅读、思考或者创造性工作。而且噪音会造成精神疲惫，安静空间提  
供释放积攒压力的机会。当然性格也会起作用，内向的人相比外向者，通常从  
宁静当中获得更多舒适感。  
Q2. What places in cities are usually noisy?  
Answer：  
Cities contain multiple sources of loud noise. Busy main roads, large  
shopping malls, night markets and crowded tourist spots are typically  
very noisy. Public transport hubs such as train stations also produce

## PDF第279页

constant loud sounds. Large‑scale construction sites add further noise  
pollution. Residential communities can also become noisy during peak  
hours or when people hold group gatherings.  
中文翻译：  
城市里有很多产生巨大噪音的场所。繁忙主干道、大型商场、夜市、拥挤旅游  
景点通常十分嘈杂。火车站这类交通枢纽也持续产生巨大声响。大型建筑工地  
进一步加剧噪音污染。居民小区在高峰时段或者举办集体聚会的时候，同样会  
变得喧闹。  
Q3. Is it hard to find quiet places in big cities?  
Answer：  
It can be challenging to seek out quiet spots in large metropolises.  
Population density and heavy human activity generate constant noise  
everywhere. Nevertheless, quiet locations still exist, such as library  
reading rooms, small‑scale community parks and some museum  
exhibition halls. People just need to make an effort to look for them.  
They may not be right around every street corner, but they are still  
accessible.

## PDF第280页

中文翻译：  
在大都市寻找安静地方会有一定难度。人口密度高，人类活动频繁，到处都会  
源源不断产生噪音。但安静场所依旧存在：图书馆阅览室、小型社区公园、部  
分博物馆展厅。人们只是需要花心思去找。虽然不会每条街角都有，但依然可  
以找到。  
Q4. Would you like to live in a very quiet place in the future?  
Answer：  
I would appreciate living in a relatively quiet area, but total absolute  
silence is not what I desire. If an environment is completely isolated and  
too silent, it may feel lonely and disconnected from society. A balanced  
location with low noise yet convenient access to public facilities suits  
me best. I want peace, but I also do not wish to be cut off from city life  
entirely.  
中文翻译：  
我会比较喜欢住在相对安静的地方，但并不追求绝对万籁俱寂。如果环境太过

## PDF第281页

与世隔绝、一片死寂，会让人感觉孤独，和社会脱节。低噪音，同时公共设施  
又很方便的平衡地点最适合我。我向往宁静，但也不希望彻底脱离城市生活。  
Q5. What are the disadvantages of living in noisy cities?  
Answer：  
Long‑term exposure to city noise brings many drawbacks. Constant  
noise disturbs sleep quality and makes it harder for people to focus on  
work or study. Psychologically, loud surroundings may increase feelings  
of anxiety and mental exhaustion. In the long run, persistent noise can  
even cause gradual damage to people’s hearing ability.  
中文翻译：  
长期生活在城市噪音环境下有很多弊端。持续噪音干扰睡眠质量，让人很难专  
注工作学习。心理层面，喧闹环境会加剧焦虑和精神耗竭。长期来看，不间断  
噪音甚至会慢慢损伤人的听力。  
Q6. Do you think people need some time of silence every day?

## PDF第282页

Answer：  
I believe most people benefit from a short period of silence every single  
day. In modern busy life, we are flooded with all kinds of sounds and  
information. A little quiet time allows our brains to rest and process our  
inner thoughts. It does not have to last for hours; even 15 or 20 minutes  
can bring noticeable mental relief.  
中文翻译：  
我认为大多数人每天拥有一小段安静时光是有益的。现代忙碌生活中，我们被  
各种各样声音、信息淹没。短暂安静可以让大脑休息，梳理内心想法。不需要  
长达数小时，就算十五到二十分钟，也能带来明显的精神舒缓。

✨Part2 保留题 18 收到的特别礼物  
Describe a special gift you received  
You should say:  
‑ What the gift was

## PDF第283页

‑ Who gave it to you  
‑ On what occasion you received it  
And explain why you think it was special  
Answer：  
The special gift I would like to talk about is a hand‑bound notebook  
given to me by my best friend on my eighteenth birthday. The  
notebook itself had simple but elegant cover design. Inside, my friend  
had filled many pages with hand‑written messages. She recalled our  
happy memories from high‑school days, wrote encouraging words for  
my future and added several small hand‑drawn sketches. Unlike  
mass‑produced commercial presents you can easily buy in shops, every  
page contained her genuine time and effort. Many other birthday gifts I  
got were practical consumer goods. This one carried unique emotional  
value. Whenever I open it, I can feel her sincere care and support. It  
reminds me of our precious friendship. Even now I keep it carefully  
stored on my bookshelf. For me, the true value of this gift lies not in its  
price, but in the heartfelt feelings behind it, which makes it so special  
among all my presents.

## PDF第284页

中文翻译：  
我想聊的一份特别礼物，是我十八岁生日时，好朋友送给我的一本手工装订笔  
记本。本子封面简约雅致。本子里面，我的朋友写满好多页手写文字。她回忆  
我们高中时期的快乐往事，写下对我未来的鼓励话语，还画上几幅小小的手绘  
简笔画。它和商店随处可买到的量产商品礼物不一样，每一页都倾注了她的时  
间与心血。我收到的其他生日礼物大多是实用消费品。而这份礼物承载独一无  
二的情感价值。每当翻开本子，我都能感受到她真挚的关心与支持，想起我们  
珍贵的友谊。直到现在我都把它小心存放在书架上。对我来说，这份礼物的真  
正价值不在于价格，而在于背后饱含的心意，这让它在所有礼物当中显得格外  
特别。  
Part3  
Q1. What kinds of gifts are popular in your country?  
Answer：  
In China, gift choices change according to different festivals and  
receivers. For family elders, health‑related products and high‑quality  
food are widely welcomed. For young friends, practical stationery,  
creative handicrafts or snacks are common picks. On important festivals,

## PDF第285页

gift boxes with tea, fruit or local special‑ties are also very popular.  
People tend to select presents matching the receiver’s age and  
personal preferences.  
中文翻译：  
在中国，礼物会随着节日和送礼对象发生变化。送给家中长辈，健康相关产  
品、高品质食品很受欢迎。送给年轻朋友，实用文具、创意手作、零食很常  
见。重要节日，茶叶、水果、地方特产礼盒也十分流行。人们倾向挑选符合收  
礼人年龄和个人喜好的礼物。  
Q2. Why do people send gifts?  
Answer：  
Gift‑giving serves multiple social functions. People present gifts to  
express gratitude, congratulations or best wishes on special occasions  
such as birthdays and festivals. Giving presents also helps maintain and  
strengthen relationships between family members, relatives and friends.  
It is a symbolic way to show you care about another person, far beyond  
the material value of the item itself.

## PDF第286页

中文翻译：  
送礼承担多重社会功能。生日、节日这类特殊场合，人们通过礼物表达感谢、  
祝贺以及美好祝愿。送礼也帮助维系巩固家人、亲戚、朋友之间的关系。送礼  
是表达关怀的象征性方式，意义远远超出礼物本身的物质价值。  
Q3. Is money a good gift?  
Answer：  
Giving money as a gift has both advantages and disadvantages. Cash  
allows receivers to buy exactly what they really want. However, pure  
monetary gifts sometimes lack personal emotional warmth. In Chinese  
traditional customs, red envelopes of money are widely accepted for  
festivals and weddings. Still, for close friends, physical gifts with  
personal touches are often more touching.  
中文翻译：  
给钱当礼物有利也有弊。现金让收礼人可以买到自己真正想要的东西。但单纯  
金钱礼物有时候缺少人情温度。在中国传统习俗中，节日婚礼红包是广为接受  
的形式。但对于亲密好友，带有个人心意的实物礼物往往更动人。

## PDF第287页

Q4. Should the value of a gift be expensive?  
Answer：  
The real meaning of a gift does not depend on how expensive it is.  
Thought‑out inexpensive presents with sincere feelings can be far more  
memorable than costly goods. Pursuing overly expensive gifts may even  
create economic pressure for both givers and receivers. What truly  
counts is genuine thought rather than high market price.  
中文翻译：  
礼物的真正意义并不取决于价格高低。用心准备、价格不贵却饱含诚意的礼  
物，会比贵重商品更加令人难忘。一味追求高价礼物，甚至会给送礼和收礼双  
方造成经济压力。真正重要的是那份心意，而不是高昂市价。  
Q5. What difficulties can people have when choosing gifts?  
Answer：  
People may meet several troubles while selecting presents. Sometimes

## PDF第288页

they know little about the receiver’s hobbies and real needs. Budget  
limits also create obstacles. Besides, worrying whether the gift will  
appear too cheap or overly extravagant causes hesitation. Without  
enough information about the person, it becomes hard to pick  
something suitable.  
中文翻译：  
挑选礼物的时候人们会遇到不少难题。有时候并不了解收礼人的爱好和真实需  
求，预算限制也是阻碍。另外还会纠结礼物会不会显得太廉价或者过于贵重。  
如果对对方了解不足，就很难挑到合适的礼品。  
Q6. Do you think online shopping changes gift‑giving?  
Answer：  
Online shopping greatly transforms gift‑giving customs. It offers  
abundant product choices and supports direct delivery to the  
receiver’s address, saving plenty of time. However, many people just  
select generic popular goods, so gifts become less distinctive.  
Convenience increases, yet some personal touch may get lost in the  
process.

## PDF第289页

中文翻译：  
网购极大改变了送礼的习惯。它提供海量商品选择，还可以直接寄到收礼人手  
中，节省大量时间。但很多人直接选爆款通用商品，礼物变得缺少特色。便利  
度提升的同时，一部分个人心意也随之淡化。

✨Part2 保留题 19 想要学习的外语  
Describe a foreign language you want to learn (not English)  
You should say:  
‑ What language it is  
‑ How you would learn it  
‑ Why you want to learn this language  
And explain how you think you would feel after learning it  
Answer：

## PDF第290页

The foreign language besides English that I really hope to learn is  
Japanese. I plan to combine multiple learning resources to study it step  
by step. I will take online beginner‑level courses to master basic  
grammar and pronunciation. In my spare time, I intend to watch short  
videos and TV shows with bilingual subtitles to get used to real‑life daily  
conversations. I also plan to memorise useful vocabulary on a daily  
basis. I have several main reasons for picking this language. I am deeply  
interested in its unique pop culture, including animation and  
documentaries. Knowing the language will let me enjoy original content  
without relying on translations. Besides, it could create potential  
opportunities for future travel or cross‑cultural communication. I  
imagine that after I gain basic proficiency, I will feel a strong sense of  
achievement. I will be able to directly understand different cultural ideas  
and broaden my global horizons. Mastering another foreign language  
will open a brand‑new window for me to observe the world.  
中文翻译：  
除英语之外，我很想学习的一门外语是日语。我计划结合多种学习资源循序渐  
进学习。我会上线上初级课程掌握基础语法和发音。闲暇时间，我打算看双语  
字幕短视频和剧集，适应真实日常对话。我也会每天坚持记忆实用词汇。我选  
择这门语言有几个主要原因。我对它独特的流行文化，包括动漫和纪录片很感

## PDF第291页

兴趣。掌握这门语言之后，我可以脱离翻译直接欣赏原版内容。此外，它还可  
能为未来旅行、跨文化交流创造机会。我想象当我达到基础熟练水平之后，会  
收获很强的成就感。我可以直接理解不一样的文化思想，拓宽国际视野。掌握  
另一门外语，会为我打开一扇观察世界的全新窗口。  
Part3  
Q1. What languages do children learn in school in your country?  
Answer：  
In Chinese schools, English is the main foreign language taught to most  
students from primary or junior high school. In some larger‑scale cities,  
a small number of schools offer optional second foreign‑language  
courses such as Japanese, French or Spanish. However, these are not  
compulsory subjects. Most students focus their main energy on English  
study for examinations.  
中文翻译：  
在中国学校，大部分学生从小学或者初中开始主要学习英语。部分大城市少数

## PDF第292页

学校会开设日语、法语、西班牙语这类第二外语选修课。但这些都不是必修  
课。绝大多数学生把主要精力放在应试英语学习上。  
Q2. Is it difficult for adults to learn a new language?  
Answer：  
Learning a new language is generally more challenging for adults than  
for young children. Adults’ brains are less flexible in picking up new  
pronunciation. They also have limited spare time due to work  
responsibilities. Even so, adults possess stronger logical thinking and  
self‑discipline. With consistent effort, they can still achieve satisfying  
learning outcomes.  
中文翻译：  
总体来说成年人学一门新语言比小孩子更有难度。成年人大脑在习得新发音方  
面可塑性下降，同时工作占用大量时间，空余时间有限。但成年人逻辑思维、  
自律能力更强。只要持续付出努力，依旧可以取得不错学习效果。  
Q3. Why do some people learn a foreign language?

## PDF第293页

Answer：  
People take up foreign languages for varied purposes. Some study for  
career advancement, hoping to gain advantages in the job market.  
Some learn out of pure personal interest in foreign cultures. Others  
prepare for overseas travel or further education abroad. Different life  
goals drive people to invest time in language learning.  
中文翻译：  
人们学习外语有着各种各样目的。一部分人为职业发展，希望在职场获得竞争  
优势；一部分纯粹出于对异国文化的个人兴趣；还有人为出境旅行或者海外深  
造做准备。不同人生目标推动人们投入时间学习语言。  
Q4. Which is better, to learn a language in‑country or in a foreign  
country?  
Answer：  
Studying in a foreign‑language‑speaking country provides total  
language‑immersion surroundings, which speeds up

## PDF第294页

listening‑and‑speaking improvement. Nevertheless, studying abroad  
costs large sums of money. People can also make good progress at  
home through online courses and authentic media materials. So  
overseas study is not the only path to master a foreign tongue.  
中文翻译：  
去目标语言国家学习，可以提供完全沉浸式环境，听说能力提升很快。但出国  
留学开销巨大。留在国内，依靠网课和原版素材同样可以取得不错进步。所以  
出国并不是掌握一门外语的唯一途径。  
Q5. What is the best age for people to learn a new language?  
Answer：  
Young children enjoy natural advantages in pronunciation and language  
intuition. However, older teenagers and adults have better  
comprehension for grammar rules. There is no absolute “best age”. As  
long as learners keep motivation and consistent practice, people can  
start learning a new language at any age.

## PDF第295页

中文翻译：  
低龄儿童在发音、语言直觉方面拥有天然优势。但青少年和成年人对语法规则  
理解能力更强。不存在绝对的 “最佳学习年龄”。只要学习者保持动力，坚持  
练习，任何年龄都可以开始学新语言。  
Q6. Do you think translation apps will replace human language learners  
in the future?  
Answer：  
Translation applications become increasingly powerful, yet they cannot  
fully replace people who master foreign languages. Machine translation  
often makes mistakes with subtle meanings, cultural background and  
emotional tones. Human speakers can judge context and communicate  
flexibly. Tools serve only as assistance, not total substitutes for  
real‑language ability.  
中文翻译：  
翻译软件越来越强大，但它们无法完全取代掌握外语的人。机器翻译经常在细  
微含义、文化背景、情感语气上出错。人可以判断语境，灵活沟通。工具只能  
作为辅助，不能真正替代语言能力。

## PDF第296页

Describe a short trip you enjoyed  
You should say:  
‑ Where you went  
‑ Who you went with  
‑ What you did during the trip  
And explain why you enjoyed this short trip  
Answer：  
The short enjoyable trip I would like to describe is a one‑day excursion  
to a small ancient town not far from my city. I travelled there with two of  
my close friends last autumn on a bright sunny Saturday. We took  
public transport and arrived in the morning. We wandered along old  
stone streets, visited well‑preserved traditional houses and explored  
small local craft shops. We also tasted authentic regional snacks in

## PDF第297页

family‑run restaurants. We did not arrange tight sightseeing schedules.  
Instead, we moved at a slow relaxing pace, took photographs and  
chatted happily as we walked. What made this short journey so pleasant  
was its laid‑back rhythm. We escaped heavy study pressure without  
spending too much money or wasting multiple days. The ancient town  
had beautiful scenery and unique local culture. Spending quality time  
with my good friends also added a great deal of joy. This one‑day  
getaway perfectly refreshed my mind after a long period of busy

的地。我们漫步古老石板街巷，参观保存完好的传统民居，逛本地小手工艺品

慢悠悠闲逛，边走边拍照、开心聊天。这次短途旅行之所以让人享受，在于松  
弛的节奏。我们不用花很多钱，也不用耗费好几天时间，就可以逃离繁重学业  
压力。古镇风景优美，拥有独特本土文化，再加上和好友共度美好时光，更是

新。

## PDF第298页

Part3  
Q1. Do people prefer short trips or long holidays?  
Answer：  
People’s preference between short trips and long holidays depends on  
their available time and personal circumstances. Short one‑or‑two‑day  
outings fit busy workers and students who have limited vacation days.  
Long holidays allow deeper travel experiences in distant places. Many  
people enjoy both: short getaways for quick relaxation and long  
journeys for thorough exploration.  
中文翻译：  
人们偏爱短途还是长途假期，取决于可支配时间和个人处境。只有少量假期的  
上班族、学生，很适合一两天短途出行。长假可以去往远方深度体验。很多人  
两者都喜欢：短途快速放松，长途用来深度探索。  
Q2. What problems may people have when they go on a trip?

## PDF第299页

Answer：  
Travellers can meet various unexpected troubles. Bad weather may ruin  
original travel plans. Transport delays, accommodation issues or  
over‑crowded scenic spots create unpleasant experiences. Some tourists  
also face language barriers or difficulty adapting to local food. Making  
thorough preparations can reduce risks, yet nothing can guarantee a  
completely trouble‑free journey.  
中文翻译：  
旅行者会遇到各类突发麻烦。恶劣天气毁掉原定计划；交通延误、住宿问题、  
景区人满为患，带来糟糕体验。部分游客还会遇到语言障碍，或者吃不惯当地  
食物。充分准备可以降低风险，但没有办法保证旅途完全一帆风顺。  
Q3. Why do some people like travelling alone?  
Answer：  
Solo‑travel lovers value total freedom over their travel plans. They can  
decide destinations, departure times and activities without negotiating  
with companions. They enjoy self‑reflection and meeting new people  
along the way. Of course, travelling alone also contains potential safety

## PDF第300页

risks, so individuals need to stay cautious during the whole journey.  
中文翻译：  
喜欢独自旅行的人，看重行程完全的自由度。不用和同伴商量，自己决定目的  
地、出发时间和活动。他们享受自我思考，在路上结识新朋友。当然独自旅行  
也存在潜在安全隐患，整个旅途当中个人需要保持谨慎。  
Q4. What are the advantages of group travel?  
Answer：  
Group travel brings multiple strengths. Travellers can share travel costs  
and take care of each other in unfamiliar locations. It reduces loneliness  
and creates chances for fun group interaction. Organised group tours  
also simplify planning work for tourists. However, group travellers have  
limited personal freedom to change their schedules.  
中文翻译：  
跟团集体旅行有不少优势。旅行者可以分摊开销，在陌生地方互相照应，减少  
孤独感，拥有集体互动的乐趣。跟团游还替游客简化规划工作。但缺点是个人

## PDF第301页

自由有限，很难随意改动行程。  
Q5. Do you think travel makes people more open‑minded?  
Answer：  
Proper travel can broaden people’s minds, but it is not guaranteed.  
When travellers actively contact local residents and accept different  
customs, they gain new perspectives. If tourists only stick to tourist  
zones and keep their old fixed viewpoints, travelling will produce little  
influence. The traveller’s own attitude decides whether travel changes  
their thinking.  
中文翻译：  
合适的旅行可以开阔眼界，但效果不是必然。如果旅行者主动接触本地人，接  
纳不同风俗，就会收获新视角。倘若游客只待在游客区，固守固有观念，旅行  
就很难带来改变。旅行者自身的态度，决定旅行能否改变思想。  
Q6. Where do Chinese people like to travel to?

## PDF第302页

Answer：  
Chinese tourists choose diverse travel destinations. Many favour  
domestic scenic spots, including mountains, ancient towns and coastal  
cities. A large number of travellers also go abroad for exotic overseas  
experiences. Holiday periods see huge tourist flows to popular  
locations. Meanwhile, an increasing number of people choose  
lesser‑known quiet spots to avoid crowds.  
中文翻译：  
中国游客会选择多种多样旅行目的地。很多人喜欢国内景点，例如山川、古  
镇、海滨城市。也有大量游客出国，体验异国风情。节假日热门地点人潮汹  
涌。同时越来越多人选择小众安静地方避开人潮。

serve me well. It does not only create beautiful images, but also trains

Part3  
Q1. What skills do children should learn?  
Answer：  
Children ought to develop a mixture of life skills, social skills and  
academic abilities. Basic self‑care skills such as dressing and tidying are  
fundamental. They also need to learn how to communicate, share and  
cooperate with peers. Apart from textbook knowledge, creative skills

## PDF第305页

like drawing or music are also beneficial. The goal is to help them grow  
into well‑rounded individuals, not just get high exam scores.  
中文翻译：  
孩子应当学习生活、社交、学业多方面技能。穿衣、整理这类基础自理能力是  
根本。他们也要学会沟通、分享，和同伴协作。除课本知识之外，绘画音乐这  
类创造性技能同样有益。目标是帮助孩子全面成长，而不只是拿到高分。  
Q2. Is it hard for grown‑ups to learn new skills?  
Answer：  
Adults face certain difficulties picking up fresh skills. They have less  
spare time and may struggle to break old‑formed habits. Even so, adults  
have strong logical understanding and clear learning motivation. If they  
keep consistent practice, adults can achieve great progress. Difficulty  
varies depending on personal devotion and the nature of the skill itself.  
中文翻译：  
成年人学习新技能会遇到一些困难。空余时间更少，也很难改掉已经形成的旧

## PDF第306页

习惯。但成年人逻辑理解力强，学习目标明确。只要坚持练习，成年人依旧可  
以取得很大进步。难度取决于个人投入程度以及技能本身的属性。  
Q3. What is the best way to learn new skills?  
Answer：  
The most effective way to master new skills combines theoretical input  
with repeated hands‑on practice. Simply watching tutorials or reading  
books cannot make people competent. Learners need to apply  
knowledge in real situations, make mistakes and adjust accordingly.  
Getting feedback from more‑experienced people also speeds up  
improvement. Pure passive receiving of information has limited effects.  
中文翻译：  
掌握新技能最高效的方式，是理论输入搭配反复实操练习。只看教程读书，无  
法让人真正掌握本领。学习者要在真实场景运用知识，犯错并且随之调整。从  
更有经验的人那里获取反馈，同样可以加速提升。单纯被动接收信息效果十分  
有限。

## PDF第307页

Q4. Why do some people learn skills faster than others?  
Answer：  
Several factors create gaps in learning speed. Previous related  
experience offers learners a solid foundation. Personal natural talent,  
learning motivation and time invested all play important roles. Besides,  
good learning methods and timely feedback also make a big difference.  
Quick learning does not equal long‑term mastery; consistent practice  
still matters most.  
中文翻译：  
多重因素造成人们学习速度的差距。过往相关经验打下扎实基础；个人天赋、  
学习动力、投入时间都很关键。除此之外好的学习方法和及时反馈，也会带来  
巨大差别。学得快不等于可以长久精通，持续练习才是重中之重。  
Q5. Should schools teach practical skills to students?  
Answer：  
I strongly believe schools ought to deliver practical skill courses

## PDF第308页

alongside traditional academic subjects. Real‑world skills such as  
financial awareness, time management and simple first‑aid knowledge  
prepare teenagers for future adult life. Theoretical subjects build  
intellectual ability, while practical skills solve real‑life problems. Both  
types of education are necessary for young people’s development.  
中文翻译：  
我十分认同学校除传统文化课之外，还应当教授实用技能课程。理财意识、时  
间管理、简单急救这类现实技能，帮助青少年为成年生活做好准备。理论科目  
锻炼思维，实用技能解决现实难题。两类教育对于年轻人成长都必不可少。  
Q6. What skills are most useful in modern society?  
Answer：  
In modern society, comprehensive abilities stand out. Digital‑literacy  
skills, effective communication, critical thinking and the ability to keep  
learning new things are highly valuable. Technology changes rapidly, so  
the willingness to keep updating one’s knowledge becomes extremely  
important. Professional expertise combined with these soft abilities  
creates competitive advantages for individuals.

## PDF第309页

中文翻译：  
现代社会当中综合能力格外重要。数字素养、高效沟通、批判性思维以及持续  
学习新事物的能力价值很高。科技迭代飞快，愿意不断更新自身知识变得至关  
重要。专业硬实力搭配这些软实力，才能给个人打造竞争优势。

was frustrated and even a little upset, because all my hard work seemed  
to go to waste. However, I calmed myself down after a short while. I  
realised there was nothing I could do to change this official decision.  
Instead of sinking into negative emotions, I tried to transfer what I had  
learned to my ordinary coursework. Although I failed to take part in  
that competition, the knowledge and public‑speaking practice I gained  
still brought me benefits in my later study life.

绪，而是试着把学到的东西运用到日常课程学习当中。虽然没能参加那场比

Part3  
Q1. How do modern‑day people get news?  
Answer：  
Nowadays most people acquire news mainly from digital sources.  
Short‑video platforms, news apps and social‑media feeds push real‑time  
information to users every day. Traditional channels such as  
newspapers, radio and TV still exist, yet they are less popular among  
young generations. Online information is fast‑updated, but audiences  
also need to watch out for false news spreading across the internet.  
中文翻译：  
现在大多数人主要通过数字渠道获取新闻。短视频平台、新闻软件、社交媒体  
信息流，每天向用户推送实时资讯。报纸、广播、电视这类传统渠道依旧存  
在，但在年轻人当中热度已经下降。线上信息更新速度很快，但受众也要小心

## PDF第312页

网络流传的虚假消息。  
Q2. Do you think children should know about bad news?  
Answer：  
Children should be exposed to negative news in an age‑appropriate  
way. Completely shielding kids from all bad information is unrealistic in  
modern society. Nevertheless, shocking violent news should be kept  
away from very young children. Parents can select simplified news  
content and offer proper guidance. This helps children gradually build a  
complete understanding of the real world.  
中文翻译：  
应当用符合年龄的方式，让孩子接触不好的新闻。现代社会，把孩子完全隔绝  
所有负面信息并不现实。不过过于惊悚暴力的新闻，要避免让低龄儿童看到。  
家长可以筛选简化过的新闻内容，给到恰当引导。这可以帮助孩子逐步建立对  
现实世界完整的认知。  
Q3. Why do some people like to share bad news with others?

## PDF第313页

Answer：  
People share bad news for several psychological reasons. When  
individuals suffer misfortune, talking about troubles helps them release  
inner pressure and seek emotional comfort. Some hope to get practical  
advice and support from listeners. In a few cases, people spread  
negative stories out of curiosity or gossip. But sharing bad news should  
consider other people’s feelings and the real situation.  
中文翻译：  
人们向别人传递坏消息有多重心理原因。遭遇不幸的时候，倾诉烦恼有助于释  
放内心压力，寻求情感慰藉。一部分人希望从倾听者那里得到实际建议与支  
持。少数情况下，人们出于好奇或者八卦心态传播负面消息。但传递坏消息  
时，应当顾及他人感受和现实处境。  
Q4. Is it better to deliver bad news directly or gently?  
Answer：  
It depends on the situation and the receiver’s mental state. For

## PDF第314页

objective practical matters that require immediate action, direct and  
clear expression avoids misunderstanding. If the news is emotionally  
devastating, people ought to break it gently and offer comfort. There is  
no universal perfect method. The key is to show respect and concern for  
the person who receives bad news.  
中文翻译：  
这取决于具体情况和接收消息者的心理状态。需要立刻处理的客观现实事务，  
直接清晰表述可以避免误解。如果消息会带来巨大情感打击，就要委婉告知，  
同时给予安慰。不存在万能完美方式，关键是对接收坏消息的人表达尊重与关  
心。  
Q5. How can people comfort others who receive bad news?  
Answer：  
When comforting someone who has received bad news, the most  
important thing is to be a patient listener. People do not always need  
lots of useless persuasive words. Simply staying beside them and  
acknowledging their sadness works well. If it is possible, offer practical  
help. Avoid saying empty clichés. Empathy rather than blind optimism

## PDF第315页

creates real comfort for people in difficulty.  
中文翻译：  
安慰收到坏消息的人，最重要的是做一个耐心的倾听者。当事人并不总是需要  
一大堆空洞的说教。只要陪伴在侧，认可对方难过的情绪就很有用。条件允许  
的话，可以提供实际帮助。不要说空洞套话。共情，而非一味强行乐观，才能  
真正安慰身处困境的人。  
Q6. Do you think bad news spreads faster than good news online?  
Answer：  
Unfortunately, negative information often spreads more rapidly on the  
internet. Bad news is more likely to arouse strong emotional reactions  
among audiences. Platform algorithms also tend to push  
highly‑engaging negative content to more users. That does not mean  
good news cannot go viral. But negative stories usually attract more  
public attention in a short period of time.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第316页

遗憾的是，负面消息在网上往往传播得更快。坏消息更容易激起受众强烈情绪  
反应。平台算法也倾向把互动高的负面内容推送给更多用户。这并不是说好消  
息无法走红，但负面事件通常短时间可以吸引更多大众目光。

Describe an important river or lake in your country  
You should say:  
‑ Where it is  
‑ What it looks like  
‑ What people do there  
And explain why it is important  
Answer：  
The important river I would like to describe is the Yangtze River, the  
longest river in China. It originates in the western high‑land area and

## PDF第317页

flows through multiple provinces all the way towards the eastern coast.  
The river is extremely wide in most sections, with huge volumes of  
water flowing continuously. Along its banks, you can see a mix of  
large‑scale modern cities, small towns and fertile farmland. Local  
residents carry out a wide range of activities connected with this great  
river. Cargo ships travel along its waterway for inland transportation.  
Many citizens go to the river‑side public parks for walking, jogging or  
enjoying the sunset. Besides, the water supplies irrigation for large areas  
of agricultural land. This river is tremendously significant for our nation.  
It provides water resources for industry, farming and millions of  
ordinary citizens. Throughout Chinese history, countless civilisations  
grew up alongside its waters. It supports economic development and  
carries rich cultural meaning, so it is regarded as our mother river.  
中文翻译：  
我想描述的国内一条重要河流是长江，中国最长的江河。它发源于西部高原，  
流经多个省份，一路奔向东部海岸。大部分河段江面十分宽阔，巨大水量源源  
不断奔涌。沿江两岸，你既能看到现代化大城市，也有小镇和肥沃农田。当地  
居民开展许许多多和这条大河相关的活动。货轮在内河航道往来运输货物。很  
多市民去江边城市公园散步、慢跑，欣赏日落。同时江水为大片农田提供灌溉  
水源。这条江河对我们国家意义重大。它为工业、农业以及数百万普通民众供

## PDF第318页

给水资源。纵观中国历史，无数文明在它的沿岸孕育成长。它支撑经济发展，  
同时承载厚重文化内涵，因此被我们视作母亲河。  
Part3  
Q1. Why do many cities develop near rivers?  
Answer：  
Since ancient times, human settlements have preferred locations close  
to rivers. Rivers offer steady drinking water for residents. They also  
support crop irrigation and convenient water‑borne transport for  
goods. Building cities near rivers helps trade and agriculture flourish.  
Even in modern times, waterfront areas still attract population and  
investment for urban construction.  
中文翻译：  
自古以来人类聚居地就偏爱靠近江河。河流给居民提供稳定饮用水源，支持农  
作物灌溉，还提供便利水上货运。在河边建城市，贸易和农业更容易繁荣发  
展。就算现代，滨水区域依旧吸引人口与城市建设投资。

## PDF第319页

Q2. What kinds of activities do people do in rivers and lakes?  
Answer：  
People take part in diverse activities on rivers and lakes. Some enjoy  
water sports such as boating and kayaking. Sightseeing cruises are  
popular among tourists. Local fishermen carry out fishing work. In  
addition, waterfront parks allow visitors to walk and appreciate natural  
scenery. However, certain dangerous water activities need proper safety  
supervision.  
中文翻译：  
人们在江河湖泊开展多种多样活动。一部分人体验划船、皮划艇这类水上运  
动。观光游船深受游客喜爱。当地渔民从事捕鱼作业。此外滨水公园供游人散  
步欣赏自然风光。当然部分危险水上活动，需要完善的安全监管。  
Q3. Should governments protect rivers and lakes?  
Answer：

## PDF第320页

Governments bear major responsibility for protecting rivers and lakes.  
These water bodies supply vital water resources for human society.  
Without official protection, industrial waste and domestic sewage will  
cause severe water pollution. Once water environments get damaged,  
restoration will cost massive money and time. Therefore relevant laws  
and supervision are absolutely necessary.  
中文翻译：  
政府肩负保护江河湖泊的主要责任。这些水体给人类社会提供至关重要的水资  
源。缺少官方保护，工业废水、生活污水会造成严重水污染。水环境一旦被破  
坏，修复会耗费巨额资金与时间。所以相关法律和监管必不可少。  
Q4. Do you think water sports are safe?  
Answer：  
Water sports can be enjoyable yet they contain hidden risks. Their  
safety heavily depends on safety equipment, professional guidance and  
environmental conditions. Some activities such as swimming in wild  
rivers are quite dangerous. If people ignore warnings and lack  
protection, accidents may easily take place. So participants must follow

## PDF第321页

safety rules and take protective measures.  
中文翻译：  
水上运动很好玩，但同时暗藏风险。安全程度很大程度取决于防护装备、专业  
指导以及环境条件。在野外江河游泳这类活动危险性很高。如果人们无视警  
示，缺少防护，很容易发生意外。参与者必须遵守安全规范，做好保护措施。  
Q5. Why do many people like to live near water?  
Answer：  
Lots of people enjoy living close to water for multiple reasons.  
Water‑side scenery creates a relaxing, peaceful mood. Besides,  
convenient access to rivers or lakes supports recreation and leisure  
activities. In real‑estate markets, waterfront housing often has higher  
market value. Nevertheless, living near water may also bring flood risks  
in some regions.  
中文翻译：  
很多人喜欢住在水边有多方面原因。滨水风景营造放松安宁的心境。而且靠近

## PDF第322页

河湖可以方便开展休闲娱乐。房地产市场当中，临水住宅往往拥有更高价值。  
不过部分地区住在水边，也会面临洪水隐患。  
Q6. What are the threats to rivers and lakes?  
Answer：  
Rivers and lakes face several main threats. Industrial pollution and  
untreated domestic sewage pollute water quality. Over‑fishing damages  
aquatic ecological balance. In addition, excessive land reclamation and  
rubbish dumping also destroy water‑side habitats. Human‑caused  
damage is the biggest danger for inland water systems nowadays.  
中文翻译：  
江河湖泊面临几大主要威胁。工业污染、未经处理的生活污水破坏水质；过度  
捕捞打破水生生态平衡。除此之外，过度围垦、垃圾倾倒也会破坏滨水栖息  
地。人为造成的破坏，是如今内陆水系最大的危险。

## PDF第323页

✨Part2 保留题 24 帮助过你的陌生人  
Describe a time a stranger helped you  
You should say:  
‑ Where it happened  
‑ When it happened  
‑ What difficulty you had  
And explain how the stranger helped you  
Answer：  
The experience when a stranger offered me help took place on a busy  
city street one year ago. I was heading to take an important written  
examination by public transport. Unfortunately, I got off the bus at the  
wrong stop because I was busy reviewing my notes on the ride. When I  
realised my mistake, I felt quite anxious. I had no idea how to get to the  
exam venue quickly and my mobile phone ran out of battery power all  
of a sudden. A middle‑aged lady passing by noticed my worried look.  
She stopped and asked what trouble I was in. After hearing my  
situation, she lent me her mobile phone so I could check the correct

## PDF第324页

route. She also pointed out the nearest bus stop and told me which  
number bus I ought to take. Thanks to her kindness, I rushed to the  
examination location just in time. I really appreciated this stranger’s  
willingness to give me a hand without any expectation of reward. This  
incident showed me that small acts of kindness from unknown people  
can make a huge difference.  
中文翻译：  
陌生人帮助我的经历发生在一年前，一条繁忙城市街道上。我要坐公交去参加  
一场重要笔试。乘车途中我忙着复习笔记，结果在错误站点下了车。意识到失  
误之后我十分焦虑。我不知道怎么快速赶到考点，手机还偏偏突然没电。一位  
路过的中年女士注意到我神色慌张，停下来询问我遇到什么麻烦。听完我的处  
境，她把手机借给我查询正确路线，还给我指明最近的公交站，告诉我应该坐  
哪一路公交车。多亏她的善意，我才及时赶到考场。我十分感激这位陌生人不  
求回报伸出援手。这件事让我看到，陌生人小小的善举，也可以带来巨大改  
变。  
Part3  
Q1. Should we help other people on the street?

## PDF第325页

Answer：  
Generally speaking, we ought to offer help to people in need on the  
street. However, people also need to keep basic self‑protection  
awareness. We can give assistance within safe boundaries. For example,  
calling relevant departments or guiding lost pedestrians. It is unwise to  
put ourselves into risky situations. Kindness should go hand‑in‑hand  
with reasonable self‑protection.  
中文翻译：  
总体来说，在街上遇到需要帮助的人，我们应当伸出援手。但同时人们也要具  
备基本自我保护意识。可以在安全范围内提供帮助，比如拨打相关部门电话，  
或者给迷路路人指路。不要让自己置身危险处境。善意要和合理的自我保护并  
行。  
Q2. What kinds of help do people often need in public places?  
Answer：  
In public settings, people frequently meet minor troubles. Tourists may

## PDF第326页

get lost and need directions. Some elderly citizens struggle with  
smart‑device operations. Others may drop belongings or meet small  
unexpected accidents. Most of these are small‑scale practical difficulties,  
which can be solved with simple assistance from passers‑by.  
中文翻译：  
在公共场所，人们经常遇到一些小麻烦。游客迷路，需要问路；部分老年人搞  
不懂智能设备；还有人遗失物品，或是遭遇小型意外事故。大多都是不大的现  
实难题，路人简单搭把手就可以解决。  
Q3. Are people more willing to help strangers now than in the past?  
Answer：  
People’s willingness to assist strangers is complicated. Modern society  
has higher awareness of self‑protection because of some negative social  
cases. Even so, online volunteer activities and public‑welfare projects  
show that many people still hold kind hearts. People are not less  
kind‑hearted; they just become more cautious before offering help.

## PDF第327页

中文翻译：  
现代人帮助陌生人的意愿情况比较复杂。受到部分社会负面事件影响，大众自  
我保护意识变强。但线上志愿、公益项目也能看出很多人依旧心存善意。人们  
并不是变得冷漠，只是伸出援手之前会更加谨慎。  
Q4. Why are some people unwilling to help others?  
Answer：  
Several reasons account for people refusing to offer help. Some are in a  
hurry and have no spare time. Some worry that helping strangers may  
bring unexpected trouble. A small number of individuals are extremely  
self‑centred and pay little attention to other people’s difficulties.  
Different personal life experiences also shape their attitudes towards  
giving assistance.  
中文翻译：  
人们不愿伸出援手有多重原因。一部分人行色匆匆，没有空余时间；一部分担  
心帮助陌生人会惹上意想不到的麻烦。少数人极度以自我为中心，不太在意别  
人的困境。不同人生经历，也会塑造他们助人的态度。

## PDF第328页

Q5. How can schools teach children to help others?  
Answer：  
Schools can cultivate children’s awareness of helping others in  
multiple ways. Teachers can set good examples in daily campus life, and  
organise voluntary group activities. Besides, stories about kindness can  
be used in classes. What matters most is real practice rather than empty  
lectures. Small practical experiences make these values sink in for young  
kids.  
中文翻译：  
学校可以通过多种方式培养孩子乐于助人的意识。老师在校园日常生活以身作  
则，组织集体志愿活动。课堂上也可以讲述善意相关故事。最重要的是真实实  
践，而不是空洞说教。小小的实际经历，才能让这些价值观真正深入孩子内  
心。  
Q6. Do you think people should get rewards for helping strangers?

## PDF第329页

Answer：  
I do not believe material rewards should become the main motivation  
for helping strangers. Kindness is more valuable when it comes from  
people’s inner willingness. That being said, receiving thanks or small  
symbolic gifts is acceptable. If society gives proper recognition for good  
deeds, more citizens may be encouraged to show kindness towards  
others.  
中文翻译：  
我认为物质回报不应当成为帮助陌生人的主要动机。发自内心的善意才更可  
贵。当然，接受感谢或者象征性小礼物无可厚非。如果社会对善举给予恰当肯  
定，可以鼓励更多民众释放善意。

‑ Why you want to learn this skill

Answer：  
The handicraft skill I am eager to learn is hand‑made pottery. I hope to  
master shaping clay into beautiful ceramic works by hand. If I had the  
chance, I would sign up for offline pottery workshops taught by  
professional craftsmen. I plan to start from basic operations such as  
kneading clay and simple shaping techniques. I will watch  
demonstrations carefully and practise repeatedly. I also intend to learn  
knowledge about glazing and low‑temperature firing. I really want to  
learn pottery for two major reasons. First, the whole creation process is  
very relaxing and helps me release study‑related pressure. Besides,  
handmade ceramic items carry unique warmth that mass‑produced  
goods lack. Once I master this skill, I plan to make practical daily‑use  
objects. I would create simple teacups and small bowls for my family  
and close friends as special hand‑made gifts. Each finished piece will be  
one‑of‑a‑kind, filled with my own time and personal feelings.

## PDF第331页

中文翻译：  
我很想学习的一门手工技艺是手工陶艺。我希望掌握用黏土手工塑造精美陶瓷  
作品的本领。如果有机会，我会报名参加专业匠人开设的线下陶艺工坊。我打  
算从揉泥、简单塑形这类基础操作学起，认真观摩示范，反复练习。我还想学

程十分解压，可以帮我释放学习压力。除此之外，手工陶瓷作品拥有量产商品  
所不具备的独特温度。一旦掌握这项手艺，我打算制作实用日用品。我会给家  
人和好朋友做简单茶杯、小碗，当作独一无二的手作礼物。每一件成品都是独  
一份，倾注我的时间与个人情感。  
Part3  
Q1. Do you think it is important for children to learn handicrafts?  
Answer：  
Learning handicrafts brings many benefits for children. Hand‑making  
improves kids’ practical abilities, imagination and patience. While  
creating things with their own hands, youngsters can gain a strong  
sense of achievement. Even so, schools should not force every child to

## PDF第332页

master complex handcrafts. It should be treated as an interesting  
optional activity instead of heavy schoolwork.  
中文翻译：  
学习手工对孩子有很多好处。手工锻炼孩子动手能力、想象力与耐心。亲手创  
作的时候，青少年可以收获满满的成就感。但学校不必强迫每个孩子掌握复杂  
手工。手工应当作为有趣的可选活动，而不是繁重课业。  
Q2. Are traditional handicrafts popular in your country?  
Answer：  
Some traditional handicrafts still enjoy great popularity in China. Many  
tourists buy hand‑craft souvenirs during travels. Cultural‑protection  
projects also help preserve old‑time skills. However, mass‑produced  
cheap goods dominate most ordinary‑people’s daily consumption. A  
number of traditional crafts face the risk of fading away because few  
young people want to take them up as careers.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第333页

国内部分传统手工艺依旧人气很高。很多游客旅行会购买手作纪念品，文化保  
护项目也助力古老技艺传承。但普通人日常消费市场，大多被廉价量产商品占  
据。不少传统手艺面临失传风险，愿意以此为职业的年轻人不多。  
Q3. Why are some traditional handicrafts disappearing?  
Answer：  
Multiple factors cause traditional handicrafts to gradually disappear.  
Mass‑produced factory goods are cheaper and more accessible,  
squeezing market space for hand‑made products. Learning these crafts  
requires long‑term hard practice, yet economic returns may not be  
attractive enough for young people. Without effective inheritance  
support, many old skills will keep declining.  
中文翻译：  
多重因素造成传统手工逐步消亡。工厂量产商品价格低廉、容易买到，挤压手  
工艺品市场。学习这类手艺需要长年苦练，但经济回报对年轻人吸引力有限。  
缺少有效的传承扶持，很多古老技艺会持续衰落。

## PDF第334页

Q4. Should schools teach traditional handicrafts to students?  
Answer：  
It is a good idea for schools to offer traditional handicraft courses as  
optional classes. These lessons help teenagers get to know national  
cultural heritage. Students can experience the charm of hand‑making.  
Still, schools need to consider material costs and teaching resources. It  
is more suitable as a hobby activity rather than a compulsory exam  
subject.  
中文翻译：  
学校开设传统手工选修课是一件好事。这类课程帮助青少年了解民族文化遗  
产，学生可以感受手工制作的魅力。但学校要考虑材料成本与师资条件，更适  
合作为兴趣活动，而不是必考科目。  
Q5. What is the difference between handmade goods and  
machine‑made goods?  
Answer：

## PDF第335页

Hand‑made items contain the maker’s personal emotion and unique  
tiny imperfections. Every single handicraft piece is one‑of‑a‑kind. On the  
contrary, machine‑manufactured products have consistent quality and  
lower prices. They are suitable for mass daily consumption. Hand‑made  
goods focus on individuality; machine‑made goods prioritise practicality  
and cost‑effectiveness.  
中文翻译：  
手工作品蕴含制作者个人情感，带有独一无二的细微不完美，每一件都是孤  
品。与之相反，机器生产的产品品质统一，价格更低，适合大规模日常消费。  
手作看重独特个性，机器制品优先考虑实用性与性价比。  
Q6. Can high‑tech products replace handicrafts?  
Answer：  
High‑tech machine products cannot fully replace handicrafts.  
Technology can copy shapes, yet it cannot copy the human emotion  
and unique craftsmanship behind hand‑made works. Handicrafts have  
special cultural and sentimental value. Of course, high‑tech  
manufacturing satisfies most of society’s practical material demands.

## PDF第336页

The two forms of production can exist side‑by‑side.  
中文翻译：  
高科技机器产品无法彻底取代手工艺品。科技可以复刻外形，却复制不了手作  
背后人的情感与独特匠心。手工拥有特殊的文化和情感价值。当然高科技生产  
满足社会绝大部分现实物质需求。两种生产方式可以共存。

‑ Who you spent it with

## PDF第337页

Answer：  
The important festival that always makes me feel happy is the Spring  
Festival, Chinese Lunar New Year. Last Spring Festival, I spent the whole  
holiday together with my whole big family, including my grandparents,  
parents and other relatives. Before the festival arrived, we cleaned our  
home thoroughly and prepared piles of tasty festival food. On New  
Year’s Eve, all family members gathered for a big reunion dinner. We  
enjoyed abundant home‑cooked dishes, watched the new‑year TV gala  
and chatted about our past year’s experiences. Young family members  
also received red envelopes as good‑luck blessings. During the  
following days, we visited relatives, exchanged greetings and shared  
snacks. I felt genuinely happy for several reasons. First of all, this festival  
gives us a precious chance for the whole extended family to get  
together. Everyone can slow down from busy work and study. Besides,  
the warm festive atmosphere and sincere good wishes from family  
members create strong feelings of belonging. Those precious reunion  
moments are what make the Spring Festival so joyful for me.  
中文翻译：  
让我感到很快乐的国内重要节日就是春节，也就是中国农历新年。去年春节，  
我和一整个大家庭共度假期，有爷爷奶奶、父母还有其他亲戚。节日来临之

## PDF第338页

前，我们彻底打扫屋子，准备一大堆美味年货。除夕夜全家人围坐吃丰盛年夜  
饭，享用家常菜，看春晚，聊聊过去一年的经历。晚辈还会收到代表美好祝福  
的红包。之后几天我们走亲访友，互相拜年，分享零食。我感到由衷开心有几  
个原因。首先这个节日给大家庭难得团聚的机会，所有人都可以从忙碌学习工  
作当中慢下来。此外浓浓的节日氛围，家人真挚的祝福，带来强烈归属感。这  
些珍贵团圆时刻，就是春节让我无比快乐的缘由。  
Part3  
Q1. What is the importance of festivals?  
Answer：  
Festivals carry multiple‑layered importance for society. They preserve  
traditional culture and pass down shared customs from generation to  
generation. They also offer opportunities for family and friends to  
gather and strengthen emotional bonds. Moreover, holidays give  
ordinary people a break from busy daily work. Without festivals, many  
traditional customs would gradually fade out of public life.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第339页

节日对社会拥有多重意义。节日传承传统文化，把共同习俗一代代延续下去。  
节日也给家人朋友团聚机会，加固情感纽带。另外假期让普通人从忙碌日常当  
中得到休整。如果没有节日，很多传统习俗会慢慢从大众生活消失。  
Q2. Do you think festivals nowadays are different from those in the  
past?  
Answer：  
Festivals have changed noticeably compared with the old days.  
Shopping online, sending digital red envelopes and watching online  
gala shows become popular new customs. However, the core meaning  
such as reunion and best wishes still remains. Modern lifestyles add new  
forms of celebration, but people’s emotional expectations for festivals  
stay largely unchanged.  
中文翻译：  
和过去相比，如今的节日发生明显变化。网购、电子红包、线上晚会成为新兴  
习俗。但团圆、祈福这类内核意义依旧保留。现代生活催生新的庆祝形式，但  
人们对于节日的情感期待大体上没有改变。

## PDF第340页

Q3. Do you think commercialisation has influenced traditional festivals?  
Answer：  
Commercialisation brings both positive and negative influences to  
traditional festivals. Business activities create rich festival‑related goods  
and colourful celebration choices. On the negative side, excessive  
commercial hype may make some people only care about consumption  
and ignore the cultural roots of festivals. So we should enjoy  
festival‑related commerce without forgetting traditional cultural  
meanings.  
中文翻译：  
商业化给传统节日同时带来积极和消极影响。商业活动催生丰富节日商品，带  
来更多庆祝选择。消极一面，过度商业炒作，会让一部分人只看重消费，忽视  
节日文化内核。所以我们可以享受节日商业，但不能忘记传统文化内涵。  
Q4. Why do people spend a lot of money during festivals?  
Answer：

## PDF第341页

People spend much money in festival periods for several reasons. They  
need to buy festival food, gifts for relatives and decorative items for  
celebration. Many take advantage of holiday time for travel. Besides,  
business promotions encourage consumers to make purchases. Some  
spending is for social customs, not merely for individual personal  
enjoyment.  
中文翻译：  
人们节日期间花销很大有多方面原因。需要置办节日吃食、走亲访友礼物、节  
日装饰；不少人利用假期出行旅游。另外商家促销也刺激消费。一部分开销出  
于社交习俗，并不仅仅为了个人享乐。  
Q5. Should schools teach students about traditional festivals?  
Answer：  
Schools should help students learn about traditional festivals. Students  
can understand cultural origins, folk customs and historical background  
behind each festival. This helps young people build cultural identity.  
Teachers can organise theme‑based activities instead of only textbook  
reading, so knowledge becomes more impressive for teenagers.

## PDF第342页

中文翻译：  
学校应当教授学生关于传统节日的知识。学生可以了解每个节日背后的文化起  
源、民俗和历史背景，帮助年轻人建立文化认同感。老师可以组织主题活动，  
而不只是读课本，让知识给青少年留下更深印象。  
Q6. Which do you prefer, celebrating festivals with family or with  
friends?  
Answer：  
It depends on which festival it is. For family‑oriented traditional festivals  
such as the Spring Festival, I value time with my family most. For some  
modern‑style holidays, hanging out with friends feels more suitable.  
Both forms of celebration have their own joy. I enjoy them in different  
suitable situations.

这取决于是什么节日。春节这类家庭向传统节日，我最看重和家人共度时光。  
一些现代节日，和朋友聚会会更合适。两种庆祝方式各有乐趣，我会在不同合

适场景分别享受。

Describe a time someone else made a decision for you

‑ What the decision was  
‑ Who made this decision  
‑ Why this person made the decision for you

Answer：  
The time when another person made an important decision for me goes  
back to my junior high‑school period. My mother made the call for me  
to take extra weekend painting classes. At that time, I had lots of spare  
time on weekends. My mother noticed I was interested in drawing

## PDF第344页

simple sketches in my notebooks. She believed systematic art training  
would develop my creativity and aesthetic sense. At the very beginning,  
I was a little unwilling. I worried the classes would take up my leisure  
time. Still, I followed her arrangement and attended the courses.  
Gradually, I grew fond of painting. I learned basic sketching and  
colour‑mixing skills. Looking back afterwards, I felt thankful for her  
choice. Even though I do not become a professional painter, those art  
lessons cultivated my eye for beauty and brought me a valuable lifelong  
hobby.  
中文翻译：  
别人替我做重要决定的经历可以追溯到我初中时期。我妈妈替我做决定，让我  
周末去上额外绘画兴趣班。那时候我周末有不少空余时间。妈妈注意到我总爱  
在本子上画简单速写。她认为系统美术训练可以培养我的创造力与审美。一开  
始我有点不情愿，担心课程会占用我的休闲时间。但我还是听从她的安排去上  
课。慢慢的，我爱上了绘画，学到基础素描和调色技巧。事后回想，我很感谢  
她的这个选择。虽然我没有成为职业画家，但那些美术课培养了我的审美，还  
给我留下一份可以陪伴一生的宝贵爱好。  
Part3

## PDF第345页

Q1. Should parents make decisions for their teenage children?  
Answer：  
Parents can offer suggestions for teenagers, yet they should not make  
every single choice on their behalf. Teenagers need opportunities to  
practise independent decision‑making. Of course, for highly risky  
matters, parents have the duty to stop children from making dangerous  
mistakes. As kids grow older, the right to choose should be gradually  
handed over to young people themselves.  
中文翻译：  
父母可以给青少年提供建议，但不应该替他们包办所有选择。青少年需要机会  
练习独立做决定。当然面对风险很高的事情，父母有责任阻止孩子做出危险错  
误。随着孩子长大，选择权应当逐步交给年轻人自己。  
Q2. Why do some people dislike others making decisions for them?  
Answer：  
Many people hate other people deciding things for them because they

## PDF第346页

desire personal autonomy. Having choices made by others may make  
them feel their own will is ignored. Even if the final result turns out  
good, they may still have a sense of losing control over their own life.  
The need for self‑determination is a basic psychological requirement for  
most adults.  
中文翻译：  
很多人讨厌别人替自己做决定，因为他们渴望个人自主权。别人包办选择，会  
让他们感觉自身意愿被无视。就算最终结果不错，依旧会产生对生活失去掌控  
的感受。自我抉择的需求，是大部分成年人基本心理诉求。  
Q3. What decisions in life are the most difficult for people?  
Answer：  
The most difficult decisions are usually those with long‑term life  
influences. Choices about major education paths, career directions,  
marriage and place of residence carry heavy consequences. These  
decisions often lack a totally perfect answer. People must weigh  
different advantages and disadvantages, and accept that every option  
also comes with certain drawbacks.

## PDF第347页

中文翻译：  
最难做的，往往是会对人生产生长远影响的抉择。重大教育道路、职业方向、  
婚姻、定居地点这类选择后果沉重。这类决定通常不存在绝对完美答案。人们  
必须权衡多方利弊，接受每一个选项都会附带一些缺憾。  
Q4. Is it good for leaders to make all decisions for team‑members?  
Answer：  
It is not wise for a leader to make every single decision for all  
team‑members. If subordinates never get chances to express opinions  
or make small‑scale judgements, they cannot grow their own  
competence. A good leader sets general directions and listens to group  
feedback, while giving proper room for team‑members to make local  
decisions by themselves.  
中文翻译：  
领导者替组员包办所有决定并不是明智之举。如果下属永远没有机会发表意  
见、做小判断，个人能力就得不到成长。优秀领导者确定大方向，听取团队反

## PDF第348页

馈，同时给到组员适度空间自行处理局部决策。  
Q5. What skills do people need to make good decisions?  
Answer：  
Making good decisions requires several key abilities. People ought to  
collect sufficient relevant information and analyse pros and cons  
objectively. They also need the capacity to foresee possible future  
outcomes. Besides, reasonable self‑awareness helps individuals  
understand their own real needs. Emotion‑control is also important so  
feelings do not cloud their judgement.  
中文翻译：  
做出好决定需要几项关键能力。人们要搜集充足相关信息，客观分析利弊，还  
要能够预判未来可能结果。此外客观自我认知，帮助人认清自己真实需求。情  
绪控制同样重要，避免感情干扰理性判断。  
Q6. Do you think it is OK to ask other people for advice before making  
big decisions?

## PDF第349页

Answer：  
Seeking advice from trusted people before big decisions is highly  
recommended. Other people can offer viewpoints we may ignore  
ourselves. Nevertheless, suggestions are only reference materials. After  
absorbing different opinions, the final choice should still depend on the  
person who will bear the consequences of that decision.  
中文翻译：  
做重大决定之前，向信任的人寻求建议十分可取。别人可以提供我们自己忽略  
的视角。但建议仅仅作为参考。吸收各方意见之后，最终选择权依旧属于要承  
担决定后果的本人。

✨Part2 保留题 28 帮助过你的家庭成员  
Describe a family member who helped you  
You should say:

## PDF第350页

‑ Who this person is  
‑ What kind of help he or she gave you  
‑ When this happened  
And explain how you felt about the help  
Answer：  
The family member who has given me lots of help is my father. The  
most impressive experience took place two years ago when I faced  
huge pressure in my study. At that time, my academic performance  
slipped and I felt greatly frustrated. I often felt anxious and doubted  
whether I could make further progress. My father noticed my bad  
mental state. Instead of blaming me for my unsatisfactory grades, he  
spent plenty of spare time talking with me patiently. He helped me  
analyse my weak points and encouraged me not to give up. He also  
gave me practical suggestions about time‑management and learning  
methods. He told me not to place overly high expectations on myself all  
the time. With his emotional support and useful guidance, I slowly  
rebuilt my confidence. Step by step, I adjusted my study plans and  
improved my performance. I am deeply grateful for his help. It was not  
only practical advice, but also the mental comfort that pulled me  
through that tough period.

## PDF第351页

中文翻译：  
给过我很多帮助的家人是我的父亲。印象最深的经历发生在两年前，我学习上  
面临巨大压力。那时候我的学业成绩下滑，内心十分挫败。我经常焦虑，怀疑  
自己还能不能继续进步。父亲察觉到我糟糕的精神状态。他没有因为不理想的  
分数指责我，而是花很多空余时间耐心和我沟通。他帮我分析薄弱环节，鼓励  
我不要放弃，还给我关于时间管理和学习方法的实际建议。他告诉我不要总是  
对自己抱有过高期待。在他的精神支持与有用的指引之下，我慢慢重建自信。  
我一步步调整学习计划，成绩得到提升。我深深感激他的帮助。不光是实用建  
议，更是这份精神慰藉，陪我熬过那段艰难时光。  
Part3  
Q1. Are family members more helpful than friends?  
Answer：  
Family and friends offer different kinds of support. Family members  
usually provide long‑term unconditional support, especially when  
people meet serious difficulties. However, close friends can better  
understand your peer‑group experiences. Neither side is absolutely

## PDF第352页

more helpful. In different situations, family or friends can play equally  
important supporting roles.  
中文翻译：  
家人和朋友提供不一样的支持。家人往往给予长期无条件的扶持，尤其是人遇  
到重大困境的时候。但亲密朋友更能理解同龄人处境。没有哪一方绝对更有  
用。在不同场景，家人、朋友都可以发挥同等重要的支持作用。  
Q2. What help do young people need from their families?  
Answer：  
Young people need multiple forms of family support. Apart from  
material resources for study and daily life, they require emotional  
comfort when they face setbacks. Family members can share life  
experience and give reasonable suggestions. Youngsters also need  
respect for their personal choices. Both practical aid and emotional care  
are equally valuable for them.  
中文翻译：

## PDF第353页

年轻人需要来自家庭多维度的支持。除学习生活的物质条件之外，遭遇挫折时  
需要情感慰藉。家人可以分享人生阅历，给到合理建议。年轻人也希望自己的  
选择得到尊重。现实帮助和情感关怀，对他们同等重要。  
Q3. Should children help their family members?  
Answer：  
It is very meaningful for children to offer help to their family. Doing  
simple house‑work or giving emotional support teaches kids  
responsibility and gratitude. They do not need to take on heavy  
adult‑level burdens. Small contributions within their ability are enough.  
These small acts help them realise they are part of the whole family unit.  
中文翻译：  
孩子帮助家人是很有意义的。做简单家务或者给予情感支持，教会孩子责任感  
与感恩之心。他们不需要承担成年人繁重压力，做力所能及的小事就足够。这  
些小小的付出，让他们意识到自己是家庭集体的一份子。  
Q4. What kinds of help do old people need from their family?

## PDF第354页

Answer：  
Elderly family members need both practical life assistance and  
emotional company. Some seniors require help with housework,  
medical‑care arrangements or digital‑device operations. Even more  
importantly, they hope for regular communication and companionship  
from their children and grandchildren. Material support alone cannot  
satisfy old people’s inner‑emotional needs.  
中文翻译：  
老年家庭成员既需要现实生活协助，也需要情感陪伴。一部分老人需要家务、  
就医安排、电子产品操作方面的帮助。但更重要的是，他们希望子女孙辈经常  
沟通陪伴。单纯物质资助无法满足老人内心情感需求。  
Q5. In what ways can modern‑technology help family members help  
each other?  
Answer：  
Modern technology strengthens family‑members’ mutual support in

## PDF第355页

many ways. Communication software enables long‑distance family  
members to keep in touch easily. People can send money, share  
health‑related information or give remote guidance through mobile  
devices. Nevertheless, high‑tech tools cannot fully replace face‑to‑face  
family company and interaction.  
中文翻译：  
现代科技在很多方面强化家人之间的互相扶持。通讯软件让分居两地的家人轻  
松保持联系。人们可以转账、分享健康资讯，或者通过手机远程指导。但高科  
技工具依旧无法完全替代家人面对面的陪伴与相处。  
Q6. Do you think family support is more important than support from  
friends?  
Answer：  
Family support and friends’ support serve different purposes and  
cannot be simply ranked. Family ties usually last for the whole lifetime.  
Meanwhile friends understand your current life situation very well.  
When people go through hard times, both sources of support are  
extremely precious. The importance depends on individual’s real‑life

## PDF第356页

circumstances.  
中文翻译：  
家人支持和朋友支持作用不同，不能简单分出高下。亲情纽带往往持续一生；  
而朋友很懂你当下的生活处境。人处在困境当中，两种支持都无比珍贵。哪一  
方更重要，取决于个人现实处境。
